### [1] [Rectifiability of the straight visible boundary and the limits of segment-based Hardy criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22407)
*H. Abbas,A. Azzouz*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将加权Hardy不等式归约到边界可见集上的低维Hausdorff内容界，并指出用直线段描述可见集存在内在障碍：若沿满足一致厚度条件的线段可见，则该点处存在截断锥，导致定量直线可见边界是有限个Lipschitz图的并、是(n-1)-可求长的，且不含任何λ>n-1的Hausdorff内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权Hardy不等式可由边界"可见部分"上的低维Hausdorff内容下界推出，而Koskela与Lehrbäck定义的基于曲线的visual boundary较难验证，因此自然希望用更易检验的直线段来描述可见集。作者旨在考察这一策略是否可行。

Method: 设Ω⊂R^n为区域，定义定量直线可见边界∂^{str}_{x,α}Ω，要求从x∈Ω出发沿线段可见并满足一致厚度条件。作者证明：若边界点ξ沿满足厚度条件的线段被看到，则Ω包含以ξ为顶点的截断锥；进而分析该可见集的几何结构与Hausdorff内容，并与(p,β)-Hardy不等式的内容型判据比较。

Result: 定量直线可见边界∩B(x,C_0 d_Ω(x))是有限个Lipschitz图的并，是(n-1)-可求长的，且对任意λ>n-1其λ-Hausdorff内容为零。由于λ≤n-1的内容条件已可在无可见性假设下推出(p,β)-Hardy不等式（β<p-n+λ），基于线段的标准要么不可满足、要么冗余，永远无法达到真正需要可见性的区域β≥p-1。作者还证明指数n-1可取到，故可求长定理是最优的；给出一个属于Koskela-Lehrbäck visual boundary但不属于任何∂^{str}_{x,α}Ω的边界点；并指出舍弃厚度条件的"原始直线可见性"可绕过该障碍，对λ≤n-1已足够，而λ>n-1情形仍开放。

Conclusion: 以直线段描述可见集以推导加权Hardy不等式存在内在障碍：厚度条件迫使可见集退化为低维可求长集，导致相应内容判据或不可满足、或与已有无可见性结果冗余，无法覆盖β≥p-1的关键区域。因此该途径本质上受限，而仅用原始直线可见性（无厚度条件）可能是更合适的方向，其中λ>n-1的情形尚待解决。

Abstract: Weighted Hardy inequalities on a domain $Ω\subset\R^n$ can be deduced from lower Hausdorff-content bounds on suitable visible parts of the boundary, and it is natural to look for visible sets described by straight segments, which are easier to verify than the curve-based visual boundary of Koskela and Lehrbäck. We show that this approach is subject to an intrinsic obstruction. If a boundary point $ξ$ is seen from $x\inΩ$ along a segment satisfying a uniform thickness condition, then $Ω$ contains a truncated cone with vertex $ξ$; consequently the quantitatively straight visible boundary $\partial^{\mathrm{str}}_{x,α}Ω\cap B(x,C_0d_Ω(x))$ is a finite union of Lipschitz graphs, is $(n-1)$-rectifiable, and carries no $λ$-Hausdorff content for any $λ>n-1$. Since content conditions with $λ\le n-1$ already imply the $(p,β)$-Hardy inequality for $β<p-n+λ$ without any visibility assumption, segment-based criteria are either unsatisfiable or redundant: they never reach the regime $β\ge p-1$ in which visibility is genuinely needed. We show that the exponent $n-1$ is attained, so that the rectifiability theorem is sharp; we exhibit a boundary point lying in the visual boundary of Koskela and Lehrbäck but in no $\partial^{\mathrm{str}}_{x,α}Ω$; and we observe that raw straight visibility, without the thickness condition, escapes the obstruction, is sufficient for $λ\le n-1$, and remains open for $λ>n-1$.

</details>


### [2] [P-adic Curved Kakeya Sets, Projection Theorem and Covering Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22485)
*Yi Lou*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 设 $E\subset\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ 在每个领先方向都含 $\mathbb{Z}_p$ 的 $d$ 次多项式像，则 $\dim_H E\ge\dim_H V+1$，并给出联合块值多项式赋值、解析/有界系数集的赋值维数与覆盖数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $p$ 进欧几里得空间中含多项式像的集合的豪斯多夫维数下界问题，将经典维数不等式推广到分块多项式赋值与系数集情形，并刻画例外参数集的维数。

Method: 使用 Nadjimzadah 的多项式到直线提升映射，结合 Dhar 的素幂 Kakeya 集估计，以及由该估计导出的管极大不等式。

Result: 得到尖锐维数界 $\dim_H E\ge\dim_H V+1$；联合块值赋值的总入射维数至少为 1 加上像维数上确界；解析系数集下 Haar 几乎处处赋值达到该上确界，并控制像维数下降的参数集的 Hausdorff 维数；有界系数集与固定各向异性块尺度下，给出带公共 Haar 零例外集的定量覆盖比较。

Conclusion: 所证边界为尖锐，且赋值维数与覆盖数行为可由提升映射、Kakeya 估计与管极大不等式统一处理。

Abstract: Let $n,d\geq1$, and suppose that $E\subset \mathbb{Q}_p^n$ contains a degree-$d$ polynomial image of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ in every leading direction of a nonempty set $V\subset\mathbb{P}^{n-1}(\mathbb{Q}_p)$. We prove the sharp bound $\dim_H E\geq\dim_H V+1$. For joint block-valued polynomial evaluations, the total incidence dimension is at least one plus the supremum of the image dimensions. For analytic coefficient sets, Haar-almost every evaluation attains this supremum, and we bound the Hausdorff dimension of the parameters where the image dimension drops. For bounded coefficient sets and arbitrary fixed anisotropic block scales, we prove a quantitative covering comparison with a common Haar-null exceptional set. Outside this set, each evaluation's covering number dominates the maximum over any compact reference ball, up to any positive power loss at all sufficiently small scales. The proofs use the polynomial-to-line lifting map due to Nadjimzadah, together with Dhar's prime-power Kakeya set estimate and a tube maximal inequality derived from it.

</details>


### [3] [A new type of deterministic Salem sets and its spectrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22772)
*Chun-Kit Lai,Ruxi Shi,Yu-Hao Xie*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一类Cantor集，其Fourier维数与Hausdorff维数均等于给定值s∈(0,1]，并给出首个一维奇异Salem谱测度例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造Fourier维数与Hausdorff维数相等的Cantor集，并寻找奇异Salem谱测度（即具有指数正交基的奇异测度）的一维实例。

Method: 采用确定性的Cantor-Moran构造，收缩比为整数倒数；利用解析数论中的Weil界获得快速Fourier衰减；并取等权Cantor-Moran测度作为实现近最优Fourier衰减的测度。

Result: 对所有0<s≤1构造出Fourier维数与Hausdorff维数均为s的Cantor集；等权Cantor-Moran测度具有近最优Fourier衰减，且其L²空间存在指数正交基{e^{2πiλx}: λ∈Λ}。

Conclusion: 给出了R¹中首批奇异Salem谱测度，表明存在同时具有分数维数、近最优Fourier衰减与指数正交基的奇异测度。

Abstract: For all $0< s \le 1$, we provide a new deterministic construction of Cantor sets whose Fourier dimension and Hausdorff dimension are both equal to $s$. The construction is based on a straightforward Cantor-Moran construction with contraction ratios given by reciprocals of integers. The key tool to obtain the fast Fourier decay is due to the Weil bound in analytic number theory. Furthermore, we show that the natural equal-weighted Cantor-Moran measure is the desired measure admitting the near optimal Fourier decay and the measure admits an exponential orthonormal basis $\{e^{2πi λx}: λ\in Λ\}$ for its $L^2$ space. This gives the first examples of singular Salem spectral measures in ${\mathbb R}^1$.

</details>


### [4] [$P$-adic $s$-energy and Fourier dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22881)
*Cheng Liu,Lingmin Liao*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 将 $s$-能量理论推广到 $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$，给出 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 上有限 Borel 测度 $s$-能量关于 Fourier 系数的精确表示，并首次计算了 $p=2$ 情形下 $p$-adic Riesz 乘积测度的 Fourier 维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 $s$-能量理论主要建立在欧氏空间 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上，且其 Fourier 表示多为近似形式；同时 $p$-adic Riesz 乘积测度在 $p=2$ 情形下的 Fourier 维数此前未被研究。作者希望将相关理论推广至 $p$-adic 空间并弥补这一空白。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$ 上建立 $s$-能量理论；针对支集位于 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 的有限 Borel 测度，利用其 Fourier 系数给出 $s$-能量的精确表示；并据此计算 $p$-adic Riesz 乘积测度的 Fourier 维数。

Result: 得到了 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 上有限 Borel 测度 $s$-能量的 Fourier 系数精确表示，其强度优于 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中的近似表示；并求得 $p$-adic Riesz 乘积测度的 Fourier 维数，包括此前未被研究的 $p=2$ 情形。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $s$-能量理论成功拓展到 $p$-adic 框架，提供了更强的精确 Fourier 表示，并补充了 $p=2$ 时 Riesz 乘积测度 Fourier 维数的结果。

Abstract: We extend the theory of $s$-energy to $\mathbb{Q}_p^n$. We give an exact representation for the $s$-energy in terms of the Fourier coefficients of a finite Borel measure with support in $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$, which is stronger than the approximate representation in $\mathbb{R}^n$. We also compute the Fourier dimension of the $p$-adic Riesz product measure, even in the case $p=2$, which has never been studied before.

</details>


### [5] [On higher-order differential equations for Laguerre-Hahn orthogonal polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22922)
*Mohamed Khalfallah,Zélia da Rocha*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 提出一种构造性方法，可推导任意阶Laguerre--Hahn正交多项式的齐次线性微分方程，并可算法化实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中主要研究四阶微分方程，缺乏对任意阶微分方程的系统构造方法，尤其在半经典情形下。

Method: 基于Laguerre--Hahn形式的基本结构关系，递归构建结构关系层级，从而导出任意阶齐次线性微分方程；方法完全算法化并用Mathematica®实现。

Result: 四阶微分方程作为特例被自然恢复；对两个类似于Hermite的零类Laguerre--Hahn族，给出了显式结构关系和五阶、十阶微分方程；经典Hermite序列作为第一族的极限情形被恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了已有四阶结果，为Laguerre--Hahn正交多项式任意阶微分方程提供了系统、可算法的构造途径。

Abstract: In this work, we present a constructive method to derive homogeneous linear differential equations of arbitrary order for Laguerre--Hahn orthogonal polynomials. The approach is based on a hierarchy of structure relations, built recursively from the fundamental structure relation of the Laguerre--Hahn forms. The fourth-order differential equation, which has been extensively studied in the literature, is naturally recovered as a particular case of this general framework. Special attention is devoted to the semiclassical case. The method is fully algorithmic and has been implemented in {\it Mathematica$^{\circledR}$}. We apply it to two Laguerre--Hahn families of class zero analogous to Hermite, providing explicit structure relations and differential equations of orders five and ten. We also examine the classical Hermite sequence, which is recovered as a limiting case of the first family.

</details>


### [6] [On the Rajchman property of homogeneous self-affine measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23474)
*De-Jun Feng,Tian-Han Yi*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了傅里叶变换在无穷远处不趋于0的齐次自仿射测度，证明了整自仿射测度具有幂次傅里叶衰减当且仅当它绝对连续，并给出判定概率向量使相关自仿射测度绝对连续的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自仿射测度的傅里叶衰减行为与绝对连续性密切相关，但刻画傅里叶变换在无穷远处不趋于0的测度、以及幂次衰减与绝对连续性的等价关系尚缺乏完整的代数刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，并为判定绝对连续性提供可计算的方法。

Method: 采用代数方法对齐次自仿射测度的傅里叶变换进行完整刻画；针对整自仿射测度，建立幂次傅里叶衰减与绝对连续性之间的等价关系；对给定整仿射IFS，设计算法判定使关联自仿射测度绝对连续的概率向量。

Result: 得到傅里叶变换在无穷远处不趋于0的齐次自仿射测度的完整代数刻画；证明整自仿射测度具有幂次傅里叶衰减当且仅当其绝对连续；给出判定对应概率向量使自仿射测度绝对连续的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作以代数手段统一刻画了自仿射测度的傅里叶衰减与绝对连续性，所提算法为具体IFS下确定绝对连续测度提供了可操作途径。

Abstract: We provide a complete algebraic characterization of homogeneous self-affine measures whose Fourier transform does not tend to $0$ at infinity. Moreover, we prove that an integral self-affine measure has power Fourier decay if and only if it is absolutely continuous. For a given integral affine IFS, we also provide an algorithm for determining those probability vectors for which the associated self-affine measure is absolutely continuous.

</details>


### [7] [The Coulhon--Duong conjecture for the Riesz transform on complete Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23503)
*Rui Chen,Renjin Jiang,Bo Li,Hong-Quan Li*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了完备非紧黎曼流形上Riesz变换的弱(1,1)型估计（实值函数常数2），进而得到1<p≤2时L^p有界性，验证Coulhon–Duong猜想；方法基于次马尔可夫半群自伴算子的障碍分解，并推广到具有局部Hilbert微分计算的Dirichlet空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Riesz变换在一般完备非紧黎曼流形上的端点估计问题，即Coulhon–Duong猜想，摆脱以往所需的几何或热核假设。

Method: 对具有次马尔可夫半群的正自伴算子进行障碍分解（obstacle decomposition），将此分解应用于平移的平方根Laplacian，并利用Sobolev微分的局部性得到端点估计；随后推广到具有局部Hilbert微分计算的Dirichlet空间。

Result: Riesz变换为弱(1,1)型，实值函数情形下常数至多为2；因此在1<p≤2时L^p(M)上有界，常数仅依赖p；并在Dirichlet空间框架下得到相应结果。

Conclusion: 在无需几何或热核假设的条件下证明了Coulhon–Duong猜想，表明该端点估计的成立源于算子半群的次马尔可夫性与微分局部性这一更本质的结构。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a complete, non-compact Riemannian manifold. We prove that its Riesz transform is of weak type $(1,1)$, with constant $2$ for real-valued functions. Consequently, it is bounded on $L^p(M)$ for $1<p\leq2$, with constants depending only on $p$, which proves the Coulhon--Duong conjecture. The proof uses an obstacle decomposition for positive self-adjoint operators with sub-Markovian semigroups. Applying this decomposition to the shifted square-root Laplacian and using locality of the Sobolev differential yields the endpoint estimate without geometric or heat kernel assumptions.
  We also obtain the corresponding result for Dirichlet spaces admitting a local Hilbertian differential calculus.

</details>


### [8] [Endpoint estimates for commutators of singular integral operators associated with admissible functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23662)
*The Anh Bui,Xuan Thinh Duong,Luong Dang Ky*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在齐型空间上引入与容许函数ρ相关的奇异积分算子类，并在局部化BMO空间下建立交换子从Hardy空间到弱L¹、L¹及Hardy空间的有界性，且给出H¹_ρ上有界性的充分必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦于齐型空间上特定奇异积分算子与局部化BMO函数生成的交换子刻画问题。

Method: 构造容许函数ρ下的奇异积分算子框架，定义相关局部化BMO空间，系统研究交换子[b,T]的端点有界性并刻画其H¹_ρ有界性。

Result: 给出交换子从H¹_ρ到L^{1,∞}、L¹、H¹_ρ的有界性，并获得H¹_ρ上有界的充要条件，将其应用于分层Lie群上的Schrödinger算子及卷积型Laguerre算子。

Conclusion: 所得结果推广了经典框架下的理论，即使对R^n上的Schrödinger算子亦属新结果。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal X, d, μ)$ be a space of homogeneous type and let $ρ$ be an admissible function on $\mathcal X$. In this paper, we introduce a new class of singular integral operators associated with $ρ$, including a wide range of operators arising in harmonic analysis. For such an operator $T$ and a function $b$ belonging to suitable localized $\mathrm{BMO}$ spaces associated with $ρ$, which are strictly larger than the classical space $\mathrm{BMO}(\mathcal X)$, we establish the boundedness of the commutator $[b, T]$ from the Hardy space $H^1_ρ(\mathcal X)$ into $L^{1,\infty}(\mathcal X)$, $L^1(\mathcal X)$, and $H^1_ρ(\mathcal X)$. Moreover, the boundedness of $[b,T]$ on $H^1_ρ(\mathcal X)$ is characterized by necessary and sufficient conditions. We then apply this to investigate the boundedness of commutators of singular integrals in various settings, including Schrödinger operators on stratified Lie groups and Laguerre operators of convolution type. Our results are new even for Schrödinger operators on $\mathbb R^n$.

</details>


### [9] [Endpoint Estimates for Commutators of the Dirichlet Riesz Transform on the Half-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23672)
*The Anh Bui,Xuan Thinh Duong,Xuejing Huo,Luong Dang Ky*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在上半空间 Dirichlet 拉普拉斯算子的 Riesz 变换框架下，建立了限制型 Hardy 空间 $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 上交换子的端点理论：对任意 $b\in\mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb R^n_+)$，$[b,\mathfrak R]$ 从 $H^1_r$ 到 $L^{1,\infty}$ 有界；并给出强端点刻画 $[b,\mathfrak R]:H^1_r\to H^1_r$ 有界当且仅当 $b\in\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$，且 $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$ 范数与 $\mathrm{BMO}$ 范数及分量交换子 $H^1_r$ 算子范数之和定量等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Riesz 变换交换子的端点理论主要在欧氏全空间及 $L^p$、$H^1$ 等经典函数空间上发展，而带 Dirichlet 边界条件的上半空间因其几何特殊性（存在到边界的距离衰减机制）需要单独处理。已有结果多集中于弱型端点（$L^{1,\infty}$）或非临界情形，对于限制型 Hardy 空间 $H^1_r$ 上交换子的强型端点刻画及其与对数型 BMO 条件的内在联系尚不完整。本文旨在填补这一空白：一方面给出 $H^1_r$ 上交换子的弱 $(1,1)$ 型端点估计，另一方面刻画强端点有界性的精确函数类条件，说明 Dirichlet 几何在其中的本质作用。

Method: 以 Dirichlet 拉普拉斯算子的 Riesz 变换 $\mathfrak R=\nabla(-\Delta_D)^{-1/2}$ 为研究对象，在限制型 Hardy 空间 $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 上分析其与 $\mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 函数生成的交换子 $[b,\mathfrak R]$。采用端点情形（$p=1$）的交换子理论框架：先建立由 $H^1_r$ 到 $L^{1,\infty}$ 的弱型有界性；再引入刻画到边界距离的对数型 BMO 空间 $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$，通过分解交换子与逐分量估计，证明强型 $H^1_r\to H^1_r$ 有界性等价于 $b\in\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$，并比较 $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$ 范数、$\mathrm{BMO}$ 范数与分量交换子 $[b,\mathfrak R_j]$ 的 $H^1_r$ 算子范数之和。对数条件源于 Dirichlet 几何中距离边界衰减的权重结构。

Result: 对任意 $b\in\mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb R^n_+)$，交换子 $[b,\mathfrak R]$ 从 $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 到 $L^{1,\infty}(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 有界。进一步得到强端点刻画：$[b,\mathfrak R]:H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)\to H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ 有界当且仅当 $b\in\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}(\mathbb R^n_+)$。此外 $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$ 范数与 $\mathrm{BMO}$ 范数加上分量交换子 $[b,\mathfrak R_j]$ 的 $H^1_r$ 算子范数之和定量等价。

Conclusion: 本文在上半空间 Dirichlet 几何下完整刻画了 Riesz 变换交换子在限制型 Hardy 空间 $H^1_r$ 上的端点行为：弱型端点对全部 $\mathrm{BMO}$ 函数成立，而强型端点由对数型 $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$ 条件精确控制，且范数具有定量等价。对数条件记录到边界的距离信息，是 Dirichlet 几何的内在特征，表明边界几何通过对数权重进入交换子刻画，区别于全空间经典理论。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak R=\nabla(-Δ_D)^{-1/2}$ be the Riesz transform associated with the Dirichlet Laplacian on the upper half-space $\mathbb R^n_+$, and let $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ denote the Hardy space of restrictions. We establish an endpoint theory for commutators with $\mathfrak R$ on $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$. For every $b\in \mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb R^n_+)$, the commutator $[b,\mathfrak R]$ extends boundedly from $H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)$ to $L^{1,\infty}(\mathbb R^n_+)$. We then prove the sharp strong-endpoint characterization \[ [b,\mathfrak R]:H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+)\to H^1_r(\mathbb R^n_+) \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad b\in \mathrm{BMO}^{\log}(\mathbb R^n_+). \] Moreover, the $\mathrm{BMO}^{\log}$ norm is quantitatively equivalent to the sum of the usual $\mathrm{BMO}$ norm and the $H^1_r$ operator norms of the component commutators $[b,\mathfrak R_j]$. The logarithmic condition records the distance to the boundary and is therefore intrinsic to the Dirichlet geometry.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a unified spectral and complex analytic theory of the Lame equation through its canonical Heun realization. Starting with ellipsoidal separation, we derive essential elements, including the Lame-preserving anharmonic parameter action and finite accessory spectral polynomials. We construct the global Heun monodromy representation on the four-punctured sphere, connected to the (2,2,2,2) pillowcase orbifold and the SL(2,C) character surface. The accessory parameter is framed within a Riemann-Hilbert map that links spectral values to monodromy conditions. Utilizing Seifert van Kampen theory reveals the fundamental group of the punctured sphere, and the Nielsen Schreier rank formula applies to monodromy coverings. The vanishing of higher homotopy groups for unbranched covers and the emergence of a Riemann-Hurwitz genus formula post-compactification are noted. Colored Schreier graphs are employed for a visual calculus of the covering data, connecting elliptic geometry and topology.

Abstract: We consider a discrete coagulation-fragmentation-diffusion system on a bounded domain of $\mathbb{R}^d$. In dimension $d\leq 2$, we prove mass conservation (that is absence of gelation) when the coagulation coefficient satisfies the critical additive bound $a_{i, j} \leq C(i + j)$ for some constant $C > 0$. We thereby close the remaining gap between the spatially homogeneous setting and the spatially inhomogeneous diffusive setting. Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^2$ be a smooth bounded domain containing the origin and invariant under reflection across the coordinate axes, and let $0<λ<λ_1(Ω)$, where $Λ_1 (Ω)$ is the first eigenvalue for $-Δ$ on $Ω$ under Dirichlet boundary conditions. ### [17] [On the Zero-Mass Problem of Bandle, Levine, and Zhang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22458)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方程 \(u_t-\Delta u=|u|^p+w(x)\) 的临界行为此前仅在 \(w\) 具有正质量（\(\int w>0\)）的情形被理解。Bandle、Levine与Zhang于2000年提出零总质量情形（\(\int_{\mathbb{R}^N}w\,dx=0\)）作为开问题，其关键在于零质量扰动既不提供"净能量"、又不可忽略，因此临界指数的判定并不显然，二十余年来未获解决。

Method: 针对 \(N\ge3\)、\(p>1\)、\(w\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^N)\) 非平凡且总质量为零的情形，在 \(1<p\le N/(N-2)\) 范围内利用弱解框架下的先验估计与非线性抛物方程的临界性分析（结合热核/位势估计与零质量条件带来的抵消效应）证明整体弱解的不存在性。

Result: 证明了当 \(1<p\le N/(N-2)\) 时该 Cauchy 问题不存在整体弱解。结合已知的"数据充分小时存在整体解"的超临界结果，刻画了存在与不存在两种regime的分界。

Conclusion: 零总质量情形下的临界指数仍为 \(p_c=N/(N-2)\)，与正质量情形相同；这一结论完整回答了Bandle-Levine-Zhang提出的开问题，说明在线性项总质量为零时扰动并不改变临界指数。

Abstract: We study the critical behavior of the inhomogeneous semilinear heat equation \[ u_t-Δu=|u|^p+w(x) \qquad\text{in }(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^N, \] where \(N\geq3\), \(p>1\), and \(w\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^N)\) is nontrivial and has zero total mass: \[ \int_{\mathbb{R}^N}w(x)\,dx=0. \] This zero-mass case was posed as an open problem by Bandle, Levine, and Zhang in 2000 and has remained unresolved since then. We provide a complete answer to this problem by proving nonexistence of global weak solutions for \[ 1<p\leq\frac{N}{N-2}. \] Combined with the known supercritical existence result for sufficiently small data, this shows that the critical exponent separating the nonexistence and existence regimes remains \[ p_c=\frac{N}{N-2}, \] the same as in the positive-mass case.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hill球涡与Norbury涡环是理想化、无旋且非光滑的涡环模型，其涡度由涡核上的缩放特征函数给出。核心动机是回答"去奇异化"问题：这些非光滑行波解能否被光滑行波解逼近，以及允许旋流后逼近性质是否改变。

Method: 基于Stokes流函数的五维重构，结合精细薄域分析、奇异参数Newton型非线性反演以及拓扑不动点论证进行构造。分别处理无旋子空间与允许旋流两种情形。

Conclusion: 这是首次验证小通量Hill–Norbury族在光滑涡环集合中并非孤立，即该族处处不被孤立于光滑涡环之外。

Abstract: An investigation of local aspects of the space of axisymmetric traveling-wave solutions to the three-dimensional Euler equations near Hill's zero-flux spherical vortex and Norbury's small-flux vortex rings is the endeavor undertaken herein. The aforementioned family consists of idealized, swirl-free, and nonsmooth objects, their azimuthal relative vorticity being prescribed by a scaled characteristic function of the compact vortex core. Abstract: We study the Bregman proximal point method for the obstacle problem, a fundamental variational inequality arising in contact mechanics, optimal design, and mathematical finance. We first establish a well-posedness and strict feasibility theory for these subproblems. We then analyze the convergence of the resulting iteration in the $H^1$-norm. Our convergence analysis is based on a sequential strict local minimality inequality. We show abstractly how the order of minimality determines the convergence rate. Assuming that the initial guess lies above the exact solution and a one-sided Bregman growth condition holds, we establish sequential strict local minimality of order $s \geq 2$ and derive sublinear convergence rates in the $H^1$-norm of the form $O(k^{-ζ})$, where $ζ\in(1/2,1]$ depends on the choice of Legendre function. For the Shannon and Tsallis entropies, we show that these rates are sharp in a uniform worst-case sense. Under additional assumptions on the free boundary and the obstacle, we establish sequential strict local minimality of order $s = 1$ with the Shannon and Spence entropies. The non-conservative nature and the coexistence of two distinct-speed Huygens waves demand dedicated nonlinear coupling analysis and add further complexity. Our study reveals fundamentally different physical behaviors of this non-conservative two-phase model under equal and unequal interfacial pressures. For equal pressure, the model admits a stationary diffusion wave comprising a single Huygens wave, analogous to classical Navier-Stokes equations. Abstract: The Whitham equation has given its name to a wider family of nonlocal, nonlinear equations with very weak dispersion, which all feature highest waves. Abstract: The branched transport problem is a nonconvex and nonsmooth variational optimization problem on normal $1$-currents in $\mathbb{R}^n$ with prescribed boundary. Abstract: We construct global finite-energy weak solutions for a 1D barotropic Navier--Stokes--Maxwell system with displacement current and the algebraic Ohm law.   A key ingredient is a weak-to-strong compactness principle for the Maxwell--Ohm subsystem: weak convergence of the velocity coefficients in \(L^2_tH^1_x\), together with strong convergence of the initial fields, yields strong electromagnetic trajectories in \(C_tL^2_x\). This permits identification of the weak current and the distributional Lorentz force without strong convergence of the velocity. The electromagnetic closure is combined with the classical artificial-viscosity and artificial-pressure construction for compressible flow. A density-primitive effective-flux argument identifies the physical pressure and eliminates the physical and artificial pressure-concentration defects.

Abstract: We establish a spectral weak-to-strong stability criterion and a sharp Sobolev dichotomy for Maxwell--Ohm evolution with prescribed velocity on $\T^d$, $d=2,3$. Uniform spatial spectral tightness, together with weak convergence of each fixed spatial Fourier mode and strong convergence of the electromagnetic initial states, yields strong convergence of the electromagnetic fields, weak convergence of the currents, and distributional convergence of the Lorentz forces. In particular, weak convergence of the velocity coefficients in $L^2_tH^s_x$ suffices when $s>d/2$.
  At the critical index $s=d/2$, this conclusion fails sharply. We construct smooth divergence-free velocities converging strongly to zero in the critical Sobolev space, together with electromagnetic initial states converging strongly to zero, while the corresponding terminal fields remain of order one and the currents stay bounded. In two dimensions the construction is based on a moving logarithmic core, whereas in three dimensions it uses a Fourier-capacity core built from Leray-projected lattice modes and a polarized six-component Maxwell packet with exact magnetic divergence constraint.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 假设T的谱位于角扇区{ρe^{it}:0&lt;ρ≤1,|t|≤c}（c&lt;π），本质谱位于{e^{it}:|t|≤c_e}。通过主对数定义实幂，建立分数轨道综合与圆弧上投影向量值指数综合之间的紧扰动等价；对一致分离时间集Λ引入单侧下均匀密度D_+^-(Λ)与对数块密度L(Λ)，并将方法推广至广义差商与每单元一点采样。

Result: 该归约使半Fredholm性质与Fredholm指标得以转移。对一致分离Λ，当D_+^-(Λ)&gt;c_e/π且L(Λ)&gt;c/π时构成框架；在L(Λ)&gt;c/π阈值处完备性只需谱落在相应角扇区的可逆算子与完备整数多轨道。当L(Λ)&gt;2c/π时，互补性质等价于通道循环子空间中的有限条件，并给出实相位恢复结论。广义差商获得相同紧归约及带重数的框架定理。对单元长度r的任意采样，充分原始采样条件为rc&lt;π与2rc_e&lt;π；本质谱含于{1}时全范围rc&lt;π成立，无限制类中尖锐普适范围为rc&lt;π/2；差商预处理对所有算子恢复rc&lt;π并将精确碰撞转为对数导数数据。

Abstract: We study frames of unilateral iterations generated by finitely many vectors under an invertible bounded operator $T$. Suppose that the spectrum of $T$ lies in $\{ρe^{it}:0<ρ\le1,\ |t|\le c\}$, where $c<π$, and that its essential spectrum lies in $\{e^{it}:|t|\le c_{\mathrm e}\}$, where $0\le c_{\mathrm e}\le c$. Real powers are defined through the principal logarithm. Our structural result identifies fractional-orbit synthesis, up to an invertible map and a compact perturbation, with synthesis of projected vector-valued exponentials on an arc containing the essential spectrum. Semi-Fredholm properties and Fredholm index therefore transfer.
  For a uniformly separated temporal set $Λ$, this reduction gives a frame when its one-sided lower uniform density satisfies $D_+^-(Λ)>c_{\mathrm e}/π$ and its logarithmic block density satisfies $L(Λ)>c/π$. Completeness at the latter threshold requires only an invertible operator with spectrum in the corresponding angular sector and a complete integer multiorbit. At $L(Λ)>2c/π$, the complement property is equivalent to a finite condition in channel-cyclic subspaces, with real phase-retrieval consequences.
  Generalized divided differences admit the same compact reduction and the analogous frame theorem with multiplicities counted. For arbitrary one-point-per-cell sampling with cell length $r$, the sufficient raw-sampling conditions are $rc<π$ and $2rc_{\mathrm e}<π$. Thus, the full range $rc<π$ holds for raw samples when the essential spectrum is contained in $\{1\}$; over the unrestricted class, the sharp universal range is $rc<π/2$. Divided-difference preconditioning restores $rc<π$ for all operators considered and converts exact collisions into logarithmic-derivative data.

</details>

Abstract: We establish several sufficient conditions ensuring that a perturbed family of operators in a given Hilbert-Schmidt frame for a separable Hilbert space remains a Hilbert-Schmidt frame. Several examples are presented to illustrate the applicability of the results.

Abstract: Let \(\Gm=\prod_{k\ge0}\mathbb Z_{m_k}\) be a Vilenkin group that is not necessarily bounded, i.e., \(\sup_k m_k=\infty\). We prove that, for every UMD Banach space \(X\) and every \(1<p<\infty\), the Vilenkin partial-sum operators are uniformly bounded on \(L^p(\Gm;X)\), with a bound depending only on \(p\) and the UMD constant of \(X\), and not on \(\mathbf m\). This resolves an open problem arising from the work of Clément et al.~\cite{ClementDePagterSukochevWitvliet2000} and later recorded explicitly in the book of Hytönen et al.~\cite[p.~362]{HNVWI}. The proof reduces the partial-sum estimate, via a Paley conjugation identity and a tangent-sequence decoupling argument, to a decoupling inequality for Fourier projections on finite cyclic groups, which appears to be new. The same approach also yields \(\mathcal R\)-boundedness for the family of partial-sum operators associated with the finer block decomposition, thereby resolving another related problem communicated to us by Fedor Sukochev.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the characterizations of Schatten \(p\)-class Toeplitz operators on smoothly bounded convex domains of finite type in \(\C^n\). On one hand, by using discrete Kobayashi lattice, we characterize the Schatten \(p\)-class Toeplitz operators by employing a probabilistic selection method for \(0<p<1\). At the same time, we show that the characterizations obtained by Kobayashi lattice are equivalent to the Berezin transform for \(p > \frac{n}{n+1}\). On the other hand, as an application, the criterion gives a geometric characterization for Schatten \(p\)-class weighted composition operators for \(0<p<2\) and also gives an analytic characterization for \(p>\frac{2n}{n+1}\). Our main results give an answer to the question that find a geometric or analytic characterization of the Schatten \(p\)-class weighted composition operators whenever \(0<p<2\) raised by Xiao-Yang-Yuan \cite{Xiao2026}.

Abstract: Recent years have seen significant interest in finding equivalent norms on Cesàro function spaces together with their optimal equivalence constants. In this work, we continue this line of research by deriving sharp two-sided inequalities relating the $L^p$-norms of $\mathcal{C}_m f$ and $\mathcal{C}_n f$, where $\mathcal{C}_k$ is the generalized Cesàro operator of order $k \in \mathbb{N}$ acting on non-negative measurable functions $f$ on $(0,\infty)$. A parallel study is also conducted for the corresponding adjoint operators.

</details>

By proving the exact value of this distance, we confirm a conjecture originally formulated by A. Ben Said, S. Boza, and J. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 利用底层空间X的拓扑性质，完整刻画了C_k(X)的Asplund性质，建立了该性质与X拓扑结构之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 该综述为局部凸空间中的Asplund性质提供了统一框架，表明纯拓扑方法可替代可微性技术处理相关问题，且内容自包含、适合专家与初学者阅读。

Abstract: The Asplund property plays an important role in Banach space theory due to its connections with differentiability properties of continuous convex functions, optimization problems, and the weak topology of Banach spaces. Motivated by the variety of nonequivalent definitions proposed in the literature for locally convex spaces, in this survey we provide a unified framework for studying the Asplund property beyond the Banach space setting.
  The main object of our study is the locally convex space of continuous functions $C_k(X)$ endowed with the compact-open topology, where $X$ is an arbitrary Tychonoff space. We completely characterize the Asplund property for $C_k(X)$ in terms of topological properties of the underlying space $X$.
  As an essential step, we revisit the proof of several classical results, including the Namioka--Phelps theorem. Our approach is independent of differentiability techniques and relies solely on topological methods.
  The exposition is self-contained, and all major results are provided with complete proofs, making the paper accessible to both specialists and newcomers.

</details>


### [82] [The weak maximizing property, compact perturbations and duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23198)
*Domingo García,Manuel Maestre,Damián Pinasco,Ignacio Zalduendo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 构造了一族与 $\ell_2$ 同构且 Banach-Mazur 距离任意接近 1 的实自反 Banach 空间 $X_c$，使得 $(X_c,X_c)$ 不满足弱极大化性质（WMP），而其对偶 $(X_c^*,X_c^*)$ 满足 WMP；从而说明 WMP 在自反情形下不具对偶稳定性，且紧扰动性质不蕴含 WMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱极大化性质（WMP）与紧扰动性质在自反 Banach 空间中的关系，特别是 WMP 是否在对偶变换下保持，以及紧扰动性质能否推出 WMP，即使在任意接近 Hilbert 空间的情形下。

Method: 构造单参数族 $(X_c)_{0<c<1}$，令其与 $\ell_2$ 同构且 $d_{\mathrm{BM}}(X_c,\ell_2)\le c^{-1}$。用不取范数但具有非弱零极大化序列的对角算子见证 $(X_c,X_c)$ 上 WMP 失效；在对偶侧，用分离块估计经对偶化导出反向勾股不等式与 Opial 性质的定量形式，结合性质 $(M)$ 证明 $(X_c^*,X_c^*)$ 满足 WMP。另外证明自反对的紧扰动性质是自对偶的。

Result: $(X_c,X_c)$ 不满足 WMP，而 $(X_c^*,X_c^*)$ 满足 WMP；每个 $(X_c,X_c)$ 因 WMP 蕴含紧扰动性质而具有紧扰动性质。

Conclusion: 紧扰动性质即使在任意接近 Hilbert 空间时也不蕴含 WMP；在自反情形下 WMP 不具备对偶稳定性。

Abstract: We construct a one-parameter family $(X_c)_{0<c<1}$ of real reflexive Banach spaces, isomorphic to $\ell_2$ and satisfying $d_{\mathrm{BM}}(X_c,\ell_2)\le c^{-1}$, such that $(X_c,X_c)$ fails the weak maximizing property while $(X_c^*,X_c^*)$ has it. The failure of the WMP for $X_c$ is witnessed by a diagonal operator which does not attain its norm but admits a non-weakly null maximizing sequence. On the dual side, a separated-block estimate yields, after dualization, a reverse Pythagorean inequality and a quantitative form of the Opial property; together with property $(M)$, this gives the weak maximizing property for $X_c^*$. We also show that the compact perturbation property is self-dual for reflexive pairs. Since the weak maximizing property implies the compact perturbation property, each $(X_c,X_c)$ has the compact perturbation property. Consequently, the compact perturbation property does not imply the weak maximizing property even arbitrarily close to Hilbert space, and the weak maximizing property is not stable under duality in the reflexive setting.

</details>

*Nouffou Diarra,Pokou Nagacy,Bérenger Akon Kpata*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究Fofana空间及其前对偶空间$\mathcal{H}(p'(\cdot),q',\alpha')$的赋范Köthe结构，识别Köthe对偶与二次对偶，并获得前对偶，同时证明这两个空间均不自反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fofana空间近期受到关注并在偏微分方程中有应用，但其变指数情形下前对偶空间的Köthe结构及对偶性质尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 在$1\leq p(\cdot)\leq \alpha\leq q\leq \infty$条件下，基于Wiener混合空间框架，研究变指数Fofana空间$(L^{p(\cdot)},\ell^{q})^\alpha$及其前对偶空间$\mathcal{H}(p'(\cdot),q',\alpha')$的赋范Köthe结构，并利用Köthe对偶理论进行刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了变指数Fofana空间的对偶理论，揭示了其非自反性，为其在偏微分方程中的应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Fofana spaces, introduced on the basis of Wiener amalgam spaces, have attracted some interest recently and have found applications in the field of partial differential equations. In this paper, we study the normed Köthe structure of the predual spaces $\mathcal{H}(p^{\prime}(\cdot),q^{\prime},α^{\prime})$ of Fofana spaces with variable exponent $(L^{p(\cdot)}, \ell^{q})^α$, where $1\leq p(\cdot) \leq α\leq q\leq \infty$. As an application of this investigation, we identify the Köthe duals and Köthe biduals of these two spaces, and we obtain a predual of $\mathcal{H}(p^{\prime}(\cdot),q^{\prime},α^{\prime})$. Finally, we prove that neither $(L^{p(\cdot)}, \ell^{q})^α$ nor $\mathcal{H}(p^{\prime}(\cdot),q^{\prime},α^{\prime})$ is reflexive.

</details>


### [84] [Dimension-free Riesz transform estimates on biased hypercubes via non-tracial baby Fock models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24458)
*Hun Hee Lee,Zhendong Xu,Sang-gyun Youn,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀乘积测度下超立方体上的无维数Riesz变换估计，通过将偏置超立方体转移至非迹baby Fock模型，证明了无维数的Meyer不等式，并在权重有界假设下获得Riesz平方函数的无维数估计，同时推广至q-Araki-Woods代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Riesz变换的维数无关估计是调和分析的核心问题；均匀超立方体情形已有成熟结果，而非均匀乘积测度下carré du champ平方函数与无权Riesz平方函数一般不再重合，因此需要建立适应非均匀结构的新算子与转移方法。

Method: 采用从偏置超立方体到非迹baby Fock模型的转移方法；引入新的湮灭算子及偏置超立方体上的Riesz变换，推导其与非迹baby Fock模型Riesz变换的显式关系；核心分析工具为受Pisier与Lust-Piquard启发的旋转论证，用于证明非交换Riesz变换的无维数L_p估计。

Result: 对2≤p<∞，证明关联carré du champ的无维数Meyer不等式，常数不依赖于维数与乘积测度；在权重参数额外有界假设下，进一步得到无权Riesz平方函数的无维数估计；同一分析机制还给出q-Araki-Woods代数上的无维数Riesz变换估计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非均匀乘积测度超立方体上无维数Riesz变换估计的完整框架，揭示了偏置情形与均匀情形的本质差异，并通过非迹baby Fock模型的转移与旋转论证将结果推广至非交换q-Araki-Woods代数。

Abstract: We study dimension-free Riesz transform estimates on hypercubes equipped with non-uniform product measures. For $2\leq p<\infty$, we prove a dimension-free Meyer inequality for the associated carré du champ, with constants independent of both the dimension and the product measure. In contrast to the uniform case, the carré-du-champ square function does not in general coincide with the unweighted Riesz square function. Under an additional boundedness assumption on the weight parameters, we further obtain dimension-free estimates for the latter.
  Our proof is based on a transference from the biased hypercube to a non-tracial baby Fock model. We introduce new annihilation operators and the associated Riesz transforms on the biased hypercube, and derive explicit formulas relating them to the Riesz transforms on the non-tracial baby Fock model. The main analytic ingredient is a dimension-free $L_p$-estimate for the resulting noncommutative Riesz transform, proved by a rotation argument in the spirit of Pisier and Lust-Piquard. The same analytic mechanism also yields dimension-free Riesz transform estimates on $q$-Araki--Woods algebras.

</details>

Result: 给出了 $\mathrm{BMO}_\omega^p$ 符号 Toeplitz 算子有界性的 Berezin 型变换刻画；对局部可积符号给出 $T_f^\omega$ 有界与紧的若干充分条件；对共解析符号，得到 $T_{\bar f}^\omega:A_\omega^1\to A_\omega^1$ 与 $H_{\bar f}^\omega:A_\omega^1\to L_\omega^1$ 有界性的充分必要条件。

Conclusion: 在 $\mathcal{D}$-加权 Bergman 空间上建立了 Toeplitz 与 Hankel 算子有界性、紧性的系列判定准则，特别给出了 $p=1$ 及共解析符号情形下 $T_{\bar f}^\omega$ 与 $H_{\bar f}^\omega$ 有界性的充要条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we focus on the boundedness and compactness of the Toeplitz operators and the Hankel operators on the $\mathcal{D}$-weighted Bergman spaces $A_ω^p$ ($1\leq p<\infty$). In particular, the boundedness of Toeplitz operators with $\mathrm{BMO}_ω^p$-symbols on $A_ω^p$ is characterized in terms of the Berezin-type transform. Several sufficient conditions for the Toeplitz operators $T_f^ω$ with locally integrable symbols to be bounded (resp. compact) for each $1\leq p<\infty$ are presented. Moreover, sufficient and necessary conditions for the boundedness of the Toeplitz operator $T_{\bar{f}}^ω:A_ω^1\rightarrow A_ω^1$ and the Hankel operator $H_{\bar{f}}^ω:A_ω^1\rightarrow L_ω^1$ with co-analytic symbol are established.

</details>


### [86] [Absolutely summing operators on Bergman spaces over convex domains of finite type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23653)
*Dong Jianxiang,Xu Chunxu*

Main category: math.FA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限型凸域上Bergman空间的绝对求和性质：希望刻画绝对r-求和的Carleson嵌入与Hankel算子，并在求和范数下估计最佳逼近误差，同时获得Toeplitz算子谱渐近以导出最优逼近速率。

Method: 利用McNeal球上归一化质量构成的对角算子刻画绝对r-求和嵌入；用局部L^q到全纯函数的距离配合适当体积权重建立大Hankel判别准则；证明结合一致局部核估计、核合成以及两个固定尺度上的解析分解；用局部Taylor多项式给出显式秩与误差界，并转移到Hankel算子。

Result: 对1<p<∞、1≤q,r<∞刻画了绝对r-求和Carleson嵌入A^p(Ω)→L^q(μ)；对1<p,q<∞、1≤r<∞得到大Hankel判别准则，均含上下界范数估计。当p=2或q=2时界可转移至一般Hankel算子；合适原子嵌入与其对角模型的最佳逼近误差相差常数。某族Hankel算子含对角理想的补子空间，在Hilbert情形对幂序列具有匹配逼近速率。对有限型椭球上的正Toeplitz算子，计算三种区域下的精确谱渐近，含临界对数因子与几何符号的首项常数，并给出求和范数下的最优逼近速率。应用于复合算子与Volterra算子，包括端点q=1。

Conclusion: 通过McNeal球对角化与局部核估计，统一刻画了有限型凸域上Bergman空间的绝对求和嵌入与Hankel有界性，并将Toeplitz谱渐近转化为求和范数下的最优逼近速率，覆盖复合与Volterra算子及端点q=1。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb C^n$ be a smoothly bounded convex domain of finite type. We characterize absolute summability on its Bergman spaces and estimate approximation errors in the summing norm. For $1<p<\infty$ and $1\le q,r<\infty$, we characterize absolutely $r$-summing Carleson embeddings $A^p(Ω)\to L^q(μ)$ by diagonal operators formed from normalized masses on McNeal balls. For $1<p,q<\infty$ and $1\le r<\infty$, we obtain a corresponding big Hankel criterion using local $L^q$ distances to holomorphic functions, with the appropriate volume weights. Both criteria give upper and lower norm estimates. The proofs combine uniform local nuclear estimates, kernel synthesis, and an analytic decomposition at two fixed scales. Local Taylor polynomials give explicit rank and error bounds for the embeddings. These bounds transfer to general Hankel operators when $p=2$ or $q=2$. Suitable atomic embeddings have the same best approximation errors as their diagonal models, up to constants. A family of Hankel operators contains a complemented copy of the diagonal ideal and has matching approximation rates for power sequences in the Hilbert case. For positive Toeplitz operators on finite-type ellipsoids, we compute exact spectral asymptotics in three regimes, including the critical logarithmic factor and the leading constants for geometric symbols. These asymptotics give optimal approximation rates in the summing norm. Applications to composition and Volterra operators include the target endpoint $q=1$.

</details>


### [87] [Closed Range and Essential Norms of Composition Operators on Weighted Dirichlet Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23667)
*Caixing Gu,Li He,Xiaofeng Wang,Yuanhao Yan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究加权Dirichlet空间$\mathcal{D}_\alpha$上有界复合算子$C_\varphi$的闭值域性与本质范数。对$-1<\alpha<0$，证明了闭值域与逆向Carleson性质、逆向Carleson圆盘条件（针对计数测度$\mu_{\varphi,\alpha}$）等的等价刻画，并在一致尾条件下给出闭值域特征。对所有$\alpha>-1$，建立了基于广义Nevanlinna计数函数局部平均的双边本质范数估计及边界尾积分精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合算子的闭值域性与本质范数是算子理论的核心问题。加权Dirichlet空间$\mathcal{D}_\alpha$在$\alpha<0$时与经典Dirichlet、Hardy空间性质差异显著，此区间刻画尚不完善；同时本质范数的精确估计与边界刻画有待深入。

Method: 对映照像经从单连通子域去掉紧子集得到的情形，结合逆向Carleson测度与圆盘条件、加权面积密度一致性、边界圆周包含及外环包含等几何测度工具刻画闭值域；引入一致尾条件；利用广义Nevanlinna计数函数的局部平均、边界尾积分及Berezin变换、消失振荡等分析手段建立本质范数估计。

Result: 对$-1<\alpha<0$：闭值域等价于逆向Carleson性质、$\mu_{\varphi,\alpha}$的逆向Carleson圆盘条件、像的均匀加权面积密度、$\mathbb{T}\subset\overline{\varphi(\mathbb{D})}$及外环包含；并给出一致尾条件下的闭值域刻画。对$\alpha>-1$：得到本质范数的双边局部平均估计与边界尾积分精确公式；当归一化计数密度在边界具有消失振荡时，得到以边界上极限、Berezin变换和局部平均表示的精确公式。

Conclusion: 加权Dirichlet空间上复合算子的闭值域性由若干等价几何-测度条件共同刻画，本质范数可通过广义Nevanlinna计数函数的局部与边界行为精确表达，统一了不同$\alpha$区间的结论。

Abstract: We study closed range and essential norms of bounded composition operators $C_\varphi$ on weighted Dirichlet spaces $\mathcal{D}_α$. For $-1<α<0$, we first consider maps whose images are obtained by removing a compact subset from a simply connected subdomain of $\mathbb{D}$. In this setting, closed range is equivalent to the Reverse Carleson property and the Reverse Carleson Disk Condition for the counting measure $μ_{\varphi,α}$, uniform weighted area density of the image, $\mathbb{T}\subset\overline{\varphi(\mathbb{D})}$, and the inclusion of an outer annulus in the image. We also obtain a closed range characterization under the Uniform Tail Condition. For all $α>-1$, we establish two-sided essential norm estimates in terms of local averages of the generalized Nevanlinna counting function and an exact formula using boundary tail integrals. If the normalized counting density has vanishing oscillation at the boundary, we obtain exact formulas in terms of its boundary limsup, Berezin transform, and local averages.

</details>


### [88] [Normalized Orbit Frames and Essential Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23804)
*James Tian*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文刻画了允许规范化轨道框架的正规算子的本质谱，结论是其本质谱恰为具有正Haar测度的单位圆闭子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道框架是框架理论、算子理论与遍历理论交叉的重要研究对象，但允许规范化轨道框架的正规算子其谱结构此前缺乏完整刻画，尤其是本质谱部分。

Conclusion: 给出了正规算子存在规范化轨道框架的充要条件，完整刻画了此类算子的本质谱结构。

Abstract: We characterize the essential spectra of normal operators admitting normalized orbit frames. These are exactly closed subsets of the unit circle with positive Haar measure.

Abstract: An approximate action of the pair groupoid of a metric space determines an action of its Lipschitz-thin groupoid on complete extended metric fibers, uniquely characterized by its local sewing estimate. We prove this assertion under a superlinear three-point defect estimate and a Lipschitz bound for finite products. As a result, we obtain a positive proof of Curry and Manchon's sewing conjecture [2], with the necessary path-scaling correction to its estimate. Further restriction to isotropy constructs the based holonomy anticipated in their Remark 4.15 as a representation of based Lipschitz loops modulo thin equivalence. Rectangular comparison estimates give full relative Lipschitz-homotopy descent, in particular under their strong knitting hypothesis and under any three point estimate of total order greater than two. We prove the corresponding flatness and basepoint-covariance statements. An area model shows that the order threshold is sharp. We also separate the exponential estimate from the general knitting assumptions and exhibit a compact disk metric for which strong knitting does not imply invariance under merely continuous homotopy. As an application, for controlled fields on a metric space, we prove that the classical rough integral descends to the thin groupoid and admits substitution with a common rough controller.

</details>


### [90] [A General Framework for Operator Continuity between Riesz Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23893)

Abstract: We introduce and study $\mathcal{FG}$-order continuous operators between Riesz spaces, based on Tantrawan's generalized (unbounded) order convergence. This framework includes several familiar classes of operators and allows their properties to be studied in a unified setting. We establish order boundedness and lattice-theoretic results; in particular, we show that strongly order continuous operators are automatically order bounded and prove that the modulus of an order bounded $\mathcal{FG}$-order continuous operator remains $\mathcal{FG}$-order continuous under suitable assumptions. The latter result gives an affirmative answer to a previously posed problem for strongly order continuous operators. We also establish a general result ensuring classical order continuity, from which order continuity results for several familiar classes of operators follow. Furthermore, we characterize, under suitable hypotheses, when the space of order bounded $\mathcal{FG}$-order continuous operators is a band. Finally, we prove an extension theorem for positive $\mathcal{FG}$-order continuous operators, providing a counterpart of Veksler's extension theorem in this framework.

</details>


### [91] [Reflexive and strictly convex Banach spaces without Property (H)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23996)

TL;DR: 该文证明性质(H)在一大类自反严格凸Banach空间中失效，并给出$\ell^s$直和下$L^{p_j}$空间反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性质(H)是与不动点性质相关的几何条件。此前已有不少空间被验证具有或可能具有性质(H)，作者希望判定自反严格凸空间这一宽泛类是否必然保持性质(H)，从而界定其适用范围。

Method: 构造性反例：取$\ell^s$意义下的$L^{p_j}(\Omega_j,\mu_j)$无穷直和，要求$p_j$无界且每个分量无穷维，利用这些条件下构造出破坏性质(H)的向量序列。

Conclusion: 自反严格凸性不足以保证性质(H)；性质(H)对$\ell^s$直和及$p_j$无界的情形并不稳定。

Abstract: We prove that Property $(H)$ fails for a broad class of reflexive strictly convex Banach spaces. More generally, the real Banach space \[ \Big(\bigoplus_{j=1}^{\infty} L^{p_j}(Ω_j,μ_j)\Big)_{\ell^s} \] does not have Property $(H)$ whenever $1\le s<\infty$, $1\le p_j<\infty$, $\sup_j p_j=+\infty$, and each $L^{p_j}(Ω_j,μ_j)$ is infinite-dimensional.

</details>


### [92] [Bounds on the Minimum Eigenvalue Modulus for Hadamard Products of $\mathbf{M}$- and $\mathbf{H}$-Matrices and Their Inverses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24019)
*Bharat Pratap Chauhan,Samir Mondal,Sushmitha P*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵 Hadamard 积 $A\circ A^{-1}$ 特征值最小模 $q(A\circ A^{-1})$ 的界，涉及 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵与 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵两类。对 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵，讨论下界 $2/n$ 的锐性及 Jacobi 迭代矩阵相关结论的逆命题；对 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵，证明 $A\circ A^{-1}$ 仍为可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵，给出 $q$ 可任意接近零的结论，并通过比较矩阵恢复下界 $2/n$，最后给出正对角可对称化情形的上界及等式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $q(A\circ A^{-1})$ 与正对角可对称化密切相关，经典结果给出了可逆 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵的界 $2/n \leq q(A\circ A^{-1}) \leq 1$。作者希望澄清下界 $2/n$ 的锐性、考察涉及 Jacobi 迭代矩阵结论的逆命题是否成立，并将研究从 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵推广到更广的 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵类。

Method: 对可逆 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵，分析下界 $2/n$ 的锐性，研究 $\rho(J_{A_k})\to 1$ 与 $q(A_k\circ A_k^{-1})\to 2/n$ 之间的关系，指出一般情形下不蕴含并识别使蕴含成立的矩阵类。对可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵，证明 $A\circ A^{-1}$ 的可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵性质；用比较矩阵 $\mathcal{M}(A)$ 的逆替代 $A^{-1}$，证明 $q(A\circ\mathcal{M}(A)^{-1})\geq 2/n$ 并给出涉及 $\mathcal{M}(A)$ 的 Jacobi 迭代矩阵的界；对正对角可对称化可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵建立上界并刻画不可约情形等号条件。

Result: 对 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵：一般情况下 $\rho(J_{A_k})\to 1$ 不蕴含 $q(A_k\circ A_k^{-1})\to 2/n$，但存在使该蕴含成立的矩阵类。对 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵：$A$ 可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵时 $A\circ A^{-1}$ 也是可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵；$q(A\circ A^{-1})$ 可任意接近零；但 $q(A\circ\mathcal{M}(A)^{-1})\geq 2/n$，并获得涉及 $\mathcal{M}(A)$ 的 Jacobi 迭代矩阵的进一步界。正对角可对称化可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵满足 $q(A\circ A^{-1})\leq 1$，不可约时刻画等号成立条件。

Conclusion: 本文将 $q(A\circ A^{-1})$ 的经典界从 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵推广到 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵：对一般可逆 $\mathbf{H}$-矩阵下界 $2/n$ 失效，但用比较矩阵的逆可恢复该下界；同时补全了 $\mathbf{M}$-矩阵情形下 Jacobi 迭代谱半径与 $q$ 收敛关系，并给出正对角可对称化情形的上界与等式刻画。

Abstract: The quantity $q(A\circ A^{-1})$, the minimum modulus of the eigenvalues of $A\circ A^{-1}$, arises naturally in connection with positive diagonal symmetrizability. For an invertible $\mathbf{M}$-matrix $A$ of order $n$, the classical bounds $\frac{2}{n}\leq q(A\circ A^{-1})\leq 1$ are known. We discuss the sharpness of the lower bound $\frac{2}{n}$ and investigate the converse of a related result involving the Jacobi iteration matrix. In particular, we show that $ρ(J_{A_k})\to 1$ does not, in general, imply $q(A_k\circ A_k^{-1})\to \frac{2}{n}$, and identify a class for which this implication holds.
  We then turn to invertible $\mathbf{H}$-matrices, a broader class that contains invertible $\mathbf{M}$-matrices. We show that $A\circ A^{-1}$ is an invertible $\mathbf{H}$-matrix whenever $A$ is an invertible $\mathbf{H}$-matrix. In contrast to the $\mathbf{M}$-matrix setting, $q(A\circ A^{-1})$ can be arbitrarily close to zero. However, replacing $A^{-1}$ by the inverse of the comparison matrix restores the classical lower bound: we prove that $q(A\circ\mathcal{M}(A)^{-1})\geq \frac{2}{n}$ and obtain further bounds involving the Jacobi iteration matrix of $\mathcal{M}(A)$. Finally, for positive diagonally symmetrizable invertible $\mathbf{H}$-matrices, we establish the upper bound $q(A\circ A^{-1})\leq1$ and, in the irreducible case, characterize when equality occurs.

</details>


### [93] [A purely metric characterization of supercritical Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24520)
*Ryan Alvarado,Efstathios Konstantinos Chrontsios Garitsis*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文给出了局部超临界Sobolev空间中连续映射的纯度量、无导数刻画：连续映射属于局部Sobolev空间当且仅当它是紧Hölder映射，并推广到Ahlfors正则度量空间与Banach空间取值的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Sobolev空间的刻画通常依赖弱导数或分布导数，而在度量空间等缺乏线性结构的框架下导数概念不易定义。作者希望寻找一种纯度量、无导数的等价刻画，以统一欧氏情形与度量空间情形。

Method: 引入(p,1-n/p)-compactly Hölder映射（紧Hölder类CH^{p,1-Q/p}）这一度量概念，在局部超临界情形p>n（或p>Q）下，将连续映射的Sobolev正则性与紧Hölder条件等价起来；并在完备Q-Ahlfors正则、支持p-Poincaré不等式的度量空间上，与Hajłasz空间M^{1,p}_loc及Newtonian空间N^{1,p}_loc进行比较。

Result: 证明了连续映射f:R^n→R^m属于W^{1,p}_loc当且仅当它是(p,1-n/p)-compactly Hölder映射（p>n）；更一般地，当X为完备Q-Ahlfors正则且支持p-Poincaré不等式（p>Q）、V为Banach空间时，CH^{p,1-Q/p}(X:V)恰为M^{1,p}_loc(X:V)与N^{1,p}_loc(X:V)的连续代表元，并建立了相应半范数之间的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该研究为超临界Sobolev空间提供了无导数的度量刻画，揭示了紧Hölder类与Hajłasz、Newtonian Sobolev空间连续代表元的一致性，并给出了半范数的定量对应关系，统一并推广了欧氏与度量空间的结果。

Abstract: In this paper we provide a purely metric and derivative-free characterization of continuous mappings in the local supercritical Sobolev space. More specifically, we show that a continuous mapping $f\colon\mathbb{R}^n\to\mathbb{R}^m$ lies in the local Sobolev space $W^{1,p}_\text{loc}(\mathbb{R}^n\colon\mathbb{R}^m)$ if and only if $f$ is a $(p,1-n/p)$-compactly Hölder mapping, where $p>n$. In fact, we prove the following more general result: whenever $X$ is a complete $Q$-Ahlfors regular metric space supporting a $p$-Poincaré inequality with $p>Q$, and $V$ is any Banach space, the compactly Hölder class $CH^{p,1-Q/p}(X\colon V)$ consists precisely of the continuous representatives of the local Hajłasz and Newtonian Sobolev spaces $M^{1,p}_\text{loc} (X \colon V)$ and $N^{1,p}_\text{loc}(X\colon V)$. We also establish quantitative comparisons between the corresponding seminorms.

</details>


### [94] [Lipschitz-free spaces are strongly unique preduals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24392)
*Ramón J. Aliaga,Marek Cúth,Felipe Vico*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意度量空间 $M$，Lipschitz-free 空间 $\mathcal{F}(M)$ 是 $\mathrm{Lip}_0(M)$ 的强唯一等距预对偶，解决了 Weaver 长期未决的问题，并给出了 Weaver 原证明中余维一继承命题的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weaver 曾提出一个长期未决的公开问题：Lipschitz-free 空间 $\mathcal{F}(M)$ 是否为 $\mathrm{Lip}_0(M)$ 的强唯一等距预对偶？此前该问题仅部分情形已知，本文旨在彻底解决这一公开问题。

Method: 分两步证明。首先针对长度度量空间，通过分析 $M$ 中路径上 Lipschitz 函数的行为，以及在某类 barrier-set 上取零的函数子空间的拓扑性质来完成证明；随后通过将一般度量空间嵌入到长度空间中，并选取合适的预对偶使得 Lipschitz 函数的限制算子关于这些预对偶是弱$^*$连续的，从而推广到一般度量空间。此外还构造反例检验 Weaver 原证明中使用的余维一继承断言。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{F}(M)$ 对任意度量空间 $M$ 都是 $\mathrm{Lip}_0(M)$ 的强唯一等距预对偶；同时给出了 Weaver 原始证明中关于有界且测地度量空间的余维一继承断言的反例，说明该断言不成立。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了 Weaver 的公开问题，并指出 Weaver 原证明路径中的一处缺陷（余维一继承断言），从而为 Lipschitz-free 空间的唯一预对偶性质提供了新的、更为严谨的论证框架。

Abstract: We prove that the Lipschitz-free space $\mathcal{F}(M)$ is the strongly unique isometric predual of $\mathrm{Lip}_0(M)$ for every metric space $M$, solving a longstanding open problem of Weaver. The result is proved first for length metric spaces by analyzing the behavior of Lipschitz functions on paths in $M$ and the topological properties of subspaces of functions vanishing on certain barrier-sets of $M$. Then, the result is extended to general metric spaces by embedding them into length spaces in such a way that the restriction operator for Lipschitz functions is weak$^*$ continuous with respect to their selected preduals. Finally, we also give a counterexample to the codimension-one inheritance assertion used in Weaver's original proof for bounded and geodesic metric spaces.

</details>


### [95] [Transfinite octahedrality in spaces of operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24414)
*Abraham Rueda Zoca*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了使 $L(Y,X)$ 对任意 Banach 空间 $Y$ 都成为超限（刚性）八面体空间的 Banach 空间 $X$ 的充要条件，给出若干实例，并首次构造出刚性八面体但非超限八面体的 Banach 空间，同时改进了超幂空间中八面体性的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八面体性是 Banach 空间几何理论中的重要性质，其超限版本（transfinite octahedrality）是较新的加强概念。已有文献缺乏对算子空间 $L(Y,X)$ 继承超限八面体性的系统刻画，也缺少区分刚性八面体性与超限八面体性的具体反例，同时超幂空间中的相关结论有待改进。

Method: 通过泛函分析与 Banach 空间几何方法，建立 $L(Y,X)$ 对任意 $Y$ 均为超限（刚性）八面体的充要条件，构造满足条件的 $X$ 实例，并借助该刻画构造反例、改进超幂空间结果。

Result: 给出了 $L(Y,X)$ 超限（刚性）八面体性的充要条件及相应实例；首次得到刚性八面体但不满足任何超限八面体版本的 Banach 空间；改进了超幂空间中八面体性的若干已有结果。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了算子空间超限八面体性的传递机制，厘清了刚性八面体性与超限八面体性之间的严格层次关系，并推进了超幂空间八面体性的研究。

Abstract: We study necessary and sufficient conditions on a Banach space $X$ such that $L(Y,X)$ is transfinite (rigid) octahedral for any Banach space $Y$. We present several examples of such $X$. As application of our results, we find the first example in the literature of Banach space which is rigid octahedral but fails any transfinite version of octahedrality. We also improve some previous results about octahedrality in ultrapower spaces.

</details>


### [96] [On absolutely Cesàro bounded operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24601)
*Arafat Abbar,Loris Arnold,Clément Coine*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究 $p$-绝对 Cesàro 有界算子，涵盖自提升、加权移位与线性动力学，给出正算子自提升定理、后向移位刻画、动力学障碍与生成性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对 Cesàro 有界性是 Cesàro 有界性的强化形式，与算子幂的增长速率和线性动力学性质密切相关，但尚缺乏系统的自提升结果、具体算子类（如加权移位）的刻画，以及动力学方面的限制性结论。

Method: 通过算子理论中的 Cesàro 平均工具，结合 $L^p(\Omega)$ 上正算子的序结构证明自提升；利用加权 $\ell^p$ 空间上后向移位的权序列刻画 $q$-绝对 Cesàro 有界性并构造例子；借助 Baire 纲原理与动力学概念（混沌、上频繁超循环）分析障碍并引入 Cesàro 比率有界性。

Result: 对 $1<p<\infty$，$L^p(\Omega)$ 上每个正的绝对 Cesàro 有界算子自动是 $p$-绝对 Cesàro 有界的；刻画了加权 $\ell^p$ 空间上后向移位的 $q$-绝对 Cesàro 有界性（$q\geq p$），并构造出幂增长速率多样的例子；证明非零 Banach 空间上强 Cesàro 有界算子不可能是混沌或上频繁超循环的，给出绝对与强 Cesàro 有界性的新障碍；引入并比较 Cesàro 比率有界性；由一般 Baire 纲原理得到 $p$-绝对 Cesàro 有界性失效的生成性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了 $p$-绝对 Cesàro 有界性在正算子与加权移位上的行为，揭示了其与强 Cesàro 有界性在动力学上的本质区别，并通过生成性结果表明该性质在一般意义下是脆弱的。

Abstract: We study $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operators, with particular emphasis on self-improvement, weighted shifts, and linear dynamics. Our first main result shows that, for $1<p<\infty$, every positive absolutely Cesàro bounded operator on $L^p(Ω)$ is automatically $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded. We then characterize $q$-absolute Cesàro boundedness of backward shifts on weighted $\ell^p$-spaces for $q\geq p$ and use this characterization to construct examples exhibiting a wide range of possible growth rates of the powers. In the dynamical direction, we obtain new obstructions to absolute and strong Cesàro boundedness; in particular, no strongly Cesàro bounded operator on a nonzero Banach space is chaotic or upper frequently hypercyclic. We also introduce Cesàro ratio-boundedness and compare it with absolute Cesàro boundedness. Finally, a general Baire-category principle yields a genericity theorem for the failure of $p$-absolute Cesàro boundedness.

</details>


### [97] [Compactness of Toeplitz Operators on the Bergman Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24614)
*Guangfu Cao,Li He,Shuqing Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究无权重Bergman空间A²(𝔻)上Toeplitz算子T_φ的紧性，用符号自身的局部平均（Bergman圆盘平均与Carleson盒平均）刻画紧性，并给出一个反例说明逐固定角度趋于零不足以保证紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Axler-Zheng用Berezin变换给出了紧性的充要条件，但对一般有界可测符号φ，其Berezin变换不易反映φ的内在性质；Zhu因此提出用符号本身刻画紧Toeplitz算子的猜想，本文即受此启发。

Method: 设φ∈L^∞(𝔻)，在无权重Bergman空间A²(𝔻)上研究T_φ。通过分析符号在Bergman圆盘上的局部平均以及Carleson盒平均来建立紧性判别准则。

Result: 证明T_φ紧当且仅当符号在任意固定半径的Bergman圆盘上的平均趋于零；并给出等价判据：Carleson盒平均在角度变量上一致趋于零。此外构造出一个非负有界符号，其Carleson盒平均在每个固定角度处趋于零，但对应Toeplitz算子不紧。

Conclusion: 该工作以符号局部平均的形式验证并推广了Zhu关于紧Toeplitz算子的猜想，同时通过反例表明角度逐点收敛弱于一致收敛，凸显一致性的必要性。

Abstract: Let \varphi\in L^{\infty}(\D). We study compactness criteria for \(T_\varphi\) on the Bergman space A^{2}(\D). Axler and Zheng~\cite{AZ1998} established a necessary and sufficient condition for compactness in terms of the Berezin transform. For a general bounded measurable function \varphi, however, its Berezin transform \tilde{\varphi} does not readily reveal the intrinsic properties of \varphi. Motivated by a characterization in terms of the symbol itself, Zhu~\cite{ZhuSlides} proposed a conjecture on compact Toeplitz operators. In this paper, we characterize compactness of $T_\varphi$ on the unweighted Bergman space in terms of local averages of the symbol. We prove that compactness is equivalent to the vanishing of averages over Bergman disks of any prescribed fixed radius. We also establish an equivalent criterion in terms of Carleson box averages that tend to zero uniformly in the angular variable. Finally, we construct a nonnegative bounded symbol whose Carleson box averages tend to zero at every fixed angle, although the associated Toeplitz operator is not compact.

</details>


### [98] [Rayleigh-Residual Flow I: Normal Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24901)
*Eric Shen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究复射影空间上平方Rayleigh残差泛函及其负梯度流的临界结构与动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画平方Rayleigh残差泛函的临界点、局部极小及梯度流动力学，用以理解有限时间谱探索与随机多项式滤波的改进机制。

Method: 分析任意复矩阵正残差临界点的切线方向；对正规矩阵用穿越谱点的圆描述临界集，并将振幅动力学化为秩至多四的复制子方程；计算临界分量附近的横向逃逸率；分析多项式重加权对不稳定坐标的影响；区分局部鞍点俘获与Haar分布初始态的集中，导出滤波系综密度。

Result: 证明正残差临界点存在严格下降切向，局部极小恰为特征线；正规矩阵临界集由谱点圆刻画，振幅满足秩≤4复制子方程并给出显式对数首次积分与内部轨道约化表示；获得横向逃逸率、重加权效应、鞍点俘获与Haar初始态的区分及滤波系综密度；数值实验展示收敛、非单调Rayleigh运动及低次随机多项式滤波对有限时间谱探索的改进。

Conclusion: 该工作将平方Rayleigh残差流的临界结构、复制子动力学与随机初始化/滤波行为统一起来，为谱探索与相关优化动力学提供理论刻画。

Abstract: We study the squared Rayleigh-residual functional on complex projective space and its negative gradient flow. For an arbitrary complex matrix, every positive-residual critical point has a strictly descending tangent direction; consequently, the local minima are precisely the eigenlines. For normal matrices, we describe the critical set in terms of circles through spectral points and identify the amplitude dynamics with a replicator equation whose payoff matrix has rank at most four. This gives explicit logarithmic first integrals and a reduced representation of interior trajectories. We compute transverse escape rates near critical components and analyze how polynomial reweighting changes unstable coordinates. Finally, we distinguish local saddle trapping from concentration of Haar-distributed initial states and derive the density of the filtered ensemble. Numerical examples with random spectra and a Jordan block illustrate convergence, nonmonotone Rayleigh motion, and the improvement of finite-time spectral exploration by low-degree random polynomial filters.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Superpolynomially Large Support in Every Irreducible Factor of Lacunary Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22286)
*Bhawesh Mishra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 存在无穷多个恰有 m 个非零系数的有理系数多项式 F，使得其每个不可约因子都有 exp(Ω(√(m/log m))) 个非零系数，从而给出稀疏因子化的无条件下、与次数无关的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数运算中的"反向稀疏性"（reverse-sparsity）现象：对于幂、复合等运算已有反向稀疏原理，但乘法是否具有类似性质尚不明确。作者希望刻画乘法与这些运算在稀疏性行为上的根本差异，并补充依赖系数高度、指数位置、互反结构或次数界等假设的正向因子稀疏性结果。

Method: 构造性方法：显式构造无穷多个有理系数多项式族 F，每个 F 恰好含有 m 个非零系数，并分析其不可约因子的系数稀疏性；通过下界估计证明每个不可约因子的非零系数个数至少为 exp(Ω(√(m/log m)))。

Result: 证明了存在无穷多个恰有 m 个非零系数的 F∈Q[x]，其所有不可约因子的非零系数个数均为 exp(Ω(√(m/log m)))，即因子稀疏性存在超多项式级别的下界。

Conclusion: 乘法在反向稀疏性上显著区别于幂、复合等运算：稀疏多项式的不可约因子可以极其稠密。该结果提供了无条件下、与次数无关的稀疏因子化障碍，与需要额外假设的正向因子稀疏性结果形成互补。

Abstract: We show that there exist infinitely many polynomials $F\in\mathbb{Q}[x]$ with exactly $m$ nonzero coefficients such that every irreducible factor of \(F\) over \(\mathbb{Q}\) has \(\exp\) \(\!\bigl(Ω(\sqrt{m/\log m})\bigr)\) nonzero coefficients. This places multiplication in a sharply different category from powers, composition, and other algebraic operations for which reverse-sparsity principles are known. It also gives an unconditional, degree-free obstruction to sparse factorization, complementing positive factor-sparsity results that impose hypotheses on coefficient height, exponent positions, reciprocal structure, or degree bounds.

</details>


### [100] [Iwasawa theory of class field towers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22297)
*Anwesh Ray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb{Z}_p$-扩张中层 $K_n$ 的最大非分歧 pro-$p$ 扩张的伽罗瓦群 $G_n$ 的增广滤链与 Zassenhaus 滤链的渐近行为，在 Iwasawa $\mu$-不变量为正的假设下给出了生成元数、关系数、增广商、Hilbert 级数等的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Iwasawa 理论中 $\mu$-不变量为正时，$K_n$ 的最大非分歧 pro-$p$ 扩张的伽罗瓦群 $G_n$ 规模随 $n$ 指数增长，其结构渐近尚缺系统性描述；本文旨在刻画 $G_n$ 的生成元、关系以及增广/Zassenhaus 滤链随 $n\to\infty$ 的渐近性质。

Method: 设 $p$ 为奇素数，$K_\infty/K$ 为 $\mathbb{Z}_p$-扩张，$G_n=\mathrm{Gal}(K_n^{\mathrm{ur},p}/K_n)$。假定经典 Iwasawa $\mu$-不变量为正，记 $d_n$ 为 $G_n$ 的最小生成元数，通过分析 $G_n$ 的增广滤链与 Zassenhaus 滤链，比较第 $m$ 次增广商与 $d_n$ 个生成元的自由 pro-$p$ 群的相应商，并研究 Hilbert 级数与增广滤链的指数增长率。

Result: 存在整数 $\delta>0$ 使得 $d_n=\delta p^n+O(1)$，而最小关系数为 $O(p^n)$；对每个固定 $m\ge2$，当 $n\to\infty$ 时第 $m$ 次增广商与 $d_n$ 个生成元的自由 pro-$p$ 群的相应增广商具有相同的主项渐近；同时得到 Hilbert 级数、增广滤链指数增长率以及 Zassenhaus 商的相应渐近。

Conclusion: 在 $\mu>0$ 情形下，$G_n$ 是“近乎自由”的 pro-$p$ 群：生成元数线性于 $p^n$ 增长，关系数仅为其 $O(1)$ 倍量级，且其增广与 Zassenhaus 滤链的渐近由自由 pro-$p$ 群主导，从而将 Iwasawa 理论中的 $\mu$-不变量与 pro-$p$ 群的滤链渐近联系起来。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $K_\infty/K$ be a $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension. For each finite layer $K_n$, let $G_n$ be the Galois group of its maximal unramified pro-$p$ extension. We study the augmentation and Zassenhaus filtrations of the groups $G_n$ as $n$ tends to infinity. Assume that the classical Iwasawa $μ$-invariant is positive. If $d_n$ denotes the minimal number of generators of $G_n$, then there is an integer $δ>0$ such that $d_n=δp^n+O(1)$, while the minimal number of relations is $O(p^n)$. For each fixed $m\geq2$, as $n\to\infty$, we show that the $m$-th augmentation quotient has the same leading asymptotic as that of a free pro-$p$ group on $d_n$ generators. We also obtain corresponding asymptotics for the Hilbert series, the exponential growth rate of the augmentation filtration, and the Zassenhaus quotients.

</details>


### [101] [A quadratic-residue dichotomy for two partition functions modulo 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22340)
*Jiyuan Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了两个受限5模分拆函数 \(f_{0,1,4}(n)\) 与 \(f_{0,2,3}(n)\) 在模3下的一类 Ramanujan 型同余，并给出自相似性推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模5剩余类上每部分至多用两次的分拆函数，旨在建立模3下的 Ramanujan 型同余与自相似同余族，并揭示两种函数在素数模意义下何时保持、何时互换（由5是否为模p二次剩余决定）。

Method: 将生成函数模3化简为某 Rogers-Ramanujan 型 theta 函数的平方，利用 \((1-x)^{\ell}\equiv 1-x^{\ell}\pmod{\ell}\) 与 Jacobi 三重积；再借助 \(-1\) 是模 \(p\equiv 3\pmod 4\) 的二次非剩余，对所得二元二次型进行 dissection。显式常数 \(a(p),b(p)\) 由 \(20a(p)\equiv -9\)、\(20b(p)\equiv -1\pmod{p^2}\) 确定。

Result: 对每个素数 \(p\equiv 3\pmod 4\)，有
\[
f_{0,1,4}(p^2m+a(p))\equiv f_{0,1,4}(m)\quad\text{或}\quad f_{0,2,3}(m)\pmod 3,
\]
\[
f_{0,2,3}(p^2m+b(p))\equiv f_{0,2,3}(m)\quad\text{或}\quad f_{0,1,4}(m)\pmod 3,
\]
具体保持或互换由 \(p\equiv \pm1\pmod 5\) 或 \(p\equiv \pm2\pmod 5\) 决定，即等价于5是否为模 \(p\) 的二次剩余。最小例子为
\[
f_{0,1,4}(9m)\equiv f_{0,2,3}(m),\qquad
f_{0,2,3}(9m+4)\equiv f_{0,1,4}(m)\pmod 3.
\]


Conclusion: 该工作统一刻画了两类受限5模分拆函数模3下的同余关系，说明其在素数模下保持或互换完全由二次剩余性质决定；并推出模 \(p\) 进一阶同余（带例外）以及模 \(p^{dn}\)（\(d=2,4\)）的自相似同余族，常数分别为 \(9(p^{dn}-1)/20\) 与 \((p^{dn}-1)/20\)。

Abstract: Let $f_{0,1,4}(n)$ denote the number of partitions of $n$ into parts congruent to 0, 1 or 4 modulo 5 with each part used at most twice, and let $f_{0,2,3}(n)$ be defined analogously for parts congruent to 0, 2 or 3 modulo 5. We prove that for every prime $p \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ there are explicit non-negative integers $a(p)$ and $b(p)$, determined by $20a(p) \equiv -9$ and $20b(p) \equiv -1 \pmod{p^2}$, such that $f_{0,1,4}(p^2m + a(p))$ and $f_{0,2,3}(p^2m + b(p))$ are congruent modulo 3 to $f_{0,1,4}(m)$ and $f_{0,2,3}(m)$ when $p \equiv \pm 1 \pmod 5$, and to $f_{0,2,3}(m)$ and $f_{0,1,4}(m)$ when $p \equiv \pm 2 \pmod 5$. By quadratic reciprocity the two functions are preserved exactly when 5 is a quadratic residue modulo $p$, and interchanged otherwise. The smallest cases are $f_{0,1,4}(9m) \equiv f_{0,2,3}(m)$ and $f_{0,2,3}(9m+4) \equiv f_{0,1,4}(m)$. The proof reduces each generating function modulo 3 to the square of a Rogers-Ramanujan-type theta function by means of $(1-x)^{\ell} \equiv 1-x^{\ell} \pmod{\ell}$ and the Jacobi triple product, and then dissects the resulting binary quadratic form using the fact that $-1$ is a quadratic non-residue modulo $p$. As corollaries we obtain, for each such $p$, a congruence with exceptions on a progression of modulus $p$, and an infinite family of self-similarity congruences on progressions of modulus $p^{dn}$ with $d = 2$ or $d = 4$ and constants $9(p^{dn}-1)/20$ and $(p^{dn}-1)/20$.

</details>


### [102] [On periods of the Tribonacci sequence modulo primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22364)
*Liam Baker,Florian Luca,Kerry Porrill*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Tribonacci序列的$p$-进零点，补全了Bilu 2024年工作中$p=11$的情形，并在广义$abc$猜想下证明了存在无穷多个素数$p$使得某相邻两项首次被$p$整除时不同时被$p^2$整除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tribonacci序列的$p$-进零点分类此前由Yuri Bilu于2024年开展，但$p=11$的情形尚未完成；此外，关于素数整除相邻两项并涉及整除重数（$p$与$p^2$）的分布问题也值得深入探讨。

Method: 针对$p=11$对Tribonacci序列$T_n$的$p$-进零点进行具体分析以补全分类；对于第二个问题，研究素数$p$，考察序列中首次同时被$p$整除的两个相邻值$T_n$与$T_{n-1}$，并借助广义$abc$猜想进行推导。

Result: 完成了Bilu 2024工作中$p=11$情形下的$p$-进零点分析，补全了该分类；在广义$abc$猜想假设下，证明了存在无穷多个素数$p$，使得首次被$p$整除的相邻两项$T_n$与$T_{n-1}$不同时被$p^2$整除。

Conclusion: Tribonacci序列的$p$-进零点研究在$p=11$处得到完善；同时，在广义$abc$猜想下得到关于相邻项整除重数性质的无穷性结果，表明满足更严格整除条件的素数只有有限多个这一现象并非普遍。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the $p$-adic zeros of the Tribonacci sequence $T_n$, for a prime $p$. In particular, we complete the analysis that was done in a 2024 paper of Yuri Bilu by investigating the case of $p=11$. In addition, we investigate the primes $p$ for which at the first two values $T_n$ and $T_{n-1}$ which are both divisible by $p$, both of these are actually divisible by $p^2$, proving under the assumption of a generalisation of the $abc$ conjecture that there are infinitely many $p$ which are not such.

</details>


### [103] [Monogenic Fields of Cryptographic Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22374)
*Swechchha Adhikari,Daphne Plott,Parker Torgersen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究回文变换 F(x)=x^n f(x+x^{-1}+c)，给出判别式公式、不可约性条件、单项生成性判据，并通过迭代构造高次单项生成数域（如次数512）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对给定整数多项式 f 与整数 c，构造次数翻倍的回文型多项式 F，并刻画其算术性质（判别式、不可约性、单项生成性），为生成高次单项生成数域提供系统方法。

Method: 定义回文变换 F(x)=x^n f(x+x^{-1}+c)；推导判别式公式 disc(F)=f(c+2)f(c-2)disc(f)^2；给出 F 不可约的充分条件；证明当 f 单项生成且 f(c+2)f(c-2) 无平方因子时 F 单项生成，并给出对应的非单项生成判据；迭代该变换。

Result: 得到判别式公式、不可约性充分条件与单项生成判据及匹配的非单项生成判据；迭代变换可由固定基多项式生成次数为 2^k n 的单项生成数域。显式构造了由 x^4+x+1 得到次数 512 的单项生成域。

Conclusion: 该回文变换为从低次基多项式迭代构造高次单项生成数域提供了有效工具，兼具判别式、不可约性与单项生成性的完整刻画。

Abstract: For a monic irreducible $f \in \mathbb{Z}[x]$ of degree $n$ and an integer $c$, we study the palindromic transform $F(x) = x^n f(x + x^{-1} + c)$, which produces a polynomial of degree $2n$. We give a discriminant formula $\operatorname{disc}(F) = f(c+2)f(c-2)\operatorname{disc}(f)^2$, sufficient conditions for the irreducibility of $F$, and a criterion showing that $F$ is monogenic when $f$ is monogenic and $f(c+2)f(c-2)$ is squarefree, together with a matching non-monogenicity criterion. Iterating the transform yields monogenic number fields of degree $2^k n$ from a fixed base polynomial. As an explicit example, we construct a monogenic field of degree 512 from $x^4 + x + 1$.

</details>


### [104] [Bad Reduction of Genus Two Curves with complex multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22544)
*Jan Hendrik Bruinier,Tonghai Yang,Peng Yu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究Jacobian具有双二次CM域乘法的亏格2曲线，给出其稳定坏约化素数上界，并证明此类素数必存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亏格2曲线的Jacobian在双二次CM域作用下可分解为同一虚二次域中两个具CM的椭圆曲线之积，需刻画其稳定坏约化素数，并建立与CM域判别式的关系。

Method: 通过signature (3,2)正交Shimura簇上的小CM循环显式描述具双二次CM的主极化阿贝尔曲面；证明该CM循环与Humbert曲面的算术相交公式，用非协调Eisenstein级数系数及三元正定二次型表示数表出；再利用高阶Green函数的算术性质。

Result: 得到稳定坏约化素数由CM序判别式给出的上界，并经高阶Green函数算术性质证明稳定坏约化素数总存在。

Conclusion: 双二次CM的亏格2曲线其稳定坏约化素数受CM序判别式控制，且此类坏约化素数必有出现。

Abstract: We study genus two curves whose Jacobians have complex multiplication by a biquadratic CM field. In this setting the Jacobian decomposes as a product of two elliptic curves with complex multiplication by orders in the same imaginary quadratic field. We derive upper bounds for the primes of stable bad reduction of such curves in terms of the discriminants of the CM orders. To do so, we give an explicit description of principally polarized abelian surfaces with CM by a biquadratic field via small CM cycles on an orthogonal Shimura variety of signature (3,2). We use it to prove an arithmetic intersection formula between such CM cycles with Humbert surfaces in terms of coefficients of incoherent Eisenstein series and representation numbers of ternary positive definite quadratic forms. Finally, employing the arithmetic properties of higher Green functions, we show that primes of stable bad reduction always exist.

</details>


### [105] [Almost Trivial Units in Group Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22560)
*Efrat Shani,Anupam Srivastav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了所有使群环 RG 具有几乎平凡单位的整数环 R 与有限群 G，并确定了此类群环局部自由类群中的 Swan 子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群环的单位群结构是代数数论与代数 K 理论的核心问题之一。"几乎平凡单位"（almost trivial units）刻画了单位群中除平凡单位外可能存在的、由环与群结构共同诱导的特殊单位，因此分类具有该性质的群环有助于理解单位群的整体结构。

Method: 对整数环 R 与有限群 G 进行联合分类，通过分析群环 RG 的单位群，确定其满足"几乎平凡单位"条件的充要情形；随后利用类群与 K 理论工具（如局部自由类群与 Swan 子群的定义）计算相应 Swan 子群。

Result: 完整给出了所有使 RG 具有几乎平凡单位的整数环 R 与有限群 G 的分类，并对这些群环确定了其局部自由类群中的 Swan 子群。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了几乎平凡单位群环的完整分类及其 Swan 子群的明确描述，为群环单位群与类群理论提供了新的结构性结果。

Abstract: We find all ring of integers, R, and finite groups G, such that RG has almost trivial units. Moreover, for such a group ring we determine the Swan subgroup of its locally free classgroup

</details>


### [106] [Zero density theorems for short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22624)
*Andrew Fiori*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文给出了黎曼ζ函数在临界带边缘附近短区间内零点个数的显式界，证明即使在短区间内也有正比例的零点不靠近临界带边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究ζ函数零点在短区间及临界带边缘附近的分布，量化零点远离临界带边缘的比例。

Method: 对ζ函数在临界带边缘附近短区间内的零点个数给出显式上界估计。

Result: 当h>100、t>10^100时，虚部落在[t-h,t+h]且实部落在[1/16,15/16]的零点比例至少为13%；当t>10^43时即可得到正比例。

Conclusion: 即使在短区间内，ζ函数也有正比例的零点不靠近临界带边缘，且给出了显式的数值界限。

Abstract: We provide explicit bounds on the number of zeros for $ζ$ in short intervals near the edge of the critical strip. Ultimately we show that even in short intervals a positive proportion of zeros will not be near the edge of the critical strip. For example with $h>100$ and $t>10^{100}$ the proportion of zeros of $ζ$ with imaginary part in $[t-h,t+h]$ which have real part in $[\frac{1}{16},\frac{15}{16}]$ is at least $13\%$ and a positive proportion is already obtained at $t>10^{43}$.

</details>


### [107] [Maximal Unramified $p$-Extensions with Prescribed Galois Groups: A Quantitative Refinement of Ozaki's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22766)
*Kwang-Seob Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究将有限 $p$-群 $G$ 实现为某数域的 $p$-类塔群所需的最小基域次数 $\tau_p(G)$。作者利用 $G$ 的 Frattini 结构，对奇素数 $p$ 和 $|G|=p^n$ 证明了 $\tau_p(G)\leq p^{\ell_\Phi(G)+\lceil\log_p(\binom{n+2}{2}+1)\rceil}$，将 Hajir–Maire–Ramakrishna 的 $|G|$ 阶界改进为对有界 Frattini 长度群成立的 $O_p(n^2)$ 二次界，并对初等交换群 $E_m$ 给出 $\tau_p(E_m)\asymp_p m^2$ 的精确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ozaki 定理保证每个有限 $p$-群都可作为某数域最大非分歧 $p$-扩张的 Galois 群，Hajir、Maire 与 Ramakrishna 将其有效化，但所得基域次数为 $|G|$ 阶，随群阶指数增长。本文旨在通过纳入 $G$ 的 Frattini 结构来降低该次数界，从而对具有有界 Frattini 长度的群获得更优的复杂度。

Method: 对奇素数 $p$ 与阶为 $p^n$ 的有限 $p$-群 $G$，作者引入并利用迭代 Frattini 长度 $\ell_\Phi(G)$，结合 Frattini 结构分析，推导 $\tau_p(G)$ 的上界；其中 $\tau_p(G)$ 表示使 $G$ 成为某数域 $p$-类塔群的最小基域次数。并对初等交换 $p$-群 $E_m=(\Z/p\Z)^m$ 进行专门分析以获得匹配的上下界。

Result: 证明了 $\tau_p(G) \leq p^{\ell_\Phi(G)+\lceil\log_p(\binom{n+2}{2}+1)\rceil}$。因此对 Frattini 长度有界的群，原先 $p^n$ 的阶尺度界被替换为二次界 $O_p(n^2)$。对 $E_m=(\Z/p\Z)^m$，进一步证明精确估计 $\tau_p(E_m)\asymp_p m^2$。

Conclusion: 通过考虑 $G$ 的 Frattini 结构，本文实质性地改进了有限 $p$-群作为 $p$-类塔群实现时的基域次数上界；对有界 Frattini 长度群给出二次增长界，并在初等交换情形验证了该二次尺度是紧的。

Abstract: Ozaki proved that every finite $p$-group occurs as the Galois group of a maximal unramified $p$-extension of a number field. Hajir, Maire and Ramakrishna made this theorem effective, obtaining a base-field degree of order $|G|$. In this article, we reduce that degree by taking the Frattini structure of $G$ into account. More precisely, for an odd prime $p$ and a finite $p$-group $G$ of order $p^n$, we prove \[
  τ_p(G)
  \leq
  p^{\ell_Φ(G)+
  \left\lceil\log_p\left(\binom{n+2}{2}+1\right)\right\rceil}. \] Here $τ_p(G)$ is the least degree of a number field realizing $G$ as its $p$-class tower group, and $\ell_Φ(G)$ is the iterated Frattini length of $G$. Thus, for groups of bounded Frattini length, the order-scale bound $p^n$ is replaced by the quadratic bound $O_p(n^2)$. For $E_m=(\Z/p\Z)^m$, we further prove the sharp estimate $τ_p(E_m)\asymp_p m^2$.

</details>


### [108] [On a Turán's theorem for small primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22777)
*T. Makoto Minamide,Haruka Sakai,Yoshio Tanigawa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 Granville–Soundararajan (2007) 计算高阶矩的方法推广到截断素因子计数函数 ω_z(n)，在 k 奇数（k≥3）、z 与 x 的特定限制下给出 ∑_{n≤x}(ω_z(n)−log log z)^k 的渐近公式，并梳理了该方法的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ω(n) 的高阶矩已有 Granville–Soundararajan (2007) 的新方法，但该方法尚未被用于 ω_z(n) 这类只统计不超过 z 的不同素因子个数的函数；对奇数次矩（k≥3）的渐近行为仍缺乏结果，因此有必要推广并检验该方法的适用范围。

Method: 沿用 Granville–Soundararajan 计算 ∑_{n≤x}(ω(n)−log log x)^k 的方法，将其应用于 ω_z(n)；在 k 为奇数（k≥3）、并对 k、z、x 施加一定限制的条件下，推导 ∑_{n≤x}(ω_z(n)−log log z)^k 的渐近公式；同时总结该方法的总体框架。

Result: 对奇数 k≥3，在关于 k、z、x 的特定限制下，得到 ∑_{n≤x}(ω_z(n)−log log z)^k 的渐近公式；并给出了该方法的一个框架性描述。

Conclusion: Granville–Soundararajan 的高阶矩方法可以推广到截断素因子函数 ω_z(n)，并可处理奇数次矩情形；作者还提示该方法的框架具有进一步推广的潜力。

Abstract: Denote by $ω(n)$ the number of distinct prime divisors of the natural number $n$. In 2007, Granville and Soundararajan gave a quite new method to compute the higher moments $\sum_{n\leq x}(ω(n)-\log\log x)^{k}$, for a wide range of integers $k\geq 2$. In this notes, we shall apply the method for $ω_{z}(n)$ which denotes the number of distinct prime divisors $\leq z$ of $n$. Especially, for odd integers $k\geq 3$, we lead asymptotic formulas for $\sum_{n\leq x}(ω_{z}(n)-\log\log z)^{k}$, under certain restrictions on $k$, $z$, and $x$. Also, we refer to a framework of the approach.

</details>


### [109] [On a Turán's theorem for arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22784)
*T. Makoto Minamide,Haruka Sakai,Yoshio Tanigawa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究素数在算术级数中分布的离散矩：对满足 (a,m)=1 的整数 a 与参数 z，考察 ω_z(n;m,a) 相对期望值 (1/φ(m))log log z 的 k 阶中心矩的渐近行为，并给出对应的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数论中 ω(n) 的分布（如 Erdős–Kac 定理、Kubilius 不等式）是核心问题。将其限制到算术级数 p≡a (mod m) 上并按截断参数 z 控制素数大小，可细刻画素数在级数中的分布对整数因子结构的累积影响。已有工作多关注一阶或方差情形，而对任意正整数 k≥2 的高阶中心矩缺乏系统渐近结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 沿用 Granville 与 Soundararajan 研究 ω(n) 高阶矩的方法：以一阶中心矩的幂展开为出发点，利用 k 阶矩与 k-1 阶矩之间的递推关系式；结合算术级数中素数的分布（含必要的截断处理）与矩估计；对 ω_z(n;m,a) 与 ω(n;m,a) 两种设定分别进行渐近分析，得到对应的渐近主项。

Result: 对 Σ_{n≤x}(ω_z(n;m,a) − (1/φ(m))log log z)^k 在 x→∞ 时给出渐近公式，适用于较广范围的正整数 k≥2；同时对 Σ_{n≤x}(ω(n;m,a) − (1/φ(m))log log x)^k（其中 ω(n;m,a) 计数满足 p≡a (mod m) 的不同素因子个数）亦给出相应的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Granville–Soundararajan 关于 ω(n) 高阶中心矩的方法推广至算术级数限制的素因子计数函数，明确刻画了其 k 阶矩的渐近主项，从而更精细地揭示了素数在给定同余类中的分布如何影响整数的素因子结构。

Abstract: Let $m\geq 1$ be a fixed integer, $a$ an integer satisfying $(a,m)=1$, and $z\geq 1$ a real parameter. Denote by $ω_{z}(n;m,a)$ the number of distinct prime divisors $p$ of $n$ satisfying $p\equiv a\, (m)$ and $p\leq z$. We study an asymptotic behaviour of $\sum_{n\leq x}\left(ω_{z}(n;m,a)-\frac{1}{\varphi(m)}\log\log z\right)^{k}$ as $x\to\infty$ for a wide range of positive integer $k\geq 2$, where $\varphi(\cdot)$ is the Euler function. Following a method of Granville and Soundararajan we lead an asymptotic formula for the above. Also, we investigate $\sum_{n\leq x}\left(ω(n;m,a)-\frac{1}{\varphi(m)}\log\log x\right)^{k}$, where $ω(n;m,a)$ denotes the number of distinct prime divisors $p$ of $n$ such that $p\equiv a\, (m)$.

</details>


### [110] [Romanoff type theorems for polynomials over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22875)
*Enci Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究有限域上多项式 $f=h+g^k$ 的表示个数，证明部分 $f$ 至少有 $c\log n$ 个表示，并在 $\delta\rho_g<1$ 时正比例多项式至少有两种表示；对幂和形式给出正比例可表示条件与不可表示剩余类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Erdős 关于整数表示为素数加幂和的经典问题推广到有限域多项式环，研究多项式 $f$ 写成单项不可约多项式 $h$ 加上 $g$ 的幂的表示个数与正比例可表示性。

Method: 设 $g$ 为次数 $\delta>0$ 的有限域多项式，考察次数 $n$ 的首一多项式 $f=h+g^k$，其中 $h$ 为次数 $n$ 的首一不可约多项式且 $\delta k<n$，利用 $\rho_g=\varphi_q(g)/|g|$ 等量进行计数估计；并推广到幂和 $g^{\lfloor k_i^{r_i}\rfloor}$ 的多项式表示。

Result: 部分 $f$ 至少存在 $c\log n$ 个这样的表示；当 $\delta\rho_g<1$ 时，正比例的次数 $n$ 多项式至少有两次表示。对幂和 $g^{\lfloor k_i^{r_i}\rfloor}$，只要 $\sum_{i=1}^{t}r_i^{-1}\ge1$，就有正比例多项式可表示；同时某些模不可约多项式的剩余类不含可表示多项式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出有限域多项式环中 Erdős 型表示的定量下界与正比例可表示性，并揭示幂和指数条件及模不可约多项式剩余类对可表示性的限制。

Abstract: Given a polynomial \(g\) of degree \(δ>0\) over a finite field, we study monic polynomials \(f\) of degree \(n\) that can be written as \(f=h+g^k\), where \(h\) is a monic irreducible polynomial of degree \(n\) and \(k\in\mathbb{N}\) with \(δk<n\). Following Erdős, we show that some \(f\) admit at least \(c\log n\) such representations. If \(δρ_g<1\), where \(ρ_g=\varphi_q(g)/|g|\), then a positive proportion of degree-\(n\) polynomials admits at least two such representations. More generally, for sums of powers \(g^{\lfloor k_i^{r_i}\rfloor}\), we prove that a positive proportion of polynomials of degree \(n\) is representable whenever \(\sum_{i=1}^{t}r_i^{-1}\ge 1\), while certain residue classes modulo some irreducible polynomial contain no representable polynomial.

</details>


### [111] [Steinness of the Basic $\mathrm{GL}_n$ Local Shimura Tower in Odd Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22921)
*Jiawei Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明 $\mathrm{GL}_n$ 在 $\mu=(1,1,0^{n-2})$、基本 Newton 斜率 $2/n$ 下的基本局部 Shimura 塔在 $\breve{\mathbf{Q}}_p$ 上具有 Stein 性，覆盖所有奇数 $n\ge3$、所有有限层与所有素数 $p$（含 $p=2$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本局部 Shimura 塔的几何性质（如 Stein 性）是 $p$ 进局部几何与 Langlands 纲领中的核心问题，此前结果多受限于特定 $n$、斜率或排除 $p=2$。本文针对 $\mathrm{GL}_n$、$\mu=(1,1,0^{n-2})$、基本 Newton 斜率 $2/n$ 的情形，试图在最大一般性（任意奇数 $n\ge3$、任意素数和任意有限层）下建立 Stein 性。

Method: 从晶体周期映射构造整体解析函数；通过 Tate 格的完美胚归一化证明其次水平集的紧性；进而证明所得函数映射到仿射空间是有限态射。关键几何输入包括：万有覆盖的 Fargues–Fontaine 实现、秩二轨迹上的归一化行列式，以及 $W(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ 上的整 PEL 实现，用以产生有界的整体 Hodge 生成元。整性构造亦涵盖 $p=2$。

Result: 证明了对 $G=\mathrm{GL}_n$、$\mu=(1,1,0^{n-2})$、基本 Newton 斜率 $2/n$，每个奇数 $n\ge3$、每个有限层、每个素数 $p$，基本局部 Shimura 塔在 $\breve{\mathbf{Q}}_p$ 上均为 Stein。

Conclusion: 基本局部 Shimura 塔在 $\breve{\mathbf{Q}}_p$ 上的 Stein 性得到全一般性证明；证明所依赖的整体解析函数、完美胚 Tate 格归一化与整 PEL 实现等工具可推广至更广的局部 Shimura 簇几何研究。

Abstract: We prove Steinness over $\breve{\mathbf{Q}}_p$ for the basic local Shimura tower attached to $G=\mathrm{GL}_n$, $μ=(1,1,0^{n-2})$, with basic Newton slope $2/n$, for every odd $n\ge3$, at every finite level and for every prime $p$. The proof proceeds by constructing global analytic functions from the crystalline period map, proving compactness of their sublevel sets via a perfectoid normalization of Tate lattices, and showing that the resulting function map to affine space is finite.
