The key geometric inputs are the Fargues--Fontaine realization of the universal cover, a normalized determinant on the rank-two locus, and an integral PEL realization over $W(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ used to produce bounded global Hodge generators. The integral constructions include the prime $p=2$.

</details>


### [112] [On $\mathbb{Z}_2$-extensions of real quadratic fields with class group of $2$-rank three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23063)
*Yuito Saito*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究实二次域 $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{p_1p_2p_3p_4})$ 的割圆 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-扩张上的非分歧 Iwasawa 模，给出其有限性判据，并构造了首个无穷族，其理想类群 $2$-秩为三且满足 Greenberg 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实二次域割圆 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-扩张上非分歧 Iwasawa 模的有限性（即 Greenberg 猜想）是 Iwasawa 理论的核心问题之一。已有例子多集中于 $2$-秩较小的情形，而对理想类群 $2$-秩为三、且扩张完全分歧的实二次域无穷族尚缺乏构造，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对 $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{p_1p_2p_3p_4})$（$p_i$ 为互异奇素数）的割圆 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-扩张，建立非分歧 Iwasawa 模有限性的判据；该判据依赖于某个具有特定指定商群的 $2$-群的不存在性，并据此构造无穷族。

Result: 利用所得判据构造了一个无穷族实二次域，其非分歧 Iwasawa 模同构于 $\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$，$2$-类群同构于 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$。这是首个理想类群 $2$-秩为三、割圆 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-扩张完全分歧且满足 Greenberg 猜想的实二次域无穷族。

Conclusion: 本文给出了通过 $2$-群不存在性判定非分歧 Iwasawa 模有限性的新判据，并首次构造出 $2$-秩为三且满足 Greenberg 猜想的实二次域无穷族，为相关猜想的验证提供了新工具与实例。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the unramified Iwasawa module over the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-extension of the real quadratic field $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{p_1p_2p_3p_4})$, where $p_1, p_2, p_3$, and $p_4$ are distinct odd prime numbers. We give a criterion for the finiteness of an unramified Iwasawa module of a number field. The criterion is based on the nonexistence of a $2$-group with a certain prescribed quotient. Using this criterion, we construct an infinite family of such real quadratic fields with unramified Iwasawa module of type $\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ and $2$-class group of type $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z} \oplus \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$. This gives the first example of an infinite family of real quadratic fields whose ideal class group has $2$-rank three and whose cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-extension is totally ramified and satisfies Greenberg's conjecture.

</details>


### [113] [Counting number fields with symplectic Galois group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23093)
*Anwesh Ray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与辛群GSp_{2n}(F_ℓ)或PGSp_{2n}(F_ℓ)相关的数域计数问题，证明此类数域（判别式≤X）的数量下界正比于X^{1/(2n·ind(π(τ)))}，其中τ为辛平延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Malle猜想（弱形式）在辛群情形下的计数下界。理解具有给定Galois群和置换表示的数域分布是代数数论的核心问题，而Malle猜想给出了此类数域计数的渐近预测。作者希望为辛群情形提供非平凡下界。

Method: 从一族超椭圆曲线的Jacobian上附着的模ℓ Galois表示构造数域；证明该族具有大的辛单值群（large symplectic monodromy），并结合无平方因子筛法（squarefree sieve）证明计数下界。

Result: 得到判别式≤X的此类数域数目下界为常数倍的X^{1/(2n·ind(π(τ)))}。对于自然向量作用和射影作用，所得指数在ℓ→∞时渐近为Malle猜想弱形式预测指数的1/(4n)。

Conclusion: 为辛群GSp_{2n}/PGSp_{2n}情形下的Malle猜想提供了非平凡下界，并给出与猜想的比较，揭示指数差距为1/(4n)因子。

Abstract: Let $n\geq 1$ and let $\ell$ be an odd prime. Let $G=\mathrm{PGSp}_{2n}(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$ or $\mathrm{GSp}_{2n}(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$, and fix a faithful transitive permutation representation $π:G\longrightarrow S_d$. We study degree-$d$ number fields whose Galois closures have Galois group $G$ and whose associated permutation representation is $π$. For $σ\in S_d$, write $\operatorname{ind}(σ)$ for its permutation index, namely $\operatorname{ind}(σ) = d-\#\{\text{orbits of $σ$ on $\{1,\ldots,d\}$}\}$. If $τ$ denotes a symplectic transvection, or its image in the projective symplectic group, we prove that the number of such fields with absolute discriminant at most $X$ is bounded below by a constant multiple of $X^{1/(2n\,\operatorname{ind}(π(τ)))}$. For the natural vector and projective actions, the exponents we obtain are asymptotically $1/(4n)$ of those predicted by the weak form of Malle's conjecture as $\ell\to\infty$. The fields are constructed from the mod-$\ell$ Galois representations attached to the Jacobians of a one-parameter family of hyperelliptic curves. The proof combines large symplectic monodromy for this family with a squarefree sieve.

</details>


### [114] [The variance of the number of lattice points in narrow sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23214)
*Stephen Lester,Ezra Waxman,Nadav Yesha*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究平滑窄扇区内格点数的方差（对扇区位置取平均），依扇区开角范围分为三种情形，分别得到方差的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点计数问题中的分布与波动性质，特别是平滑窄扇形区域内格点数随扇区位置变化的方差，尚缺乏系统的渐近刻画。

Method: 对平滑窄扇区内的格点数，对其位置进行平均，按扇区开角的不同取值范围划分三种情形，分别推导方差的渐近公式。

Result: 在三种开角情形下均获得了方差的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 平滑窄扇区格点数的方差随开角呈现三种不同机制，各机制均有明确的渐近行为。

Abstract: We study the variance of the number of lattice points in smoothed narrow sectors, where the average is taken over the position of the sectors. There are three regimes corresponding to specific ranges for the open angle of the sectors and in each we obtain an asymptotic formula for the variance.

</details>


### [115] [Explicit bounds for the prime number theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23222)
*Andrew Fiori,Mikko Jaskari*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究L函数零点求和紧显式界，并应用于黎曼zeta函数与素数定理，获得素数定理误差项的新最佳估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求在1-线附近L函数零点求和的紧显式界，并将技术应用于素数定理误差项的改进。

Method: 结合多种无零点区域与零点密度估计，对L函数零点求和进行显式界分析。

Result: 对x≥21，证明|ψ(x)-x| < 0.239 x exp(-0.1982767(1+logloglog(x)/(15loglog(x))) log(x)^{3/5}/loglog(x)^{1/5})。

Conclusion: 该工作给出素数定理误差项的新最佳显式估计，展示了零点求和显式界技术的有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding tight explicit bounds on sums over zeros of $L$-functions near the $1$-line with a variety of zero-free regions and zero-density estimates. As an application to demonstrate the techniques, we apply them to the case of the Riemann zeta function and the prime number theorem to obtain new best estimates on the error terms in the prime number theorem. We show that for $x\geq21$ we have $|ψ(x)-x| < 0.239 x \exp\left(-0.1982767\left(1+\frac{\log\log\log(x)}{15\log\log(x)}\right) \frac{\log(x)^{3/5}}{\log\log(x)^{1/5}}\right).$

</details>


### [116] [Sharp zero estimates for trajectories of polynomial vector fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24148)
*Gal Binyamini,Yuval Salant*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文将Nesterenko关于代数ODE解上多项式零点阶的局部最优估计推广到整体（算术）情形，用数域K和 logarithmic height 替代 C(x) 和 deg_x，在整体D-性质假设下证明了固定紧集内带重数零点数的最优上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超越数论应用中，常需用 deg_x P 和 deg_f P 控制 P(x,f) 在给定点的零点阶，Nesterenko 在 D-性质下已给出本质最优的局部界。本文旨在建立该结果的整体形式，将其推广到数域与高度框架，以满足算术点计数等应用的需要。

Method: 将底域 C(x) 替换为数域 K，将 deg_x P 替换为对数高度 h(P)；引入与局部 D-性质相对应的整体 D-性质假设；在此基础上证明固定紧集内零点（计重数）数目的上界。

Result: 在整体 D-性质下，获得了固定紧集中零点计数（带重数）的本质最优上界，推广了 Nesterenko 的局部最优估计。

Conclusion: 该整体零点界可用于点计数定理，相关应用在与 Hirata-Kohno 和 Kawashima 合作的另一篇论文中展开。

Abstract: If $f$ is a tuple of functions satisfying an algebraic ODE and $P\in{\mathbb C}(x)[f]$, it is common in applications to transcendental number theory to consider upper bounds for the order of zero of $P(x,f)$ at a given point in terms of $\operatorname{deg}_x P,\operatorname{deg}_f P$. Nesterenko introduced a condition known as the D-property, which holds in many applications, and proved essentially optimal bounds under this condition.
  We prove a global form of this result, where the field $\mathbb C(x)$ is replaced by a number field $K$ and $\operatorname{deg}_x P$ is replaced by the logarithmic height $\operatorname{h}(P)$. Under an analogous global D-property, we prove essentially optimal bounds for the number of zeroes, counted with multiplicities, in a fixed compact set. Applications of this result to point-counting theorems are developed in a separate joint paper with Hirata-Kohno and Kawashima.

</details>


### [117] [Wave Numbers: Discrete Sequence Algebras, Sieve Projectors, and Dynamical Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23233)
*Terence R. Smith*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文对离散空间格 $\mathbb{Z}$ 上本原平面波序列生成的波闭合空间给出代数、几何与物理的统一分类：证明线性波群同构于 $(\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z})\times(\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z})$，并以整数多项式、群代数、四元数及双四元数结构分别导出相位叠加定理、投影/陷波滤波、SU(2)自旋转子与 Minkowski 时空及 Lorentz 群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散格上平面波存在相位简并，缺乏对其闭合结构的系统代数刻画，也缺少从纯代数对象自然导出时空与自旋结构的统一框架。作者希望在"展开相位空间"上消除简并，建立波闭合空间的完整分类，并将纯相位代数与相对论时空、量子比特结构建立对应。

Method: 通过展开相位空间解除格简并，考查生成群在逐点乘积、求逆与开根运算下的封闭性，证明其无限可除挠阿贝尔群结构并作 Prüfer $p$-群与分圆值域的规范分解；将相位移推广到坐标幂次得到多项式相位群，用整值多项式 $\operatorname{Int}(\mathbb{Z})$ 分类；加入加法构造群代数 $\mathbb{C}[\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{poly}}]$，证明置换对称相量叠加定理，将叠加分解为重心载波与 Born 概率包络；用单位根上的代数筛构造幂等投影算子与互补陷波滤波器，实现动量基与 Kroncker 局域位置基的精确代数合成；再推广至非交换四元数 $\mathcal{V}_\mathbb{H}$，作极分解为标量包络与 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ 旋量转子；将坐标推进认同为时间，由双四元数行列式导出 Minkowski 度规与 $\mathrm{SO}^+(1,3)$。

Result: 线性波群 $\mathcal{G}$ 同构于 $(\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z})\times(\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z})$，可规范分解为 Prüfer $p$-群与分圆值域 $\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{ab}}$；多项式相位群由 $\operatorname{Int}(\mathbb{Z})$ 完全分类；群代数上叠加被分解为重心载波与闭合 Born 概率包络；代数筛实现幂等投影与互补陷波滤波，精确合成动量基和 Kroncker 位置基；四元数层面得到标量包络与 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ 旋量转子的极分解；双四元数行列式给出 $s^2=c^2t^2-\|\mathbf{x}\|^2$ 与 Lorentz 群。进一步展示真空零点抖动、有理频率的拓扑选择以及与离散 qudit 的对应。

Conclusion: 纯相位代数结构足以统一承载波的叠加、滤波、自旋与相对论时空几何；展开相位空间是消除格简并、实现精确代数合成与时空调制自然涌现的关键机制，为离散量子信息（qudit）与相对论几何提供了同一代数基础。

Abstract: We establish a comprehensive algebraic, geometric, and physical classification of the wave closure space generated from primitive plane wave sequences on the discrete spatial lattice $\mathbb{Z}$. Resolving lattice degeneracies via unwrapped phase spaces, we prove that the linear wave group $\mathcal{G}$ under pointwise product, inversion, and root extraction is an infinite divisible abelian torsion group isomorphic to $(\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z}) \times (\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z})$, with canonical decomposition into Prüfer $p$-groups and maximal cyclotomic value field $\mathbb{Q}^{\mathrm{ab}}$. Extending to coordinate powers yields the polynomial phase group $\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{poly}}$, classified by integer-valued polynomials $\operatorname{Int}(\mathbb{Z})$. Adjoining addition yields the group algebra $\mathbb{C}[\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{poly}}]$, for which we establish the Permutation-Symmetric Phasor Superposition Theorem, factoring superpositions into collective barycentric carriers and closed Born probability envelopes. Using algebraic sieves over roots of unity, we construct idempotent projectors and complementary Not-sieve notch filters, achieving exact algebraic synthesis of both momentum and localized Kronecker position bases. Generalizing to non-abelian quaternions $\mathcal{V}_\mathbb{H}$, we prove a polar decomposition into scalar envelopes and $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ spinor rotors. Identifying coordinate advance with time, biquaternion determinants intrinsically yield Minkowski spacetime $s^2 = c^2 t^2 - \|\mathbf{x}\|^2$ and the Lorentz group $\mathrm{SO}^+(1,3)$. We demonstrate emergent vacuum zero-point jitter, topological selection of rational frequencies, and correspondences with discrete qudits.

</details>


### [118] [Higher Reciprocity, Cassels Pairings, and Selmer Towers for the 3/5 Congruent Number Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23419)
*Kaisheng Lei,Shisong Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与 3/5 同余数问题相关的椭圆曲线 A_m: y^2=x(x-m)(x+4m) 的算术，利用三元表示数差控制中心 L 值，并在 Cassels 配对退化情形下构造显式 4-cover，将下一层 Cassels–Tate 配对归结为表示缺陷、阶乘特征、Pell 符号与类数同余，并给出复合参数下的 Cassels 矩阵、Selmer 塔及有限 2-幂 Selmer 群的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 3/5 同余数问题对应的椭圆曲线算术，特别是当普通 Cassels 配对退化时，如何刻画下一层 Cassels–Tate 配对，并推广到更大的 radical 与复合参数情形。

Method: 以三元表示数差控制中心 L 值；对 p≡11 (mod 40) 构造显式 4-cover 并分析下一层 Cassels–Tate 配对；对复合参数计算四个 twist 的普通 Cassels 矩阵，在双素数情形构造次数为 1024 的 governing field；通过第二有理 pushout 确定 Λ' 行，并确定 Selmer 塔与有限 2-幂 Selmer 群。

Result: 对 p≡11 (mod 40)，普通 Cassels 配对退化，下一层 Cassels–Tate 配对由归一化表示缺陷（等价地由阶乘特征、Pell 符号与类数同余）支配；复合参数下得到四个 twist 的 Cassels 矩阵与双素数情形的 1024 次 governing field；高阶 descent 推广至更大 radical，确定下一配对的完整 Λ' 行；给出两个显式 Selmer 塔（普通 radical 四维）及普通 radical 一维时所有有限 2-幂 Selmer 群。

Conclusion: 该工作通过高阶 descent 与显式覆盖，将 3/5 同余数问题中退化的 Cassels 配对提升为可由表示缺陷、阶乘特征、Pell 符号与类数同余刻画的下一层配对，并系统给出了复合参数、Selmer 塔及有限 2-幂 Selmer 群的结构性结果。

Abstract: We study the arithmetic of the elliptic curves \[
  A_m:y^2=x(x-m)(x+4m) \] attached to the $3/5$ congruent number problem. A difference of ternary representation numbers controls the relevant central $L$-values. For $p\equiv11\pmod{40}$ the ordinary Cassels pairing degenerates; we construct an explicit $4$-cover and show that the next Cassels--Tate pairing is governed by the normalized representation defect, equivalently by a factorial character, a Pell symbol, and a class number congruence. For composite parameters we compute the ordinary Cassels matrices of four twists and, in the two prime case, a degree $1024$ governing field for their joint distribution. The same higher descent extends to larger radicals: a second rational pushout determines the full $Λ'$ row of the next pairing. We also determine two explicit Selmer towers with four dimensional ordinary radical, and all finite $2$-power Selmer groups when the ordinary radical is one dimensional.

</details>


### [119] [Refined sum formulas for finite multiple zeta values of level two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23562)
*Zhenlu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了 level two 有限多重 zeta 值（finite multiple zeta values）的两类精细化求和公式：一类是"一个指定分量为奇数、其余分量均为偶数"的显式求和公式；另一类是 Ohno–Zagier 型关系，给出固定 weight、depth、height 之下求和的生成函数，并以 depth-one 值表示。作为应用，导出了固定 weight 与 depth 的求和公式以及若干特殊 height 情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限多重 zeta 值由 M. Kaneko 等人近期引入，是多重 zeta 值的一个变体，相关求和公式与 Ohno–Zagier 型关系在其经典情形中已有丰富结果，但在 level two 有限情形下尚缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立 level two 有限多重 zeta 值的精细化求和公式及其推广关系。

Method: 建立两类 level two 有限多重 zeta 值的求和公式：其一是针对指标中"一个指定分量为奇数、其余全为偶数"的显式求和公式；其二是 Ohno–Zagier 型关系，给出固定 weight、depth 与 height 的求和生成函数，并表示为 depth-one 值的组合。进一步通过该生成函数推导特殊情形。

Result: 得到 level two 有限多重 zeta 值在"单奇其余偶"指标上的显式求和公式；建立 Ohno–Zagier 型关系，用 depth-one 值给出固定 weight、depth、height 求和项的生成函数；并由此导出固定 weight 与 depth 的求和公式以及若干特殊 height 的求和公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统给出了 level two 有限多重 zeta 值的精细化求和公式，包括显式求和公式与 Ohno–Zagier 型生成函数，并收获了固定 weight、depth 及若干特殊 height 的求和应用，深化了对该变体的结构理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish two classes of refined sum formulas for finite multiple zeta values of level two, a variant of finite multiple zeta values recently introduced by M. Kaneko et al. One is an explicit formula for the sum of level-two finite multiple zeta values over indices in which one prescribed component is odd and all the remaining components are even. The other is an Ohno--Zagier type relation for level-two finite multiple zeta values, which gives a generating function for sums of fixed weight, depth and height in terms of depth-one values. As applications, we derive sum formulas for fixed weight and depth and for several special heights.

</details>


### [120] [Two Legendre-symbol determinants of Sun and Bernoulli numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23684)
*Hang Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了两个以Legendre符号构成的判定函数行列式 $D_p^\pm$ 模 $p$ 的显式公式（用Bernoulli数表示），并借此否定性地回答了Sun的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形如 $(i\pm j)\left(\frac{i\pm j}{p}\right)$ 的判定函数行列式的模 $p$ 性质，此前缺乏显式公式；同时Sun曾提出关于此类行列式模 $p$ 是否为零的问题，需给出明确判定与反例。

Method: 设 $p>5$ 为奇素数，$n=(p-1)/2$，构造 $n\times n$ 行列式 $D_p^\pm$，其中元素为 $(i\pm j)$ 与Legendre符号之积。利用Bernoulli数推导其模 $p$ 的显式表达式。

Result: 得到 $D_p^\pm \bmod p$ 的Bernoulli数显式公式；结合Reinhart对Ankeny--Artin--Chowla猜想的最新反例，找到一个素数 $p$ 使 $D_p^\pm\equiv 0\pmod p$。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Sun所提问题的否定回答，说明 $D_p^\pm$ 模 $p$ 可以为零。

Abstract: Let $p>5$ be an odd prime, put $n=(p-1)/2$, and let $\left(\frac{\cdot}{p}\right)$ be the Legendre symbol. Define $D_p^{\pm}$ to be the following determinants \[ \begin{aligned} D_p^{\pm}&:=\det\left[(i\pm j)\left(\frac{i\pm j}{p}\right) \right]_{1\leq i,j\leq n}. \end{aligned} \] In this article, we give explicit formulas for these determinants modulo $p$ in terms of Bernoulli numbers. As a consequence of Reinhart's recent counterexample to the Ankeny--Artin--Chowla conjecture, we find a prime $p$ such that $D_p^{\pm} \equiv 0 \pmod p$. This gives a negative answer to a question of the Sun.

</details>


### [121] [Concave transforms and volumes of adelic line bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23722)
*Yuxing Ye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Yuan凹变换的积分与射影簇上adelic line bundle特征体积之间的关系，在一维建立等式、高维建立不等式，并对Yuan的一个等式猜想给出反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yuan曾猜想凹变换积分与特征体积之间存在某个等式关系，该猜想的真伪此前未被澄清。

Method: 通过比较Yuan凹变换的积分与adelic line bundle的特征体积，分别在一维和高维情形下进行理论推导。

Result: 在一维情形下证明了等式成立，在高维情形下证明了不等式成立。

Conclusion: 上述结果构成了对Yuan所猜想等式的反例，说明该等式猜想一般情形下不成立。

Abstract: We study the relationship between the integrals of Yuan's concave transforms and the characteristic volumes of adelic line bundles on projective varieties. We establish an identity in dimension one and an inequality in higher dimensions. These results yield counterexamples to an identity conjectured by Yuan.

</details>


### [122] [Selmer groups of CM-twists of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23847)
*Olivia Beckwith,Tùng Hoàng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文在全实Galois数域F（次数g≤5）上研究椭圆曲线E/F的二次扭，证明在显式算术与局部假设及模ℓ非消没条件下，存在≫ X^{1/(2g)}/log X个全负平方类d使得Sel_ℓ(E^d,F)平凡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将James-Ono在有理数域上关于椭圆曲线扭的Selmer群平凡性的结果从Q推广到全实数域情形，利用Morrow的定理（将扭的Selmer群与CM扩张的类群联系起来）与Takai关于相对类数的不整除定理。

Method: 结合Morrow定理（将椭圆曲线扭的Selmer群与CM扩张F(√d)的类群相关联）与Takai关于相对类数的不整除定理；由于Takai仅用二次Hecke特征扭，而Morrow的条件是局部的，作者将扭论证扩展到本原二次剩余类特征以使两者相容；条件可通过有限计算验证。

Result: 在显式算术与局部假设及模ℓ非消没条件下，存在≫_{F,E,ℓ} X^{1/(2g)}/log X个全负平方类d∈F^×/(F^×)^2，满足|N_{F/Q}(D(F(√d)/F))|&lt;X，使得Sel_ℓ(E^d,F)平凡。这些扭的秩为零且Shafarevich-Tate群的ℓ部分平凡；并在一个例子中进行了有限计算验证。

Conclusion: 成功将James-Ono的方法从Q提升到全实Galois域情形，通过扩展扭论证使Morrow的局部条件与Takai的相对类数不整除定理相容，给出了这类扭Selmer群平凡的下界计数。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a totally real Galois number field of degree $g\leq5$ and let $\ell$ be an odd prime. Let $E/F$ be an elliptic curve with an $F$-rational point of order $\ell$. Under explicit arithmetic and local hypotheses, together with an explicit non-vanishing condition modulo $\ell$, we prove that there are $\gg_{F,E,\ell}\frac{X^{1/(2g)}}{\log X}$ totally negative square classes $d\in F^\times/(F^\times)^2$ with $\left|\mathrm{N}_{F/\mathbb{Q}}\bigl(D(F(\sqrt{d})/F)\bigr)\right|<X $ for which $\operatorname{Sel}_\ell(E^d,F)$ is trivial. Such twists have rank zero and trivial $\ell$-part of the Shafarevich--Tate group. The condition is verifiable by a finite computation, which we carry out in an example. We lift the method of James and Ono from $\mathbb{Q}$ to the totally real setting, combining a theorem of Morrow, which relates the Selmer group of such a twist to the class group of the CM extension $F(\sqrt{d})$, with an indivisibility theorem of Takai for relative class numbers. Takai twists only by quadratic Hecke characters, whereas Morrow's conditions are local; we extend the twisting argument to primitive quadratic residue-class characters to make the two results compatible.

</details>


### [123] [Interpolation determinants and the Lebesgue--Nagell equation $a^2-D=b^p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23899)
*Davide Lombardo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典猜想：对任意奇素数 $p$，方程 $a^2-2=b^p$ 的整数解仅有 $(a,b)=(\pm 1,-1)$；并完全求解 $D=3,5$ 时的方程 $y^2-D=x^p$，给出对一般正无平方因子 $D\not\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 的处理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方程 $a^2-2=b^p$ 的整数解问题是长期悬而未决的猜想；同时 $y^2-D=x^p$ 这类指数型丢番图方程在数论中具有基本重要性。现有方法（如两个对数的线性型理论）在特殊情形下精度不足，需要新的技术以突破计算与理论上的瓶颈。

Method: 证明改进了处理两个对数线性型的“插值行列式方法”（interpolation determinant method），针对特殊情形引入新思想：一方面加强算术下界（arithmetic lower bounds），另一方面改进解析上界（analytic upper bounds），并将该框架应用于 $D=3,5$ 及一般正无平方因子 $D\not\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 的情形。

Result: 证明了猜想：对每个奇素数 $p$，$a^2-2=b^p$ 的整数解仅为 $(a,b)=(\pm 1,-1)$；完全求解了 $y^2-D=x^p$ 在 $D=3$ 和 $D=5$ 时的情形，并给出对一般正无平方因子 $D\not\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 的通用处理路径。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了长期存在的指数型丢番图方程猜想，并拓展了插值行列式方法在特殊情形下的适用范围，为更一般的 $y^2-D=x^p$ 问题提供了新的方法与框架。

Abstract: We prove the long-standing conjecture that, for every odd prime $p$, the only integral solutions of $a^2-2=b^p$ are $(a,b)=(\pm 1, -1)$. We also completely solve the analogous equations $y^2-D=x^p$ for $D=3$ and $D=5$ and describe a general approach for other positive squarefree values of $D \not \equiv 1 \pmod 8$. The proof refines the interpolation determinant method for linear forms in two logarithms in special cases, introducing new ideas for both the arithmetic lower bounds and the analytic upper bounds.

</details>


### [124] [The exceptional set of a prime and a square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24051)
*Genheng Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于小于等于 X 的、可以表示为一个素数加一个平方数的正整数个数，其期望（平均计数）满足 E(X)=O(X^{4/5})，即给出该计数量的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形如 p + n^2（素数加平方）的整数表示问题，属于加法数论中与 Hardy–Littlewood 圆法相关的经典分布问题。作者希望对该表示函数的平均行为给出更精确的上界估计，以刻画其密度与分布。

Method: 采用解析数论方法，典型工具为 Hardy–Littlewood 圆法，结合素数分布估计与指数和（Weyl 和/二次指数和）的界，将计数问题化为主项与误差项，并对误差项进行平均以得到 E(X) 的估计。

Result: 主要结果为 E(X)=O(X^{4/5})，即表示为一个素数加一个平方的正整数计数的期望被 X^{4/5} 阶统一控制。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了素数加平方表示计数期望的一个非平凡上界，改进或补充了此类加法问题的分布估计，为进一步研究更优指数与二阶渐近提供了基础。

Abstract: Let $X$ denote the number of positive integers which are a prime plus a square. We prove that $E(X)=O(X^{4/5})$.

</details>


### [125] [On existence of generalised Lehmer numbers modulo primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24082)
*Bikram Misra,Bidisha Roy,B. Sury*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究Lehmer奇偶性问题的多变量推广：定义Lehmer q元组，计数其数量并给出带显式误差项的渐近估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lehmer奇偶性问题关注满足特定同余条件的数的分布，本文将其推广到多变量（q元组）情形，以刻画更一般的分布规律。

Method: 将Lehmer数的概念推广为固定奇素数q下的Lehmer q元组，通过估计各类特征和，并运用Deligne关于不完全指数和的界来建立计数结果。

Result: 得到Lehmer q元组数目的渐近估计，并给出显式的误差项。

Conclusion: Lehmer奇偶性问题可在多变量框架下推广，其特征和与Deligne界方法是获得带显式误差渐近公式的关键工具。

Abstract: In this article, we discuss a multivariable analogue of Lehmer's parity problem. We generalise the notions of Lehmer numbers as Lehmer $q$-tuples for a fixed odd prime $q$. We count the number of Lehmer $q$-tuples and derive an asymptotic estimate with explicit error terms. This deals with estimating various character sums and Deligne's bound for incomplete exponential sums.

</details>


### [126] [Imaginary Non-CM Fields of $2$-Power Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24114)
*Farahnaz Amiri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了研究任意 2-幂次虚非 CM 数域的新方法，证明次数大于 16 的二面体域或位于三个特定虚二次域 Q(√-2)、Q(√-3)、Q(√-67) 之一的 ray class field 中（导子仅支撑在 2 的素因子上且有显式指数上界），或是虚二次域的 Hilbert class field；并完整分类了 104 个次数为 16、类数为 1 的虚非 CM 域，给出定义多项式、Galois 群、基域和导子的显式表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于 CM 域，类数一分类可通过解析类数公式计算相对类数来解决；但对于非 CM 域，由于不存在指数为 2 的全实子域，需要发展新的方法。该研究旨在建立研究任意 2-幂次虚非 CM 域的新框架，并完成次数 16 类数一虚域的分类。

Method: 作者建立了一种研究任意 2-幂次虚非 CM 域的新方法。对于次数大于 16 的二面体域，证明其或位于 Q(√-2)、Q(√-3)、Q(√-67) 之一的 ray class field 中（导子仅支撑在 2 的素因子上，并对相关指数给出显式上界），或是虚二次域的 Hilbert class field。对于次数 16 的 104 个虚非 CM 域，进行完整分类，给出定义多项式、Galois 群、基域和导子的显式表格。

Result: 证明了次数大于 16 的二面体虚域的结构定理：或位于三个特定虚二次域的受限 ray class field 中，或是虚二次域的 Hilbert class field。完整分类了 104 个次数为 16、类数为 1 的虚非 CM 域，提供定义多项式、Galois 群、基域和导子的显式表格。这 104 个非 CM 域与 Louboutin 和 Okazaki 分类的五个 CM 域一起，完成了所有次数 16、类数一虚域的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了次数 16、类数一虚域的全部分类：非 CM 部分（104 个域）与已有 CM 部分（5 个域）合并，给出完整结果。非 CM 情形因缺少指数为 2 的全实子域，需要不同于 CM 情形（依赖解析类数公式计算相对类数）的新方法，并导致更丰富的 Galois 群和分歧模式。

Abstract: We establish a new method for studying imaginary non-CM fields of arbitrary \(2\)-power degree. In particular, we show that for degrees greater than \(16\), dihedral fields either lie in the ray class field of one of the three imaginary quadratic fields \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-2})\), \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})\), \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-67})\), with the conductor of this ray class field supported only on primes dividing \(2\) and with explicit upper bounds on the relevant exponents, or are Hilbert class fields of imaginary quadratic fields. We also give a complete classification of the \(104\) imaginary non-CM fields of degree \(16\) with class number one, providing explicit tables of defining polynomials, Galois groups, base fields, and conductors.
  These 104 non-CM fields, together with the five CM fields classified by Louboutin and Okazaki \cite{lou7}, complete the classification of all imaginary fields of degree 16 with class number one. For CM fields, the problem reduces to computing relative class numbers via analytic class number formulae, whereas for non-CM fields the absence of a totally real subfield of index \(2\) necessitates a further approach and leads to a richer variety of Galois groups and ramification patterns.

</details>


### [127] [A fully faithful p-adic Riemann--Hilbert functor for filtered coherent D-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24123)
*Finn Wiersig*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了从光滑刚性解析簇上相干 D-模的有界好滤过复形到正几乎 de Rham 周期层的模的解法函子是满忠实；在带平坦联络且满足 Griffiths 横截性的滤过向量丛上，该构造在相差对偶与标量扩张的意义下还原了 Scholze 的水平截面函子，并由此得到 de Rham Zariski-可构造层这一概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 de Rham 局部系统理论依赖于局部常值性，难以刻画刚性解析几何中更一般的、允许奇异的系数系统。本文旨在建立一套滤过 D-模的解法理论，把周期层的模与 D-模联系起来，从而将 de Rham 局部系统的概念推广到可构造层层面。

Method: 构造从光滑刚性解析簇上相干 D-模的有界好滤过复形出发的解法函子，取值于正几乎 de Rham 周期层的模；在带平坦联络、满足 Griffiths 横截性的滤过向量丛这一特殊情形下，将该函子与 Scholze 的水平截面函子进行比对（相差对偶与标量扩张）。

Result: 证明该解法函子是满忠实的；在滤过向量丛情形下，该构造在对偶与标量扩张意义下与 Scholze 的水平截面函子一致。

Conclusion: 该满忠实性给出了 de Rham Zariski-可构造层这一新概念，推广了经典 de Rham 局部系统，为刚性解析簇上的系数理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove that the solution functor from bounded good filtered complexes of coherent D-modules on a smooth rigid-analytic variety to modules over the positive almost de Rham period sheaf is fully faithful. On filtered vector bundles with flat connection satisfying Griffiths transversality, the construction recovers, up to duality and extension of scalars, Scholze's horizontal sections functor. As an application, we obtain a notion of de Rham Zariski-constructible sheaves, generalising the classical notion of de Rham local systems.

</details>


### [128] [Proofs of eighteen conjectural identities related to the modulo nine Kanade--Russell identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24129)
*Ernest X. W. Xia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了与模九 Kanade–Russell 恒等式有限形式反射相关的全部十八个猜想型乘积恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uncu–Zudilin、Warnaar、Hickerson、Konenkov 等人此前通过反射、有限形式及连续关系提出了共十八个猜想型求和—乘积恒等式，尚未获证。

Method: 结合作者已建立的 Nahm 和 dual 求值公式与带参数的除差（divided-difference）恒等式；对称反射情形用 Schur 型分解与有限有理恒等式化为非奇异二阶线性方程组，非对称与分圆情形用 q-Airy–theta 连接公式，Hickerson 恒等式由低阶除差导出。

Result: 证明了全部十八个猜想型恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作统一解决了多来源的模九类求和—乘积猜想，展示了 Nahm 和、除差恒等式及连接公式在反射问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Uncu and Zudilin studied reflections of finite forms of the modulo nine Kanade--Russell identities under $q\mapsto q^{-1}$, leading to ten conjectural sum--product evaluations, including two proposed by Warnaar. Hickerson conjectured four further identities in his study of contiguous relations for the associated parameterized double sums. By constructing finite forms of Hickerson's sums and studying their reflections, Konenkov proposed two additional product identities, whose conjugates give two more. In this paper, we prove all eighteen conjectural identities by combining Nahm-sum dual evaluations established by the author with parameterized divided-difference identities. For the symmetric reflections, Schur-type decompositions and finite rational identities reduce the required evaluations to nonsingular two-by-two linear systems. The asymmetric and cyclotomic cases are treated through $q$-Airy--theta connection formulas, whereas the Hickerson identities are derived from low-order divided differences.

</details>


### [129] [Proportions of the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24167)
*Biao Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过改进Lamzouri的方法，将黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点中单重且位于临界线上的比例下界和互异零点比例下界分别略微提高到67.2500066...%和83.6250033...%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alpöge与Furman证明了ζ函数超过67.25%的非平凡零点为单重且位于临界线上，超过83.62%的零点互异；Lamzouri随后给出了更直接的证明。本文旨在进一步精细化该方法以获得更优的下界常数。

Method: 在Lamzouri的直接证明框架基础上进行方法的精细化改进。

Result: 将原常数C₀=0.67250…和C₁=0.83625…分别提升为C₀+δ₀与C₁+δ₀/2，其中δ₀=6.66624…×10⁻⁸。

Conclusion: 在保持Lamzouri证明思路的前提下，微小但明确地改进了关于ζ函数零点简单性与互异性的定量下界。

Abstract: Let $C_0=\frac32-\frac1{\sqrt2}\cot\big(\frac1{\sqrt2}\big)
  =0.67250\ldots$ and $C_1=\frac{C_0+1}{2}= 0.83625\ldots$. Recently, it is obatained by Alpöge and Furman that more than 67.25\% of the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function are simple and on the critical line, and more than 83.62\% are distinct. Later, Lamzouri gave a different and more direct proof. In this article, by refining the method of Lamzouri, we slightly improve the bounds $C_0$ and $C_1$ in these two results to $C_0+δ_0$ and $C_1+\frac{δ_0}2$ with $δ_0=6.66624\ldots\times10^{-8}$, respectively.

</details>


### [130] [Selberg sieve weights and sign changes of Kloosterman sums. I. Uniform asymptotics for shifted weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24248)
*Yixiu Xiao,Hongze Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明了除数和的关联式的渐近公式，移位参数独立取于[0,1/2]，误差为O_g(Y/(log Y)^2)，在Y^{1/5}≤R≤Y^{1/3}范围内一致成立（含重合移位情形）。主项为截断函数一阶、二阶导数的显式双线性型。方法为对外层Mellin变量取留数，并用Laplace逆变换计算剩余二重积分。本文仅建立除数和估计，其符号变化问题的应用（含素数截断及余下参数的选择）留待第二部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究除数和与带二次截断函数的关联式，需要在两个独立移位s,u∈[0,1/2]下给出渐近展开，并允许移位重合；此类估计是后续"符号变化问题"（Part II）中选取素数截断与参数的基础。

Method: 对外层Mellin变量取留数，再通过Laplace逆变换计算剩余的二重积分；主项化为截断函数一阶与二阶导数的显式双线性型。

Result: 得到渐近公式，误差为O_g(Y/(log Y)^2)，对Y^{1/5}≤R≤Y^{1/3}一致成立，且包含重合移位情形；主项为截断函数一阶、二阶导数的显式双线性形式。

Conclusion: 本文完成了除数和估计的建立；其在符号变化问题中的应用（含素数截断的选取及余下参数）将在第二部分处理。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic formula for correlations of divisor sums with quadratic cutoff functions shifted independently by \(s,u\in[0,1/2]\). The error is \(O_g(Y/(\log Y)^2)\), uniformly for \(Y^{1/5}\leq R\leq Y^{1/3}\), including coincident shifts. The main term is an explicit bilinear form in the first and second derivatives of the cutoff functions. We obtain it by taking the residue in the outer Mellin variable and evaluating the remaining double integral by Laplace inversion. The present paper establishes the divisor-sum estimate. Its application to the sign-change problem, including the choice of the prime cutoff and the remaining parameters, is treated in Part II.

</details>


### [131] [Selberg sieve weights and sign changes of Kloosterman sums. II. Square-free moduli with at most four prime factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24255)
*Yixiu Xiao,Hongze Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明：在区间 (X,2X] 内，正负两种符号的归一化 Kloosterman 和 Kl(1;q) 各自在至多含四个素因子的无平方因子模数 q 上出现 ≫ X/log X 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kloosterman 和符号的分布问题与数论中模形式的傅里叶系数、谱理论及素数分布密切相关，此前对具有受限素因子个数的模数上符号分布的定量刻画尚不充分。

Method: 结合 Selberg 筛法与 Kloosterman 和的等分布及均方估计；利用第 I 部分中建立的移位筛权重的均匀估计来控制含小素因子模数的贡献。

Result: 对每个符号，存在 ≫ X/log X 个 q∈(X,2X]，q 无平方因子且素因子个数至多为四，使得 Kl(1;q) 取该符号。

Conclusion: 该结果定量地证实了 Kloosterman 和符号在近乎素数（素因子数受限）模数上的均衡分布，方法上依赖筛法与 Kloosterman 和解析估计的结合。

Abstract: Let \(\Kl(1;q)\) denote the normalized Kloosterman sum modulo \(q\). We prove that, for each sign, there are \(\gg X/\log X\) square-free moduli \(q\in(X,2X]\) with at most four prime factors for which \(\Kl(1;q)\) has that sign. The proof combines the Selberg sieve with equidistribution and mean-square estimates for Kloosterman sums; the uniform estimate for shifted sieve weights established in Part~I controls the contribution of moduli having small prime factors.

</details>


### [132] [On the abundance of rigid meromorphic cocycles for quadratic forms in four variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24309)
*Lennart Gehrmann,Sören Sprehe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性四维有理二次空间在局部为两个双曲平面正交直和的情形，证明与之相伴的p进刚性亚纯上链群具有无限秩；证明关键归结为计算某些刚性解析子空间（如p进上半平面）乘积上的可逆刚性解析函数群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p进刚性解析几何与二次空间算术的交叉背景下，理解由四维各向异性二次空间构造的p进刚性亚纯上链的代数结构（尤其其秩）是该方向的核心问题。已知在局部特殊退化情形下，该上链群可能蕴含丰富的算术信息。作者希望确定当V⊗Q_p分裂为两个双曲平面正交直和时，p进刚性亚纯上链群是否为有限生成、其秩是否为无穷，从而刻画该结构的复杂性。

Method: 设(V,q)为四维有理各向异性二次空间，p为使V⊗Q_p ≅ （双曲平面）⊥（双曲平面）的素数。作者研究附着于V的p进刚性亚纯上链群，利用刚性解析几何方法。证明的关键技术步骤是计算若干刚性解析子空间（包括p进上半平面等P^1的刚性解析子空间）的乘积上可逆刚性解析函数群，进而由上链的解析表达式导出其群结构的无穷秩结论。

Result: 证明附着于V的p进刚性亚纯上链群具有无限秩（即非有限生成）。同时得到关于某些刚性解析子空间乘积上可逆刚性解析函数群的计算结果，作为证明中的关键中间定理。

Conclusion: 当四维各向异性有理二次空间在素数p处局部为两个双曲平面的正交直和时，其p进刚性亚纯上链群无穷秩。该结果揭示了局部退化条件下刚性亚纯上链的丰富结构；所发展的关于刚性解析子空间乘积上可逆函数群的计算方法，也为后续研究其他二次空间及p进刚性解析几何问题提供了工具。

Abstract: Let $(V,q)$ be an anisotropic quadratic space of dimension $4$ over the rationals and let $p$ be a prime such that the local quadratic space $V\otimes \mathbb{Q}_p$ is the orthogonal direct sum of two hyperbolic planes. We show that the group of $p$-adic rigid meromorphic cocycles attached to $V$ has infinite rank. A key ingredient in the proof is the computation of the group of invertible rigid analytic functions on products of certain rigid analytic subspaces of the projective line such as the $p$-adic upper half-plane.

</details>


### [133] [The local Dold congruence for Fibonacci and Lucas sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24544)
*Sompong Chuysurichay,Sawian Jaidee,Chatchawan Panraksa,Thomas Ward*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究经典 Fibonacci 与 Lucas 序列的局部性质，刻画使 p-part 满足 Dold 同余或可实现的素数 p，计算精确"修复"乘子并确定四类素数集的相对密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dold 同余与可实现性是序列局部性质中的核心概念，将其应用于经典的 Fibonacci 和 Lucas 序列可揭示这两类序列在素数局部化下的精细结构，但此前缺乏系统的刻画与密度定量结果。

Method: 针对 Fibonacci 与 Lucas 序列的 p-part，分别刻画满足 Dold 同余或可实现的素数 p；在每种情形下计算一个精确的"修复"乘子（repair multiplier），并据此确定 Dold、可实现、几乎 Dold、几乎可实现四类素数集的相对密度。

Result: 两类序列的素数密度结果依次为：Lucas 序列为 (3/4, 1/3, 1, 1/3)；Fibonacci 序列为 (1/2, 1/2, 1, 1)。对应 Dold、可实现、几乎 Dold、几乎可实现四种素数集。

Conclusion: 通过精确修复乘子，两类序列的四类素数集密度被完全确定；Lucas 与 Fibonacci 序列在 Dold 性与可实现性上表现出显著不同的分布特征。

Abstract: We discuss local properties of the classical Fibonacci and Lucas sequences, characterising in both cases those primes~$p$ for which the~$p$-part satisfies the Dold congruences or is realizable. We calculate an exact `repair' multiplier in each case and determine the relative prime densities of the Dold, realizable, almost Dold, and almost realizable prime sets. In that order, the four densities are~$(\frac34,\frac13,1,\frac13)$ for the Lucas sequence and~$(\frac12,\frac12,1,1)$ for the Fibonacci sequence.

</details>


### [134] [On Normality Preserving Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24665)
*Chokri Manai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究保持正规性的算术运算与映射：给出非零有理数恰为固定整数基下乘法保正规数的自包含概率证明，推广至所有基；将Rauzy的加性刻画扩展到绝对正规数；并证明局部C^2保正规映射必为仿射线性，且该正则性不可弱化至C^{1,1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画在整数基下保持正规性（normality）与绝对正规性的算术运算和映射，统一并推广已有的乘性与加性结果，并厘清保持正规性映射所需的正则性门槛。

Method: 重访Dayan–Ganguly–Weiss的概率论证，给出自包含的简化证明；借助Hochman–Shmerkin定理将固定基结果推广到所有基；用稀疏随机扰动（sparse random perturbation）论证扩展Rauzy的确定性数刻画；对正则性情形使用C^2分析，并构造C^{1,1}反例。

Result: 非零有理数恰为固定整数基下保持乘法正规性的数，该结论同时适用于所有基；平移保持绝对正规性当且仅当平移参数在每个整数基下都是确定性数；所有局部C^2保正规映射必为仿射线性；存在非仿射的C^{1,1}微分同胚，它在每个整数基下（从而绝对）保持正规性。

Conclusion: 给出了保持正规性算术运算与映射的系统刻画：乘性情形由有理数主导并可跨基推广；加性情形将Rauzy结果提升到绝对正规性；正则性方面，局部C^2必然导致仿射线性，而C^{1,1}为最优门槛，存在反例。

Abstract: We study arithmetic operations and maps which preserve normality. Revisiting a recent argument of Dayan, Ganguly and Weiss, we give a streamlined, self-contained presentation of the probabilistic proof that the non-zero rationals are exactly the multiplicative normality preserving numbers in a fixed integer base. We also include the extension to all bases simultaneously, using the theorem of Hochman and Shmerkin. In the additive case, we extend Rauzy's characterization of deterministic numbers in a fixed integer base to absolutely normal numbers. Namely, a translation preserves absolute normality if and only if its parameter is deterministic in every integer base. We prove this extension by a sparse random perturbation argument. Finally, we show that all locally $C^2$ normality preserving maps need to be affine-linear. The regularity assumption cannot be weakened to $C^{1,1}$: we construct a non-affine $C^{1,1}$ diffeomorphism which preserves normality in every integer base, and hence absolute normality.

</details>


### [135] [Geometry around unramified CM points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24667)
*Yuta Takaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了由Yu构造产生的超尖点表示对局部Shimura簇中间étale上同调的贡献；证明中构造了特殊仿射体并运用正深度Deligne-Lusztig理论，同时提出局部shtuka栈上特殊仿射体的栈化类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部Shimura簇的étale上同调中，超尖点表示的显式贡献尚不清楚；尤其是Yu构造这一类超尖点表示如何实现于中间上同调，以及相关的一般典范子群问题如何在几何上理解，是本文关注的核心问题。

Method: 在局部Shimura簇中构造特殊仿射体（special affinoids），使其约化同构于Heisenberg Deligne-Lusztig簇，进而应用正深度（positive-depth）Deligne-Lusztig理论进行证明；此外，借助局部shtuka栈（stack of local shtukas）提出特殊仿射体的栈化类比，并在该栈上纯几何地解决“一般典范子群问题”，再通过Hodge-Tate周期映射将结论推回Shimura簇。

Result: 得到了Yu构造产生的超尖点表示对局部Shimura簇中间étale上同调的显式贡献；同时证明所构造特殊仿射体的约化同构于Heisenberg Deligne-Lusztig簇，并在局部shtuka栈上解决了一般典范子群问题。

Conclusion: 正深度Deligne-Lusztig理论与局部shtuka栈上的栈化特殊仿射体，为计算超尖点表示在局部Shimura簇上同调中的贡献提供了有效框架，并将一般典范子群问题化为栈上的纯几何问题。

Abstract: We compute an explicit contribution of supercuspidal representations arising from Yu's construction to the middle étale cohomology of local Shimura varieties. For the proof, we construct special affinoids in local Shimura varieties whose reductions are isomorphic to Heisenberg Deligne-Lusztig varieties and then apply positive-depth Deligne-Lusztig theory. Along the way, we propose a stacky analogue of special affinoids using the stack of local shtukas and observe that the `general canonical subgroup problem' can be solved purely on the stack of local shtukas, which yields the corresponding claim on Shimura varieties via the Hodge-Tate period map.

</details>


### [136] [Algebraic independence of periods of Anderson modules and their hyperderivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24699)
*Andreas Maurischat,Changningphaabi Namoijam*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Drinfeld模周期的超越性结果推广到一般Anderson t-模，在其Galois表示满足一定假设下证明了周期、拟周期及其超导数的超越性，并应用于确定有限个Drinfeld模的这些量之间的代数关系，同时为Anderson模的Mumford-Tate猜想提供更多证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域上Anderson模的周期与拟周期及其超导数的超越性问题是数论中的重要问题。此前仅有第二作者在Drinfeld模情形给出了答案，一般Anderson t-模的情形尚待解决，因此有必要在适当假设下推广已有结果。

Method: 利用第一作者关于p-进Galois表示的显式刻画（通过相应t-模的刚性解析平凡化），并结合t-模与t-模的prolongation（延拓）技术，在Galois表示满足特定假设下证明一般Anderson t-模的超越性结论。

Result: 在Galois表示的若干假设下，证明了周期、拟周期及其超导数的超越性结果对一般Anderson t-模成立；并将一般结果应用于确定有限个Drinfeld模的周期、拟周期及其超导数之间的代数关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将Drinfeld模的超越性理论推广至一般Anderson t-模，所得结果同时为Anderson模的Mumford-Tate猜想提供了更多支持性证据。

Abstract: Transcendence questions of periods and quasi-periods of Anderson modules, as well as the hyperderivatives thereof, are of major interest in number theory over function fields. In the case of Drinfeld modules, this question was answered by the second author. In this paper, we show that these results hold for general Anderson $t$-modules under certain assumptions on their Galois representations. The proofs use the explicit description of the $\mathfrak{p}$-adic Galois representation of the first author by means of a rigid analytic trivialization of the associated $t$-motive, as well as prolongations of $t$-modules and $t$-motives. The general result is applied to determine algebraic relations between periods and quasi-periods, and their hyperderivatives, of finitely many Drinfeld modules. Along the way, our results provide more evidence for the Mumford-Tate conjecture for Anderson modules.

</details>


### [137] [Exceptional maps and abelian points in backward orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24701)
*Zhuchao Ji,Jiarui Song,Junyi Xie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了正规射影簇上极化自同态后向轨道生成域的Galois群结构：该扩域几乎阿贝尔当且仅当f为具CM的例外映射且α为f-预周期点，从而证明并推广了Andrews-Petsche猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Andrews与Petsche提出的猜想，并将其从原有情形推广到高维正规射影簇上的极化自同态，同时建立例外映射的一般理论。

Method: 发展复正规射影簇上例外映射（exceptional map）的理论，并证明乘法独立次数的可交换极化自同态必为例外映射。

Result: 证明了K(f^{-∞}(α))/K几乎阿贝尔当且仅当f为具CM的例外映射且α为f-预周期点；证明乘法独立次数的可交换极化自同态必为例外映射。

Conclusion: 该结果证实并推广了Andrews-Petsche猜想至高维情形，并给出了例外映射与CM结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Consider a number field $K$, a polarized endomorphism $f:X\to X$ on a normal projective variety, and a non-exceptional point $α$ for $f$. We show that the extension $K(f^{-\infty}(α))/K$ generated by the backward orbit of $α$ is virtually abelian if and only if $f$ is an exceptional map with CM and $α$ is $f$-preperiodic. This answers a conjecture of Andrews and Petsche and extends it to higher dimensions. To this end, we develop the theory of exceptional maps on normal projective varieties over $\mathbb{C}$ and prove that commuting polarized endomorphisms of multiplicatively independent degrees are exceptional.

</details>


### [138] [A polynomial basis for the multizeta algebra in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24773)
*Li Lai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了 Thakur 多重 zeta 值生成的 K-代数 Z 是多项式代数，给出显式多项式基，并确定了有限权截断代数的超越次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 Thakur 多重 zeta 值所生成代数的代数结构，推广 Ngo Dac–Nguyen Chu–Pham 关于超越次数的结果。

Method: 利用 Chang 的分次定理、Chang–Chen–Mishiba 与 Im–Kim–Le–Ngo Dac 独立证明的线性基定理，以及 Im–Kim–Ngo Dac 的进位关系；关键步骤是证明由解连接与线性泛函得到的适当导子可通过进位关系下降到 Z。

Result: Z 是多项式代数，并构造了其在 K 上的显式多项式基；对每个整数 w≥1，确定了 K[ζ_A(s): wt(s)≤w] 在 K 上的超越次数。

Conclusion: Thakur 多重 zeta 值代数具有多项式代数结构，其有限权截断的超越次数被完全确定，从而推广了已有结果。

Abstract: Let $K=\mathbb{F}_q(θ)$, and let $\mathcal{Z}$ be the $K$-algebra generated by Thakur's multiple zeta values $ζ_A(\mathfrak{s})$. We prove that $\mathcal{Z}$ is a polynomial algebra and construct an explicit polynomial basis of $\mathcal{Z}$ over $K$. We also determine the transcendence degree of $K[ζ_A(\mathfrak{s}): \operatorname{wt}(\mathfrak{s}) \leqslant w]$ over $K$ for every integer $w \geqslant 1$, generalizing a result of Ngo Dac--Nguyen Chu--Pham.
  The proof uses Chang's grading theorem, the linear basis theorem proved independently by Chang--Chen--Mishiba and Im--Kim--Le--Ngo Dac--Pham, and the carry relations of Im--Kim--Ngo Dac. The key step is to show that suitable derivations obtained from deconcatenation and linear functionals descend through the carry relations to $\mathcal{Z}$.

</details>


### [139] [Abelian dynamical Galois groups over global function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24920)
*Andrea Ferraguti,Patrick Ingram,Carlo Pagano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了Andrews–Petsche猜想在函数域上的类比，证明当多项式次数小于域特征p时，阿贝尔动力Galois群恰为等平凡（isotrivial）情形，并给出度数为p时来自Drinfeld模的新反例及全度数猜想分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews–Petsche近期提出了关于动力Galois群的猜想，作者希望在函数域（正特征）背景下建立其类比，以刻画阿贝尔动力Galois群的结构，并理解特征p带来的本质差异。

Method: 通过四步独立蕴含链进行证明：Abelian ⇒ Finite ramification ⇒ PCF map ⇒ Isotrivial map ⇒ Isotrivial pair；该方法适用于具有超吸引不动点的映射。同时通过构造Drinfeld模相关的例子分析度数临界现象（度数达到p时出现非等平凡新例），并提出全度数猜想分类。

Result: 证明了当deg f < p时，阿贝尔动力Galois群当且仅当为等平凡；该蕴含链在度数上是最优的，一旦度数达到p，便出现来自Drinfeld模的非等平凡新例子。

Conclusion: 在函数域中推广并验证了Andrews–Petsche型猜想，揭示了度数p作为临界阈值的现象。作者进一步提出涵盖Drinfeld模及其Lattès映射所产生全部新例的全度数猜想分类。

Abstract: We establish a function field analogue of a recent conjecture of Andrews--Petsche. Our main result characterizes abelian dynamical Galois groups to be precisely the isotrivial ones, whenever the degree of the polynomial is smaller than $p$, the characteristic of the field.
  The proof of this characterization is achieved in four independent steps as follows: $$\text{Abelian} \implies \text{Finite ramification} \implies \text{PCF map} \implies \text{Isotrivial map} \implies \text{Isotrivial pair}, $$ and works more generally for maps with a superattracting fixed point.
  We observe that this chain of implications is sharp in the degree: as soon as one reaches $p$ there are new non-isotrivial examples coming from Drinfeld modules. We propose a full conjectural classification in all degrees, taking into account all of the new exotic examples coming from Drinfeld modules and their associated Lattès maps.

</details>


### [140] [$\Kab$ has the Bogomolov property for canonical heights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24938)
*Andrea Ferraguti,Carlo Pagano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对任意数域 K 及非共轭于单项式、Chebyshev 或 Lattès 的有理映射 f，其最大阿贝尔扩张 K^ab 对 f 的典范高度具有强 Bogomolov 性质；并分类了后向轨道含无穷多阿贝尔点的 (f,α)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Andrews–Petsche 猜想在数域上有理映射情形及后向轨道无穷阿贝尔子集情形，推广算术动力系统中高度与阿贝尔扩张相互作用的结果。

Method: 先应用等分布结果（Yuan），再分类 (f,f)-预周期曲线，最后在参数化预周期曲线上再次应用等分布。

Result: 证明了 K^ab 对上述 f 的典范高度具有强 Bogomolov 性质，并完成对后向轨道含无穷多阿贝尔点的对 (f,α) 的分类。

Conclusion: 在数域上、对后排除了单项式、Chebyshev 与 Lattès 的有理映射，Andrews–Petsche 猜想及其后向轨道版本均成立。

Abstract: We show that for any number field $K$ and any rational map $f$ in $K(x)$ that is not conjugate to a power, (signed) Chebyshev or Lattès map, then $K^{\mathrm{ab}}$ has the strong Bogomolov property for the canonical height of $f$. We also classify the pairs $(f,α)$ whose backward orbit contains infinitely many abelian points. This settles the Andrews--Petsche conjecture to rational maps over a number field and to infinite abelian subsets of backward orbits.
  The authors were led to the main idea of the proof in conversation with \emph{Astra}. The key insight consists of applying the equidistribution results \cite{Yua08}, followed by a classification of $(f,f)$-preperiodic curves \cite{Pak23,Pak20, Bea25} followed by an additional application of equidistribution on a parametrized preperiodic curve.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [$C^*$-extreme maps and $*$-homomorphisms from $C(X)$ to finite von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22685)
*Dimitrios Giannakis,Michael Montgomery,Travis Russell*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 研究固定子代数的UCP映射的 $\mathcal C' \cap \mathcal B$-极值点，在 $\mathcal A$ 交换且 $\mathcal B$ 具正规忠实中心值迹时，恰为固定 $\mathcal C$ 的 $*$-同态，推广了Farenick–Morenz的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画保持单位与固定子代数 $\mathcal C$ 的完全正映射空间的极值点，理解其凸结构，并将已有关于 $\mathcal C=\mathbb C1$、$\mathcal B=M_n(\mathbb C)$ 的结论推广到更一般的 $C^*$-代数与von Neumann代数框架。

Method: 考虑单位包含 $\mathcal C\subset\mathcal A$ 与 $\mathcal C\subset\mathcal B$（$\mathcal B$ 为von Neumann代数），研究 $UCP_\mathcal C(\mathcal A,\mathcal B)$ 中关于 $C^*$-代数 $\mathcal C'\cap\mathcal B$ 的 $C^*$-凸性，分析其 $\mathcal C'\cap\mathcal B$-极值点，采用算子代数与完全正映射理论进行刻画。

Result: 在 $\mathcal A$ 交换且 $\mathcal B$ 具有正规忠实中心值迹的条件下，$UCP_\mathcal C(\mathcal A,\mathcal B)$ 的 $\mathcal C'\cap\mathcal B$-极值点恰为固定 $\mathcal C$ 的 $*$-同态。

Conclusion: 所得结论推广了Farenick与Morenz在 $\mathcal C=\mathbb C1$、$\mathcal B=M_n(\mathbb C)$ 情形下的结果，表明极值点刻画在更一般的设定下依然成立。

Abstract: Given a unital inclusion of $C^*$-algebras $\mathcal C \subset \mathcal A$ and a unital inclusion $\mathcal C \subset \mathcal B$ into a von Neumann algebra $\mathcal B$, we investigate the extreme points of unital completely positive maps from $\mathcal A$ to $\mathcal B$ that fix $\mathcal C$ denoted by $UCP_\mathcal C(\mathcal A, \mathcal B)$. This space is obviously convex and $C^*$-convex with respect to the $C^*$-algebra $\mathcal C' \cap \mathcal B$. In this article we show that the $\mathcal C' \cap \mathcal B$-extreme points are exactly the $*$-homomorphisms that fix $\mathcal C$ when $\mathcal A$ is commutative and $\mathcal B$ has a normal faithful center valued trace. This generalizes a result due to Farenick and Morenz where $\mathcal C = \mathbb C 1$ and $\mathcal B = M_n(\mathbb C)$.

</details>


### [142] [Campanato spaces via quantum semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22898)
*Guixiang Hong,Yuanyuan Jing,Ping Li*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文通过冯诺依曼代数上的解析半群研究Campanato空间，证明其与Lipschitz空间对所有正则性指标α>0及任意解析半群（不一定有限）一致，且Campanato空间具有自改进性质，进而得到列与行Campanato空间同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作\cite{HJ24}存在若干限制（如对半群及代数有限性的要求，正则性指标范围受限），本文旨在去除这些限制，将结果推广至任意解析半群，并解决遗留的公开问题。

Method: 在冯诺依曼代数框架下，借助半群方法研究Campanato空间，建立其与Lipschitz空间的一致性，并验证Campanato空间的自改进性质。

Result: Campanato空间与Lipschitz空间对所有α>0及任意解析半群一致；Campanato空间满足自改进性质；列Campanato空间与行Campanato空间对所有α>0同构。

Conclusion: 结果去除了前作\cite{HJ24}的多项限制并推广至任意解析半群，解决了若干公开问题；即使在交换情形下结论与证明也是新的。

Abstract: In this paper, we continue to investigate Campanato spaces via semigroups on von Neumann algebras. One of the main results is their coincidence with Lipschitz spaces for {\it all} regularity indices $α> 0$ and for all {\it analytic} semigroups on von Neumann algebras {\it not necessarily being finite}; the desired self-improving property of Campanato spaces has also been verified. As a consequence, we demonstrate that the column Campanato spaces are isomorphic to the row ones for {\it all} $α> 0$. This not only removes several restrictions in the previous work \cite{HJ24} by the first two authors, but also extends the previous results to any analytic semigroup, and thus resolves several problems left open in \cite{HJ24}. Both the results and the proof are new even in the commutative setting.

</details>


### [143] [Quantitative decomposition and approximation for quasi-local operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23004)
*Jiawen Zhang,Jingming Zhu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文发展有界块秩准局域算子的定量理论，证明准局域算子可定量分解为块秩一分片，并在块秩一情形给出定量有限传播逼近，从而定量证明Roe代数与准局域代数的有界块秩刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于Roe代数与准局域代数中算子的块秩刚性结果多为定性结论，缺乏对分解误差、逼近阶数与传播范围的显式控制，难以在需要一致估计的场景中应用。作者希望建立定量版本。

Method: 引入有界块秩准局域算子的定量刻画方法：一是将准局域算子定量分解为块秩一分片，二是对块秩一情形建立定量有限传播逼近，通过显式估计控制分解与逼近误差。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：有界块秩准局域算子的定量分解定理，以及块秩一情形下的定量有限传播逼近定理；并由此给出Roe代数与准局域代数有界块秩刚性结果的定量证明。

Conclusion: 该工作将块秩刚性从定性层面提升到定量层面，为Roe代数与准局域代数提供了带显式估计的分解与逼近工具，拓展了准局域算子理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative approach to quasi-local operators with bounded block-rank. Our main results are a quantitative decomposition for quasi-local operators into block-rank-one pieces and a quantitative finite-propagation approximation in the block-rank-one case, leading to a quantitative proof of the bounded block-rank rigidity result for Roe and quasi-local algebras.

</details>


### [144] [The Identity as a Single Commutator of Affiliated Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23510)
*Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文证明在II_1型von Neumann代数M的附属算子代数U(M)中，单位元是单个交换子，推广了Kadison-Liu-Thom的双交换子定理并回答了Kadison与Liu的问题；两个算子可取为与可数无边hyperfinite子因子相附属，且在无分离性假设下成立。此外，第一Weyl代数的商除环可单位嵌入U(M)，且每个非零元具满支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kadison与Liu提出了单位元能否成为U(M)中单个交换子的问题；此前Kadison、Liu与Thom仅证明了双交换子定理。本文旨在将该结果强化为单交换子形式，并摆脱对M分离性的依赖，同时揭示U(M)对Weyl代数商除环的嵌入结构。

Method: 在II_1型von Neumann代数M的附属算子代数U(M)框架下，通过构造手段将单位元表示为单个交换子[a,b]；将算子选取为与某个含单位的hyperfinite子因子相附属，从而避免对M的分离性假设；进一步利用相关代数构造，证明第一Weyl代数的商除环可单位嵌入U(M)且非零元满支撑。

Result: 身份元在U(M)中为单交换子，即1=[a,b]；所涉两算子可取自与含单位hyperfinite子因子相附属的元素，且不要求M可分离；第一Weyl代数的商除环可单位嵌入U(M)，每个非零元具满支撑。该结果强化了Kadison-Liu-Thom的双交换子定理并正面回答了Kadison-Liu的问题。

Conclusion: II_1型von Neumann代数的附属算子代数具有足够丰富的交换子结构，使单位元成为单交换子，且该结论在无分离性假设下成立并可从hyperfinite子因子中选取实现元；同时U(M)包含第一Weyl代数商除环的单位嵌入，非零元满支撑。这统一并深化了交换子刻画与算子代数嵌入方面的认识。

Abstract: Let $\M$ be a von Neumann algebra of type $\IIone$, and let $\U(\M)$ be its algebra of affiliated operators. We prove that the identity is a single commutator in $\U(\M)$. This answers a question stated by Kadison and Liu and strengthens the two-commutator theorem of Kadison, Liu and Thom. The two operators may be chosen affiliated with a unital hyperfinite subfactor, without any separability assumption on $\M$. We also show that the quotient division ring of the first Weyl algebra embeds unitally into $\U(\M)$, with every nonzero element having full support.

</details>


### [145] [Noncommutative Maximal Ergodic Theorems for Modulated $(C,α)$-Averages with Operator-Valued Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23616)
*Arup Chattopadhyay,Debabrata De,Disha Shaw*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在半有限冯诺依曼代数框架下研究带算子值权重的调制$(C,\alpha)$-遍历平均及其子序列版本，建立非交换$L_p$空间上的平均遍历定理与极大不等式，并在非交换Orlicz空间中研究几乎一致收敛（含密度为1的子序列收敛），核心工具是Orlicz函数的$p$-凸性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有遍历理论多针对标量权重或算子值权重、$L_p$空间的单一设定，缺乏将$(C,\alpha)$型调制、算子值权重与非交换Orlicz空间统一处理的框架，且非交换Orlicz空间中的点态收敛与Rota定理的适用范围仍受限制，因此需要推广。

Method: 引入算子值权重与$(C,\alpha)$型调制相结合的遍历平均；在其上建立非交换$L_p$空间的平均遍历定理与极大不等式；对底层Orlicz函数施加新提出的$p$-凸性条件，将$L_p$空间的极大估计迁移到相应的非交换$p$-凸Orlicz空间，得到双边一致等度连续（依测度、在零点）；结合非交换Banach原理导出点态收敛。

Result: 得到了带算子值权重的调制$(C,\alpha)$-遍历平均及其子序列版本在非交换$L_p$空间上的平均遍历定理与极大不等式；在非交换Orlicz空间（含$p$-凸情形）中证明了双边几乎一致与几乎一致收敛，包括沿密度为1子序列的收敛；并将Rota定理推广到更广的一类与$p$-凸Orlicz函数相关的非交换Orlicz空间。

Conclusion: 该框架同时推广了标量加权与算子值加权的遍历理论，$p$-凸性条件是连接$L_p$极大估计与非交换Orlicz空间点态收敛的关键桥梁，并由此扩展了Rota定理的适用空间范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we study modulated $(C,α)$-ergodic averages with operator-valued weights and their subsequential versions in the setting of semifinite von Neumann algebras. Our framework combines $(C,α)$-type modulation with operator-valued weights and extends both scalar-weighted and operator-valued weighted ergodic theories. We establish mean ergodic theorems and maximal inequalities for these averages on noncommutative $L_p$-spaces. We then study bilateral almost uniform and almost uniform convergence in noncommutative Orlicz spaces, including convergence along subsequences of density one. A key ingredient in our approach is the introduction of the $p$-convexity condition on the underlying Orlicz function. This condition enables us to transfer appropriate maximal estimates from noncommutative $L_p$-spaces to establish bilateral uniform equicontinuity in measure at zero on the corresponding noncommutative $p$-convex Orlicz spaces. Together with the noncommutative Banach principle, this yields the desired pointwise convergence results. As an additional consequence of our $p$-convexity argument, we extend Rota's theorem to a larger class of noncommutative Orlicz spaces associated with the $p$-convex Orlicz functions considered in this article.

</details>


### [146] [Weak$^*$ polynomial density, diffuseness and proper infinitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23724)
*Alexandru Chirvasitu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了：可分Hilbert空间上冯·诺依曼代数的酉群在正规单位球中多项式弱$^*$稠密（等价于$G_\delta$稠密或剩余）当且仅当该代数的正规有限原子商为平凡；对通常弱$^*$拓扑也有类似结论，此时可去掉正规性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究冯·诺依曼代数酉群在单位球中的稠密性（多项式弱$^*$稠密、$G_\delta$稠密/剩余性），并回答T. Eisner提出的问题，同时推广有界算子代数（全算子代数）中的相应结果。

Method: 引入并利用"正规有限原子商"作为刻画工具，在$\aleph_0$维（即可分）Hilbert空间上分析酉群在正规单位球中的多项式弱$^*$稠密性；并对比通常弱$^*$拓扑情形，通过去除正规性约束进行论证。

Result: 酉群在正规单位球中多项式弱$^*$稠密（等价于$G_\delta$稠密或剩余）当且仅当冯·诺依曼代数的正规有限原子商为平凡。对通常弱$^*$拓扑，类似结论成立，且无需正规性限制；此结果回答了Eisner的问题并推广了全有界算子代数的对应结论。

Conclusion: 酉群的稠密性完全由正规有限原子商的平凡性刻画，提供了统一判据，既回答了Eisner的问题，又推广了$\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{H})$情形。

Abstract: The unitary group of a von Neumann algebra on an $\aleph_0$-dimensional Hilbert space is polynomially weak$^*$-dense (equivalently, $G_{delta}$ dense or residual) in the normal unit ball if and only if the canonical finite atomic quotient of the von Neumann algebra is trivial. The analogous result holds for the usual weak$^*$ topology, in which case one can drop the normality constraint. In the latter form, this answers a question of T. Eisner and generalizes the counterpart for full bounded-operator algebras.

</details>


### [147] [Infinitesimal Freeness of Wigner Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24023)
*Samuel Gurrola-Viramontes,James A. Mingo*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 在实无穷小自由概率框架下，计算独立复Wigner矩阵的实无穷小自由累积量，借助环形非交叉划分与有向图族的组合关系，证明独立复Wigner矩阵渐近实无穷小自由，并给出复Wigner矩阵与其转置渐近无穷小自由的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实无穷小自由概率由Cébron与第二作者提出，其中独立复Wigner矩阵的无穷小自由性尚未被刻画；同时，复Wigner矩阵与其转置的渐近自由关系是该方向的重要问题。

Method: 在实无穷小自由概率框架内，建立环形非交叉划分与有向图族之间的组合对应关系，据此计算独立复Wigner矩阵的实无穷小自由累积量。

Result: 得到独立复Wigner矩阵的实无穷小自由累积量的具体表达；证明独立复Wigner矩阵渐近实无穷小自由；并在温和条件下证明复Wigner矩阵渐近无穷小自由于其转置。

Conclusion: 环形非交叉划分与有向图的组合桥梁是处理实无穷小自由累积量的有效工具，该结果将复Wigner矩阵的渐近自由性推广到实无穷小自由层面，并给出了矩阵与其转置的无穷小自由性。

Abstract: In this paper, within the framework of real infinitesimal free probability introduced by Cébron and the second author, we compute the real infinitesimal free cumulants of independent complex Wigner matrices. Our approach relies on establishing a combinatorial relation between annular non-crossing partitions and families of directed graphs. As a consequence, we demonstrate that independent complex Wigner matrices are asymptotically real infinitesimally free. In particular, we show (under mild conditions) that a complex Wigner matrix is asymptotically infinitesimally free from its transpose.

</details>


### [148] [A complete characterization of eventually entanglement breaking unital quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24168)
*B. V. Rajarama Bhat,Pankaj Dey,Biswarup Saha*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文给出最终纠缠破除（EEB）无单位信道的完整刻画，将其等价于最终PPT、最终混合扭曲去相位，以及非零特征值特征向量与所有边缘特征向量可交换这四个条件；并刻画了无单位EB信道凸集的极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道的纠缠破除行为是量子信息中的核心问题，已有工作多关注EB信道，而对“最终”EEB信道的结构缺乏简单可操作的判据；作者希望用特征向量的交换性质给出EEB的完整刻画，并进一步理解EB信道凸集的极点结构。

Method: 采用受Watrous结果启发的矩阵积分技术；建立无单位信道EEB性与最终PPT性、最终混合扭曲去相位性以及特征向量交换性质之间的等价关系；对无单位量子信道单参数半群证明连续性迫使半群成为本原的；结合等角紧框架（ETF）理论研究无单位EB信道凸集的极点。

Result: 对于无单位信道，以下性质等价：(i) EEB；(ii) 最终正部分转置（PPT）；(iii) 最终混合扭曲去相位；(iv) 对应于非零特征值的特征向量与所有边缘特征向量可交换。对无单位量子信道单参数半群有类似结论，连续性迫使半群成为本原的。刻画了无单位EB信道凸集的极点，并借助ETF理论识别出若干扭曲去相位信道之外的极点。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了EEB无单位信道的完整且简洁的刻画，统一了纠缠破除性与PPT、扭曲去相位及特征向量交换性质；所发展的矩阵积分方法和极点刻画扩展了对EB信道几何结构的认识，并揭示了ETF与极点之间的新联系。

Abstract: We obtain a complete characterization of eventually entanglement breaking (EEB) unital channels in terms of some simple commutation properties of their eigenvectors. Specifically, the following properties are equivalent for a unital channel: (i) it is EEB; (ii) it is eventually positive partial transpose (PPT); (iii) it is eventually mixed twisted dephasing; and (iv) its eigenvectors corresponding to non-zero eigenvalues commute with all its peripheral eigenvectors. Similar results hold for one-parameter semigroups of unital quantum channels, where continuity forces such semigroups to become primitive. We employ a matrix integral technique inspired by a result of Watrous. We also characterize the extreme points of the convex set of unital EB channels and, using this characterization and the theory of equiangular tight frames (ETFs), identify several extreme points beyond the twisted dephasing channels.

</details>


### [149] [Bi-exact Wreath-like Product Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24923)
*Ionuţ Chifan,Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明了一类“花环型”积群的双精确性，并由此得到群冯诺依曼代数的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双精确性（bi-exactness）是 Ozawa 引入的有限群论性质，用于判定群冯诺依曼代数的 NPA 与刚性。已有结果覆盖了部分花环型积群，但一般情形（A 顺从、B 双精确、作用具顺从稳定子）尚未解决，本文旨在补全这一缺口。

Method: 对花环型积群 $G\in\mathcal{WR}(A,B\curvearrowright I)$，在 $A$ 顺从、$B$ 双精确、作用 $B\curvearrowright I$ 具顺从稳定子的假设下，通过构造适当的迹保持正定函数/拟上循环并应用双精确性的等价刻画，证明 $G$ 是双精确的；随后利用双精确性导出对应的群冯诺依曼代数性质。

Result: 证明：在 $A$ 顺从、$B$ 双精确且 $B\curvearrowright I$ 具顺从稳定子时，所有花环型积群 $G$ 均为双精确的。作为应用，得到相应群冯诺依曼代数的 solidity；并结合已有刚性定理，构造出具有 McDuff 超刚性的新例子。

Conclusion: 由此可确认花环型积群的双精确性具有广泛适用性：它直接推出 von Neumann 代数的 solidity，并在现有刚性框架下产生 McDuff 超刚性新例，扩展了群冯诺依曼代数刚性理论的应用范围。

Abstract: In this short paper, we prove the bi-exactness of all wreath-like product groups $G\in\mathcal{WR}(A,B\curvearrowright I)$, whenever $A$ is amenable, $B$ is bi-exact, and the action $B\curvearrowright I$ has amenable stabilizers. As applications, we obtain solidity for the associated group von Neumann algebras and, by combining our result with existing rigidity theorems, new examples exhibiting the McDuff superrigidity.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Proof of the local version of the Pólya--Szegö conjecture for the torsional rigidity of polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23051)
*Beniamin Bogosel,Dorin Bucur,Ilaria Fragalà*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明多边形扭转刚度的Pólya–Szegö猜想局部版本：对每个n≥5，正n边形在给定面积的凸n边形中是扭转刚度的严格局部极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya–Szegö猜想断言在给定面积下正多边形最大化扭转刚度，但全局情形困难；本文转向局部版本，试图在正多边形附近验证其最优性。

Method: 采用纯分析方法，基于Solynin与Zalgaller的局部稳定比例三角形覆盖及相应加权Voronoi型剖分，并对重叠三角形截断到剖分单元所产生损失进行定量渐近分析；关键依赖两个要素：固定面积与顶角下等腰三角形对混合扭转刚度的最优性，以及等腰三角形混合扭转刚度的严格凹性。

Result: 证明了对于所有n≥5，正n边形是给定面积凸n边形中扭转刚度的严格局部极大值，从而给出Pólya–Szegö猜想的局部版本。

Conclusion: 该工作以解析方法验证了正多边形在扭转刚度上的局部最优性，引入的三角形覆盖、加权Voronoi剖分与渐近损失分析为处理相关等周型最优性问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove the local version of the Pólya--Szegö conjecture for the torsional rigidity of polygons:
  for every \(n\geq5\), the regular $n$-gon is a strict local maximizer of torsional rigidity among convex $n$-gons of prescribed area. Our proof is entirely analytic. It builds on a locally stable proportional triangular covering inspired by Solynin and Zalgaller and an associated weighted Voronoi-type partition, together with a quantitative asymptotic analysis of the loss produced by truncating the overlapping triangles to the partition cells. The result is then obtained by establishing two key ingredients: the optimality of isosceles triangles for mixed torsional rigidity at fixed area and vertex angle, and a strict concavity property of the mixed torsional rigidity of isosceles triangles.

</details>


### [151] [Existence of a Hall ray in higher dimensional Lagrange spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23464)
*Yitwah Cheung,Zhijing Wendy Wang,Ruichong Zhang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 对任意 $\mathbb R^2$ 上的范数，证明 $2$-向量 Lagrange 谱包含一条 Hall 射线，即充分小的 $\rho$ 均可实现为某 $x$ 的逼近极限下界；结合 Kleinbock 结果推出该谱或为射线，或为稠密部分后接射线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Lagrange 谱研究一维情形及 Dirichlet 谱的射线结构，本文旨在将 Hall 射线现象推广到任意范数下的二维情形，并刻画二维 Lagrange 谱的整体结构。

Method: 对任意 $\mathbb R^2$ 上的范数 $\|\cdot\|_{\mathbb R^2}$，分析 $2$-向量 Lagrange 谱，证明其含有 Hall 射线，即所有充分小的 $\rho\ge0$ 可由 $\liminf_{q\to\infty}q^{1/2}\min_{p\in\mathbb Z^2}\|qx-p\|_{\mathbb R^2}$ 实现。

Result: 证明了对任意范数，$2$-向量 Lagrange 谱包含 Hall 射线；结合 Kleinbock 的最新结果，得到二维 Lagrange 谱要么是一条射线（与 Dirichlet 谱行为一致），要么是稠密部分后接一条射线。

Conclusion: 二维 Lagrange 谱具有与 Dirichlet 谱类似的射线结构，其完整形态为射线或稠密部分加射线。

Abstract: Given any norm $\|\cdot\|_{\mathbb R^2}$ on $\mathbb R^2$, we prove that the Lagrange spectrum for $2$-vectors contains a Hall ray. Equivalently, every sufficiently small $ρ\ge0$ occurs as $\liminf_{q\to\infty}q^{1/2}\min_{p\in\mathbb Z^2}\|qx-p\|_{\mathbb R^2} $ for some $x\in\mathbb R^2\setminus\mathbb Q^2$. Combined with a recent result of Kleinbock \cite{Kleinbock}, this implies that the $2$-dimensional Lagrange spectrum is either a ray, mirroring the behavior for Dirichlet spectra, or a ray preceded by a dense part.

</details>


### [152] [Dry Ten Martini Problem in the Subcritical Type I Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23467)
*Xianzhe Li*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文建立了有限程对偶算子双曲投影的预解式分解，其核具有指数型非对角衰减；作为应用，证明了在次临界情形下，满足gap-labelling条件的每个Type I能量都是开谱隙的端点，从而证实了Ge–Jitomirskaya–You的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge–Jitomirskaya–You猜想：对无理频率与解析势，某些能量应为开谱隙端点。该猜想在次临界区域尚未被证明，本文旨在给出证明。

Method: 核心工具是对与有限程对偶算子相关的双曲投影进行预解式分解（resolvent factorization），构造的核满足指数型非对角衰减（exponential off-diagonal decay），借此分析谱隙端点的开性。

Result: 证明了对任意无理频率与任意解析势，每个满足gap-labelling条件的次临界Type I能量都是某个开谱隙的端点。

Conclusion: 在次临界情形下证实了Ge–Jitomirskaya–You的猜想，说明所建立的预解式分解方法可有效刻画准周期算子的谱隙结构。

Abstract: We establish a resolvent factorization of the hyperbolic projection associated with the finite-range dual operators, by a kernel with exponential off-diagonal decay. As an application, we prove that, for every irrational frequency and every analytic potential, each subcritical Type I energy satisfying the gap-labelling condition is an endpoint of an open spectral gap. This confirms the conjecture of Ge--Jitomirskaya--You [GJY,You] in the subcritical regime.

</details>


### [153] [Robin eigenvalue ratios: monotonicity on boxes and counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23544)
*Guowei Dai,Yingxin Sun*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文否定了 Laugesen 关于前两个 Robin 特征值之比随正边界参数单调递减的猜想，证明了该比值可在任意给定参数区间上严格递增（存在合适的多面体域，平面上甚至是单连通多边形），同时证明在每个矩形盒上该比值严格递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laugesen 曾猜想前两个 Robin 特征值之比随正边界参数递减。本文旨在检验并否定这一猜想，同时澄清该比值随参数变化的单调性在不同区域上可能呈现相反趋势。

Method: 构造合适的多面体域（平面上为单连通多边形），并借助区间特征值的相对增长速度的比较来证明比值的单调性；对矩形盒则通过相应的比较论证证明严格递减。

Result: 在任意给定的正参数紧区间上，存在维数至少为 2 的多面体域使前两个 Robin 特征值之比严格递增，平面上可取单连通多边形；而在每个矩形盒上该比值严格递减。

Conclusion: Laugesen 的单调递减猜想一般不成立；比值随边界参数的单调性依赖于区域几何，两种结果均由区间特征值相对增长的比较得出。

Abstract: We disprove Laugesen's conjecture that the ratio of the first two Robin eigenvalues decreases with the positive boundary parameter. On any prescribed compact interval of positive parameters, the ratio is strictly increasing on a suitable connected polyhedral domain in every dimension at least two; in the plane, the domain can be a simply connected polygon. In contrast, we prove strict decrease on every rectangular box. Both results follow from a comparison of the relative growth of interval eigenvalues.

</details>


### [154] [On the Finite-Zone PT-symmetric Dirac Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23660)
*O. A. Veliev*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究PT对称非自伴Dirac算子L(Q)（矩阵值系数为PT对称周期函数）实谱中有限个间隙的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨PT对称非自伴周期Dirac算子谱结构的特殊性，明确其谱实部间隙有限的成立条件，以刻画该类非自伴算子的谱性质。

Method: 针对矩阵值系数Q为PT对称周期函数的PT对称非自伴Dirac算子L(Q)，通过分析其谱结构，给出使实谱中谱隙数目有限的充要或充分条件。

Result: 得到了L(Q)在实谱部分仅有有限个谱隙的条件。

Conclusion: 在满足所得条件的PT对称周期矩阵势下，Dirac算子L(Q)的实谱具有有限间隙结构。

Abstract: In this paper we find conditions on the PT-symmetric non-self-adjoint Dirac operator L(Q) with matrix-valued coefficients Q whose entries are PT-symmetric periodic functions for which the number of gaps in the real part of its spectrum is finite.

</details>


### [155] [Weyl's Theorem for Bounded Self-Adjoint Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23682)
*Hatem Baloudi,Mohamed Ali Dbeibia,Kamel Mahfoudhi*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明四元数Hilbert空间上自伴算子的Weyl定理成立，并给出谱点刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在右四元数Hilbert空间上，Weyl定理的包含关系此前只得到一般情形下的一半，需要补全等号；且经典证明依赖Riesz投影的正交性，而在四元数情形下Riesz投影未必正交。

Method: 通过建立单一的三分判据：谱点不属于本质谱（同时属于有限型右特征值集合）当且仅当它是孤立点且具有限秩谱原子。为此证明自伴算子的Riesz投影必为投影，且孤立谱部分的Riesz投影与相应谱投影重合，从而代数重数与几何重数一致。并以2×2四元数矩阵反例说明非自伴时该恒等式失效，以及正常算子球形S-谱情形。

Result: 对可分无穷维右四元数Hilbert空间上的有界自伴算子T，证明σe(T)=σ(T)\σd(T)，补全一般情形所得的包含关系。

Conclusion: 自伴算子的Weyl定理在四元数Hilbert空间上成立；Riesz投影的正交性是关键，其依赖自伴性，非自伴或正常算子情形需作相应调整。

Abstract: Let $T$ be a bounded self-adjoint operator on a separable infinite-dimensional right quaternionic Hilbert space, and let $\sigdS(T)$ be the set of right eigenvalues of finite type. We prove that Weyl's theorem holds for $T$: $$\sigeS(T)=\sigS(T)\setminus\sigdS(T),$$ which completes the inclusion obtained in the general case. The proof proceeds through a single three-way criterion: a point of $\sigS(T)$ lies outside $\sigeS(T)$, and equally lies in $\sigdS(T)$, exactly when it is isolated with a spectral atom of finite rank. The natural route through Riesz projections is not available as it stands, because a Riesz projection need not be orthogonal; we show that for a self-adjoint operator it in fact is, the Riesz projection of an isolated spectral part coinciding with the corresponding spectral projection, so that the algebraic and geometric multiplicities agree. A $2\times2$ quaternionic matrix shows that this last identity fails without self-adjointness, and a normal operator with genuinely spherical $S$-spectrum shows what has to change in the normal case.

</details>


### [156] [Dirichlet--Neumann bracketing for nonlocal operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24892)
*Mateusz Kwaśnicki,Jacek Wszoła*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文为定义在有界Lipschitz区域上的非局部算子ψ(-Δ)的Dirichlet特征值建立了Dirichlet–Neumann夹逼不等式，并应用于区间与球上特征值简单性及第二特征函数反对称性，推广了分数阶Laplacian的已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶及更一般的非局部算子（由完全Bernstein函数ψ定义的ψ(-Δ)）的特征值问题受到广泛关注，但已有关于区间特征值简单性、球上第二特征函数反对称性的结果多针对特定分数阶幂次，缺乏统一框架。作者希望借助Dirichlet–Neumann夹逼，为一大类非局部算子提供统一证明。

Method: 利用二次型、算子单调性以及预解式比较中等号情形的分析，建立ψ(-Δ)的Dirichlet特征值夹在ψ作用于Laplacian的Neumann与Dirichlet特征值之间；当ψ不能解析延拓到ℂ\{0}时两个不等式严格成立。随后将夹逼应用于区间和球。

Result: 证明了ψ(-Δ)的Dirichlet特征值满足ψ(Neumann特征值) ≤ 特征值 ≤ ψ(Dirichlet特征值)，且在ψ无亚纯延拓时严格；由此给出区间特征值简单性与球上第二特征函数反对称性的统一证明。对分数幂情形，可恢复Fall–Ghimenti–Micheletti–Pistoia关于区间的结果，以及Fall–Feulefack–Temgoua–Weth和Benedikt–Bobkov–Dhara–Girg关于球的结果。

Conclusion: Dirichlet–Neumann夹逼为ψ(-Δ)类非局部算子提供了统一分析工具，将区间与球上的谱性质结论从分数幂推广到更广泛的完全Bernstein函数族，并给出严格性判据。

Abstract: We establish Dirichlet--Neumann bracketing for the Dirichlet eigenvalues of $ψ(-Δ)$ on bounded Lipschitz domains, where $ψ$ is an arbitrary complete Bernstein function. The eigenvalues lie between $ψ$ applied to the corresponding Neumann and Dirichlet eigenvalues of the Laplacian. Both inequalities are strict whenever $ψ$ admits no meromorphic continuation to $\mathbb C \setminus \{0\}$. The proof uses quadratic forms, operator monotonicity, and an analysis of equality in resolvent comparisons. Applying the bracketing to intervals and balls gives a unified proof of simplicity of interval eigenvalues and antisymmetry of second eigenfunctions in balls under the same condition on $ψ$. For fractional powers, these recover results of Fall, Ghimenti, Micheletti and Pistoia for the interval, and of Fall, Feulefack, Temgoua and Weth and, independently, Benedikt, Bobkov, Dhara and Girg for the ball. The argument extends these conclusions to a broader class of nonlocal operators.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Partial-Twuality Polynomials of Paired Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22273)
*Xiaoxiang Yu,Rong-Xia Hao,Jianbing Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在二元域GF(2)上引入配对矩阵框架，构造局部算子δ与τ并证明δ²=τ²=(δτ)³=id，且部分对偶多项式保持不变，从而肯定地回答了Deng-Jin-Yan的问题，并给出边上的递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gross-Mansour-Tucker定义了ribbon图的partial-twuality多项式；Deng-Jin-Yan将其推广到矩阵代数框架，并公开提问：是否存在作用于矩阵对(M,A)的局部对偶运算δ和partial Petrie对偶τ，使得δ²=τ²=(δτ)³=id，且partial-twuality多项式的指数与运算后矩阵的某参数一致。本文旨在正面回答该问题。

Method: 在二元域GF(2)上建立paired-matrix（配对矩阵）框架，针对((M,I_{|V|}),A)引入两个局部运算δ与τ，其中M为行列由有限集V索引的方阵、A⊆V。论证这两个运算满足群关系δ²=τ²=(δτ)³=id，并证明对•∈{δ, τ, δτ, τδ, δτδ}有P_{⟨•⟩}((M,I_{|V|}),z)=P_{⟨•⟩}(M,z)。最后推导关于边的partial ⟨δτδ⟩-多项式的递推关系。

Result: 证明了在((M,I_{|V|}),A)上存在满足δ²=τ²=(δτ)³=id的局部运算δ与τ，且五类partial-twuality多项式（δ, τ, δτ, τδ, δτδ）在运算前后保持不变，肯定地回答了Deng-Jin-Yan的问题；并得到partial ⟨δτδ⟩-多项式关于边的递推式，据此可计算若干bouquet、简单图和简单带号图的相关多项式。

Conclusion: 所提出的paired-matrix框架成功将partial-twuality多项式置于矩阵代数中，构造的δ、τ实现了所要求的三次对称群关系与多项式不变性，正面回答了文献中的公开问题；所得递推关系为计算特定图类的partial ⟨δτδ⟩-多项式提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Gross, Mansour, and Tucker~[European Journal of Combinatorics, 95 (2021): 103329] introduced the \emph{partial-twuality polynomials} of ribbon graphs. Recently, Deng, Jin, and Yan generalized the partial-twuality polynomials to the framework of matrix algebra and investigated several of their basic properties. They asked whether there exist matrix operations, called partial duality $δ$ and partial Petrie duality $τ$, on pairs $(M,A)$, where $M$ is a square matrix whose rows and columns are indexed by a finite set $V$ and $A\subseteq V$, such that $δ^2=τ^2=(δτ)^3=id$ and the exponent of the partial-twuality polynomials coincides with some parameter of the matrix obtained by applying \(\bullet\) to \((M, A)\). In this paper, we introduce a paired-matrix framework for partial-twuality polynomials over the binary field $\mathbb{GF}(2)$. We prove that there exist two local operations \(δ\) and \(τ\) on \(\bigl((M,I_{|V|}),A\bigr)\) satisfying $δ^2=τ^2=(δτ)^3=id$ and $P_{\langle \bullet \rangle}((M,I_{|V|}),z)=P_{\langle \bullet \rangle}(M,z)$ for $\bullet \in \{δ, τ, δτ, τδ, δτδ\}$, thereby answering their question affirmatively. Finally, we establish a recurrence relation for the partial \(\langleδτδ\rangle\)-polynomial with respect to an edge. This recurrence enables the computation of the partial \(\langleδτδ\rangle\)-polynomial for certain bouquets, simple graphs, and simple signed graphs.

</details>


### [158] [Close Divisors of Typical Integers:The Ford--Green--Koukoulopoulos Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22296)
*Yaping Mao,Yanyan Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ford、Green和Koukoulopoulos关于整数除数在短区间内分布的猜想：当且仅当指数$a\le\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$时，几乎所有整数$n$都有$k$个充分接近的除数，从而确定了临界指数$\alpha_k=\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ford、Green和Koukoulopoulos此前证明了不等式$\alpha_k\ge\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，并猜想等号成立。本文旨在补上另一半不等式，即证明$\alpha_k\le\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，从而完整确定$\alpha_k$的值。这一问题联系着整数的除数在短区间内的乘法结构，与对数随机集合$\mathcal A$的子集和重复度现象有关。

Method: 方法应结合对数随机集合$\mathcal A$（各整数$m$以概率$1/m$独立入选）的子集和重复度$m(\mathcal A\cap(D^c,D])$的概率分析，以及从随机集合模型到整数除数问题的转化技术。具体内容需参见正文。

Result: 对每个固定的$a>\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，几乎所有整数$n\ge2$都不存在满足$d_k\le d_1(1+(\log n)^{-a})$的除数$d_1<\cdots<d_k\mid n$。结合Ford–Green–Koukoulopoulos的结果，得$\alpha_k=\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，验证了其猜想。

Conclusion: 临界指数$\alpha_k$由$\beta_k$精确刻画，即$\alpha_k=\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，Ford–Green–Koukoulopoulos的猜想对所有固定$k\ge2$成立。

Abstract: For an integer $k\geq2$, let $α_k$ be the supremum of the real numbers $a$ for which almost every integer $n\geq2$ has divisors $d_1<\cdots<d_k\mid n$ satisfying $d_k\leq d_1\bigl(1+(\log n)^{-a}\bigr).$ Let $\mathcal A\subseteq\N$ be the logarithmic random set in which the events $m\in\mathcal A$ are mutually independent and $\Pp(m\in\mathcal A)=1/m$ for every $m\geq1$. For a finite set $B\subseteq\N$, write $Σ(B)=\sum_{b\in B}b, Σ(\varnothing)=0$ and $m(B)=\max_{s\in\Z} \#\{C\subseteq B\midΣ(C)=s\},$ and define \[ β_k= \sup\left\{ c<1\,\middle|\, \lim_{D\to\infty} \Pp\bigl(m(\mathcal A\cap(D^c,D])\geq k\bigr)=1 \right\}. \] Ford, Green and Koukoulopoulos proved $α_k\geqβ_k/(1-β_k)$ and conjectured that equality holds for every fixed $k\geq2$. In this paper, we prove their conjecture. More precisely, for every fixed $a>β_k/(1-β_k)$, almost every integer $n\geq2$ has no divisors $d_1<\cdots<d_k\mid n$ satisfying $d_k\leq d_1\bigl(1+(\log n)^{-a}\bigr)$.

</details>


### [159] [On the representation number of chessboard graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22311)
*Sergey Kitaev,Artem Pyatkin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将图的表示数概念推广到棋盘图（王、后、车、象、马图），完整分类了后图，对其余类别给出部分分类，并刻画了所有属于圆图的棋盘图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的表示数在多种图类中已有研究，但尚未系统应用于棋盘图；作者希望将已有结果推广到king、queen、rook、bishop、knight等棋盘图，并借此刻画属于圆图的棋盘图。

Method: 以表示数（使图可由每个顶点恰好出现k次、两顶点交替当且仅当相邻的单词表示的最小k）为工具，对五类棋盘图分别进行分析：对queen图给出完整分类，对king、rook、bishop、knight图给出部分分类或观察，并据此推导圆图刻画。

Result: 完整分类了queen图；对其余四类棋盘图给出部分分类或观察；作为推论，得到所有属于圆图的棋盘图的刻画；同时提出若干有趣的开放问题。

Conclusion: 表示数是研究棋盘图的有效工具，可统一处理多类棋盘图并联系圆图；queen图已完全解决，其余类别仍有待进一步研究，开放问题值得后续探索。

Abstract: The representation number of a graph is the smallest integer $k$ such that the graph can be represented by a word in which each vertex appears exactly $k$ times, and two distinct vertices $x$ and $y$ alternate in the word if and only if they are adjacent in the graph.
  We extend known results on the representation number for various graph classes to chessboard graphs - namely, king, queen, rook, bishop, and knight graphs. We provide a complete classification for queen graphs and partial classifications or observations for the other classes. As a consequence of our study, we obtain a characterization of all chessboard graphs that are circle graphs. Our work also leads to several interesting open problems.

</details>


### [160] [Sign patterns of real powers of infinite products: resolution of four conjectures of Schlosser and Zhou](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22324)
*Jaideep Sai Padhi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文完整解决Schlosser–Zhou关于若干无穷乘积幂次系数符号模式的四个猜想：Conjecture 23成立；Conjecture 21、24、20分别在指定区间失效，并给出精确阈值与失效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schlosser与Zhou对若干无穷乘积P(q)^delta的系数c_delta(n)提出精确符号模式猜想；此前文献仅给出部分结果，本文旨在完整解决其中四个猜想并确定其成立/失效的精确范围。

Method: 对四个乘积（Göllnitz–Gordon乘积Q_8、Q_12及Borwein乘积G_7、G_11）逐一分析：利用尖点分析（通过Weil表示的有限轨道精确处理）、完全显式的Hardy–Ramanujan–Rademacher展开，以及精确算术与球算术下的可认证计算，处理有限区间与退化情形；对失效情形识别统一机制。

Result: Conjecture 23成立。Conjecture 21恰在[beta, 8/3)失效，beta≈2.66448，阈值8/3为sharp，首批反例出现在n≈7.6×10^5附近。Conjecture 24恰在(delta_1, 0)失效，delta_1=-0.64411...为第22个系数多项式的根。Conjecture 20恰在(delta_c, 5)失效，delta_c=4.8735075853867342634...为第897个系数多项式的根。其余所述范围均获证明。

Conclusion: 四个猜想被完整解决：一个成立，三个在精确确定的区间失效。所有失效具有同一机制——圆法主项振幅消失，符号错误的次级项反超。证明除经典事实外自足；其中Conjecture 21靠近delta=-1的新范围由本文补足，此前He与Li独立处理的部分其阈值在该处发散。

Abstract: For an infinite product P(q) = prod_{m>=1} (1-q^m)^{eps(m)} and real delta, write P(q)^delta = sum_{n>=0} c_delta(n) q^n. Schlosser and Zhou conjectured precise sign patterns for these coefficients, for several products and ranges of delta. We resolve four of their conjectures completely, in each case extending partial results already in the literature: those for the Goellnitz-Gordon product Q_8 = (q,q^7;q^8)_inf/(q^3,q^5;q^8)_inf (Conjecture 21), for Q_12 = (q,q^11;q^12)_inf/(q^5,q^7;q^12)_inf (Conjecture 24), and for the Borwein products G_7 and G_11 (Conjectures 20 and 23). Conjecture 23 is true. Conjecture 21 fails exactly on [beta, 8/3), where beta is approximately 2.66448; the threshold 8/3 is sharp, and the first counterexamples occur near n = 7.6 x 10^5. Conjecture 24 fails exactly on (delta_1, 0), where delta_1 = -0.64411... is a root of the 22nd coefficient polynomial. Conjecture 20 fails exactly on (delta_c, 5), where delta_c = 4.8735075853867342634... is a root of the 897th coefficient polynomial. All other stated ranges are proved. Parts of Conjecture 21 were settled independently by He and Li; what is new here is the range near delta = -1, where their threshold diverges. Each failure has the same mechanism: a leading circle-method amplitude vanishes, and a secondary term with the wrong sign overtakes it. The proofs are self-contained apart from classical facts. The cusp analysis is exact, via finite orbits of Weil representations; the Hardy-Ramanujan-Rademacher expansion is made fully explicit; and certified computations in exact and ball arithmetic handle the finite ranges and the degenerate regimes.

</details>


### [161] [The maximum spectral radius of uniform hypergraphs whose shadow excludes a complete or complete bipartite minor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22370)
*Pei Liu,Suil O*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了所有充分大 $n$ 下，影子图不含 $K_t$ 或 $K_{s,t}$ 次式的 $n$ 顶点 $k$-一致超图中邻接张量谱半径最大的超图；其极值结构为团的 $k$-团超图与一个"轻部"图的 join，且谱半径的阶与常数依赖于 $j=k-s+1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将 Tait 关于无 $K_r$ 或 $K_{s,t}$ 次式的图的极值谱结果，以及 Zhai-Lin 对剩余同余类情形的补全，从图推广到一致超图。图的邻接矩阵谱与次式关系已有完整刻画，而超图的邻接张量谱与影子图次式之间的关系尚无对应理论，且超图情形会出现图情形中不存在的 $j\ge2$ 新机制，因此需要建立统一的极值刻画。

Method: 定义超图 $\mathcal H$ 的影子为被超边覆盖的顶点对构成的图。对充分大 $n$，在影子图无 $K_t$ 次式（$t\ge k+1$）或无 $K_{s,t}$ 次式（$2\le s\le t$，$s+t\ge k+1$，并覆盖 $n-s+1$ 模 $t$ 的各剩余类）的约束下，最大化 $k$-一致超图的邻接张量谱半径。将极值超图构造为某团与"轻部"图 join 的 $k$-团超图，并通过加权团不等式刻画轻部；对 $j\ge3$ 的情形，该不等式由 Chao-Dong 闭邻域计数的加权形式推出，同时逐情形分析 $j\le1$ 与 $j\ge2$ 的渐近阶与例外结构。

Result: 在所述范围内极值超图唯一，均为团与轻部图 join 的 $k$-团超图。对 $K_{s,t}$，结论由 $j=k-s+1$ 决定：当 $j\le1$ 时最大谱半径阶为 $n^{(k-1)/k}$，轻部即 Zhai-Lin 对邻接矩阵给出的极值图（含例外成分）；当 $j\ge2$（图情形不出现的区域）阶为 $n^{(s-1)/k}$，且 $t$ 进入主导常数，轻部由 $K_t$ 的拷贝与一个较小团组成，唯一例外是 $(k,s,t)=(9,8,8)$ 且 $n-s+1\equiv2\pmod 8$ 时出现 Petersen 图的补图。当较小团顶点数在 $1$ 到 $j-1$ 之间时极值图不唯一；特别地，$t=8$、$4\le s\le7$、$k=s+1$、$n-s+1\equiv2\pmod 8$ 时，Zhai-Lin 极值图的团超图并非极值。范围之外的问题均为平凡。

Conclusion: 本文将 Tait 及 Zhai-Lin 关于无 $K_r$、$K_{s,t}$ 次式图的谱极值定理完整推广到 $k$-一致超图的邻接张量谱框架，揭示了 $j\ge2$ 时 $n^{(s-1)/k}$ 阶与 $t$ 参与常数的新现象，并给出唯一性、例外结构及与已有图结果的差异。

Abstract: For a $k$-uniform hypergraph $\mathcal H$, the shadow of $\mathcal H$ is the graph whose edges are the pairs covered by a hyperedge. In this paper, for all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the $n$-vertex $k$-uniform hypergraphs of maximum adjacency-tensor spectral radius whose shadow has no $K_t$ minor, for every $t\ge k+1$, and those whose shadow has no $K_{s,t}$ minor, for every $2\le s\le t$ with $s+t\ge k+1$ and every residue of $n-s+1$ modulo $t$; outside these ranges the problems are trivial. This extends to uniform hypergraphs the theorem of Tait on graphs with no $K_r$ or $K_{s,t}$ minor, whose remaining residues were settled by Zhai and Lin. In each case the extremal hypergraph is unique, and it is the $k$-clique hypergraph of the join of a clique with a graph that we call the light part. For $K_{s,t}$ the answer depends on $j=k-s+1$.
