When $j\le1$, the maximum has order $n^{(k-1)/k}$, and the light part is the one found by Zhai and Lin for the adjacency matrix, including its exceptional components. When $j\ge2$, a regime that does not occur for graphs, the maximum has order $n^{(s-1)/k}$ and $t$ enters its leading constant. The light part then consists of copies of $K_t$ and one smaller clique, with a single exception: for $(k,s,t)=(9,8,8)$ and $n-s+1\equiv2\pmod 8$, the complement of the Petersen graph appears. When the smaller clique has between $1$ and $j-1$ vertices, the extremal graph is not unique. In particular, for $t=8$, $4\le s\le7$, $k=s+1$ and $n-s+1\equiv2\pmod 8$, the clique hypergraph of the extremal graph of Zhai and Lin is not extremal. For $j\ge2$ the light part is determined by a weighted clique inequality, which for $j\ge3$ follows from a weighted form of the closed-neighborhood counting of Chao and Dong.

</details>


### [162] [The Three-Dimensional Erdős Box Problem Has Exponent $11/4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22381)
*Dean Menezes*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了3-一致超图的Zarankiewicz问题：构造无"盒子"（$K_{2,2,2}^{(3)}$）的三部超图，匹配Erdős的$O(n^{11/4})$上界，证明$z(n)=\Theta(n^{11/4})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3-一致超图Zarankiewicz问题寻求每部$n$个顶点且不含$K_{2,2,2}^{(3)}$的三部超图的最大边数$z(n)$。Erdős（1964）证明上界$O(n^{11/4})$，此前最优下界仅为Katz、Krop与Maggioni（2002）的$\Omega(n^{8/3})$，上下界之间存在$n^{1/12}$量级的差距，长期未解决。本文旨在封闭这一差距。

Method: 代数构造：对每个$q=2^m$（$m\geq1$），在${\bf F}_{q^3}$上通过幂映射$\tau(s)=s^{q^2-q+1}$定义超图，每部$q^4$个顶点、共$q^{11}$条边。证明核心是$\tau$的"方向-1"有限差分将${\bf F}_{q^3}$划分为${\bf F}_q$上两两斜交（pairwise skew）的仿射线，从而阻止盒子形成。

Result: 构造出一族无盒子超图，每部$q^4$个顶点、$q^{11}$条边，即边数满足$z(n)=\Omega(n^{11/4})$，与Erdős上界匹配，得到$z(n)=\Theta(n^{11/4})$。其中$q^{11}=(q^4)^{11/4}=n^{11/4}$，恰好达到渐近阶。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底确定$z(n)=\Theta(n^{11/4})$，解决了Erdős（1964）提出的问题，并显著改进Katz–Krop–Maggioni保持二十余年的$\Omega(n^{8/3})$下界。

Abstract: The Zarankiewicz problem for $3$-uniform hypergraphs asks for the maximum number $z(n)$ of edges in a tripartite hypergraph with $n$ vertices in each part containing no copy of $K_{2,2,2}^{(3)}$ (a ``box''). Erdős (1964) proved $z(n) = O(n^{11/4})$. The best previously known lower bound was $Ω(n^{8/3})$, due to Katz, Krop, and Maggioni (2002). We construct a family of box-free hypergraphs matching Erdős's upper bound: for each $q = 2^m$ ($m \geq 1$), our hypergraph has $q^4$ vertices in each part and $q^{11}$ edges, establishing that $z(n) = Θ(n^{11/4})$. The construction is algebraic, defined over ${\bf F}_{q^3}$ via the power map $τ(s) = s^{q^2-q+1}$. The proof shows that the direction-$1$ finite differences of $τ$ partition ${\bf F}_{q^3}$ into pairwise skew affine lines over ${\bf F}_q$, preventing boxes from forming.

</details>


### [163] [Counting $t$-wise $L$-intersecting cliques with prescribed intersection sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22393)
*Yiyan Zhan,Yichen Wang,Mei Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究 $r$-团构成 $t$-wise $L$-相交族时最大 $r$-团数 $\Psi_r(n,L,t)$ 的渐近行为：当 $\ell_1,\dots,\ell_s,r$ 非等差数列时为 $o(n^{|L|})$，为等差数列时给出渐近公式，推广了 Helliar–Liu 的 $t=2$ 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将关于相交族（$t=2$）的极值结果推广到 $t$-wise $L$-相交情形，刻画 $r$-团族在约束条件下的最大规模。

Method: 组合极值方法，分析序列 $\{\ell_1,\dots,\ell_s,r\}$ 是否构成等差数列并进行分类讨论，推导上界与匹配构造。

Result: 若 $\{\ell_1,\dots,\ell_s,r\}$ 非等差数列，则 $\Psi_r(n,L,t)=o(n^{|L|})$；若为等差数列，给出 $\Psi_r(n,L,t)$ 的渐近公式。$t=2$ 时退化到 Helliar 与 Liu 的结论。

Conclusion: $\Psi_r(n,L,t)$ 的阶由序列 $\{\ell_1,\dots,\ell_s,r\}$ 的等差性质决定，结果统一并推广了 $t=2$ 的已知定理。

Abstract: Let $r,t\ge 3$ be integers and $L=\{\ell_1,\ell_2,\ldots,\ell_s\}\subseteq [0,r-1]$ a fixed set of integers with $|L|\neq r$ and $\ell_1<\ell_2<\cdots<\ell_s$. For each integer $n$, let $Ψ_{r}(n,L,t)$ be the maximum number of $r$-cliques in an $n$-vertex graph whose $r$-cliques, viewed as a family of $r$-subsets of the vertex set, form a $t$-wise $L$-intersecting family. In this paper, we prove that $Ψ_{r}(n,L,t)=o(n^{|L|})$ when the sequence $\ell_1,\ell_2,\ldots,\ell_s,r$ does not form an arithmetic progression, and we give an asymptotic formula for $Ψ_{r}(n,L,t)$ when this sequence does form an arithmetic progression. When $t=2$, our results are exactly Helliar and Liu's results.

</details>


### [164] [Gaussian Vertex-Face Balance in Random Convex Polyhedra with Fixed Edge Count](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22402)
*Lachlan Bridges*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究了固定边数凸三维多面体组合类型的顶点数分布，证明了标准化顶点数满足中心极限定理，方差约为 e/32，并给出局部极限定理、中偏差与大偏差等精细渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Rüdinger 提出的关于固定边数凸三维多面体顶点数极限分布的问题。

Method: 基于 Bender 和 Wormald 的经典有根枚举与不对称性结果，推导相对格点局部极限定理及速率函数修正。

Result: 得到 sqrt(e)(β_e-1/2) 收敛到 N(0,1/32)，即 Var(V_e)~e/32；在 o(e^{3/4}) 窗口上有相对格点局部极限定理，o(e^{5/6}) 窗口上有四次修正，给出中尾常数、二次中偏差原理及完整 speed-e 大偏差原理；所有固定标准化矩收敛；e=3m 时两个极值顶点数概率渐近为 (6561/(32√2))(4/27)^m；有根与无根固定边数分布相对差异为 O(ρ^e)。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了固定边数凸三维多面体顶点数的渐近分布，回答了 Rüdinger 的问题并给出多种精细极限定理。

Abstract: Choose uniformly among the combinatorial types of convex three-dimensional polyhedra with a fixed admissible number $e$ of edges, and let $V_e$ be the number of vertices. This resolves a fixed-edge limit-distribution question posed by Rüdinger: if $β_e=V_e/(e+2)$, then $\sqrt e(β_e-1/2)\Rightarrow N(0,1/32)$, equivalently $\operatorname{Var}(V_e)\sim e/32$. Using the classical rooted enumeration and asymmetry results of Bender and Wormald, we derive a relative lattice local limit theorem on every $o(e^{3/4})$ window, a quartic correction from the rate function on every $o(e^{5/6})$ window, precise moderate-tail constants, a quadratic moderate-deviation principle for every $a_e\to\infty$ with $a_e=o(\sqrt e)$, and a full speed-$e$ large-deviation principle with an explicit good rate function. All fixed standardised moments converge. When $e=3m$, the two extremal vertex counts have equal probability asymptotic to $\frac{6561}{32\sqrt2}(4/27)^m$. Rooted and unrooted fixed-edge laws are also uniformly exponentially close, with relative discrepancy $O(ρ^e)$.

</details>


### [165] [Tree Bricks and Finite Tree Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22406)
*Annoy Sengupta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文用有限根树编码零关系代数上的Crawley–Boevey树模，构造确定性自底向上树自动机识别这些编码，并刻画brick性质与局部染色压缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究零关系代数上Crawley–Boevey树模的组合与自动机刻画，期望用树语言/树自动机工具描述brick（不可分解且自同态环为除环的模），并探究箭图字母表能否压缩。

Method: 设Λ=KQ/I为零关系代数。用Q的箭头及其形式逆标号有限根树来编码树模；构造确定性有限自底向上树自动机识别这些编码；定义automata-induced tree brick为无非平凡factor–image自重叠的接受树；借助Crawley–Boevey的graph-map基证明其与对应树模的brick性等价；引入Q_1的局部染色，压缩箭头字母表。

Result: 给出识别树模编码的确定性有限自底向上树自动机；证明“automata-induced tree brick”与树模brick性等价；局部染色可在不改变树数据、graph maps和brick性质的前提下压缩字母表；最优颜色数为Q的入度与出度的最大值。

Conclusion: 建立树模编码、自动机识别与brick性质的等价框架，给出字母表压缩的最优颜色数，并提出开放问题：仅由brick组成的树语言是否为正则语言。

Abstract: Let $Λ=KQ/I$ be a finite-dimensional zero-relation algebra. We encode Crawley--Boevey tree modules over $Λ$ by finite rooted trees labelled by arrows of $Q$ and their formal inverses, and construct a deterministic finite bottom-up tree automaton recognizing exactly these encodings. We define an accepted tree to be an automata-induced tree brick when it has no non-trivial factor--image self-overlap, and use Crawley--Boevey's graph-map basis to prove that this is equivalent to brickness of the associated tree module. We also introduce local colourings of $Q_1$ and show that the arrow alphabet can be compressed without changing the tree data, graph maps, or brick property. The optimal number of colours for such a compression is the maximum of the in-degree and out-degree of $Q$. We conclude by asking whether the tree language consisting only of bricks is regular.

</details>


### [166] [Collision Positivity for Three-Variable Symmetric Monomial Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22465)
*Jian Sun*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究等次指数分拆 λ≻γ≻μ（至多三部分）下三点优超差 P_{λ,γ,μ}=J_λ+J_μ−2J_γ 的正性，证明了一个充要的“碰撞判据”：三变量非负当且仅当其在一变量退化轨迹（两变量相等）上非负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称多项式不等式中一大类问题（如 Schur 不等式及其推广）涉及多变量全局正性判定，直接验证通常困难。作者希望找到一个统一、可有效检验的判据，将真三变量对称多项式不等式的正性问题化归为一元问题，并借此超越经典 Schur 族，得到新的不等式与加强形式。

Method: 设 λ≻γ≻μ 为至多三部分的等次指数分拆，J_ν 为对称单项轨道和，定义 P_{λ,γ,μ}=J_λ+J_μ−2J_γ。核心方法是证明“碰撞判据”：对非负整数指数分拆，P_{λ,γ,μ}(x,y,z)≥0 (x,y,z>0) 当且仅当 P_{λ,γ,μ}(t,1,1)≥0 (t>0)。从而将三变量正性问题约化为沿两变量重合轨迹的一元多项式不等式。

Result: 给出了该形式无穷类对称多项式不等式的统一且可有效检验的充要判据。对固定整数链，全局三变量问题被归约为单个一元多项式不等式，可用因式分解、Sturm 定理等一元方法精确验证。判据超出经典 Schur 族，产生显式的非 Schur 型不等式，并得到 Schur 不等式的实质性加强和精细化。

Conclusion: 三点优超差 P_{λ,γ,μ} 在三变量上的正性完全由其在一变量退化轨迹上的限制决定，这一“碰撞判据”为一大类对称多项式不等式提供了统一、可计算的正性判别工具，并推广和加强了 Schur 型结果。

Abstract: Let \(λ\succγ\succμ\) be equal-degree exponent partitions with at most three parts, and let \begin{equation*} P_{λ,γ,μ} = J_λ+J_μ-2J_γ, \end{equation*} where \(J_ν\) denotes the symmetric monomial orbit sum associated with \(ν\). We prove a necessary and sufficient collision criterion for the positivity of this three-point majorization difference in three variables. For nonnegative integer exponent partitions, \begin{equation*} P_{λ,γ,μ}(x,y,z)\ge0 \qquad(x,y,z>0) \end{equation*} if and only if \begin{equation*} P_{λ,γ,μ}(t,1,1)\ge0 \qquad(t>0). \end{equation*} Thus the positivity of a genuinely three-variable symmetric polynomial of this form is completely determined by its one-variable restriction to the locus where two variables coincide.
  The theorem gives a uniform and effectively checkable criterion for an infinite class of symmetric polynomial inequalities. For a fixed integer chain, the global three-variable problem is reduced to a single univariate polynomial inequality, which can often be verified exactly by factorization, Sturm's theorem, or other one-variable methods. The criterion extends well beyond the classical Schur family, produces explicit inequalities outside the Schur pattern, and yields genuine strengthenings and refinements of Schur's inequality.

</details>


### [167] [Orthogonal Pairs in Maps from the Sphere to the Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22680)
*Jonathan A. Noel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了实数正交图的圆色数为4，解决了Ghebleh 2007年提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Ghebleh于2007年提出的猜想：实数正交图的圆色数等于4，该问题长期未获解决。

Method: 对任意连续映射 \(f:S^2\to S^1\) 及任意 \(\varepsilon>0\)，证明存在正交向量 \(x,y\in S^2\)，使得 \(f(x)\) 与 \(f(y)\) 之间最短弧长不超过 \(\pi/2+\varepsilon\)。

Result: 对任意连续映射 \(f:S^2\to S^1\) 及任意 \(\varepsilon>0\)，存在正交的 \(x,y\in S^2\)，满足 \(f(x)\) 与 \(f(y)\) 的最短弧长至多为 \(\pi/2+\varepsilon\)。

Conclusion: 该结论印证了Ghebleh的猜想，即实数正交图的圆色数等于4。

Abstract: We prove that, for any $f:S^2\to S^1$ and any $\varepsilon>0$, there exist orthogonal vectors $x,y\in S^2$ such that the length of the shortest arc between $f(x)$ and $f(y)$ is at most $π/2 +\varepsilon$. This proves a conjecture of Ghebleh from 2007 that the circular chromatic number of the real orthogonality graph is equal to four.

</details>


### [168] [Exact Area-Range Minima in the Quantitative Monsky Problem for Five and Seven Triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22693)
*Muxi Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究单位正方形剖分成 n 个非退化三角形时面积极差的下确界 Δ(n)，证明其对所有 n≥2 可达，并精确求出 Δ(5)、Δ(7)，同时给出 Δ(9) 的一个代数上界及排除某类剖分族的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三角形剖分中三角形面积的最大值与最小值之差的最小可能值 Δ(n)，旨在精确确定这一极差下确界的可达性及小 n 情形下的具体数值。

Method: 结合有限组合分类与精确符号计算、整数区间证书进行证明；对 n=9 采用倾斜条带构造给出代数上界，并分析单帽双轨锯齿族的面积范围。

Result: 证明 Δ(n) 对所有 n≥2 均可达；确定 Δ(5)=(5√5−11)/8，等号成立时三个面积为 (3−√5)/4、两个为 (3√5−5)/8；确定 Δ(7)=r_7，其中 r_7 是 864r^4+2160r^3−6060r^2+4972r−1 在 (0,1/4900) 的唯一根，每个最小化者具有四个面积 (1+3r_7)/7 和三个面积 (1−4r_7)/7；对 n=9 给出 Δ(9)≤0.0001273496861283553341… 的显式代数上界，并证明完整单帽双轨锯齿族中剖分的极差大于 1/3500。

Conclusion: Δ(n) 的下确界对 n≥2 可达；n=5 与 n=7 的精确最小值及对应面积构型已确定；n=9 的全局精确值仍为开放问题，但已排除单帽双轨锯齿族成为全局最小化者，并给出拓扑内的精确上界。

Abstract: For a dissection $D$ of the unit square into $n$ nondegenerate triangles, let $R(D)=\max_i a_i-\min_i a_i, Δ(n)=\inf_D R(D).$ We prove that this infimum is attained for every $n\ge2$, and determine the exact minima for $n=5$ and $n=7$, allowing T-junctions. For five triangles, $Δ(5)=\frac{5\sqrt5-11}{8};$ equality holds precisely when three areas equal $(3-\sqrt5)/4$ and two equal $(3\sqrt5-5)/8$. For seven triangles, $Δ(7)=r_7$, where $r_7$ is the unique root in $(0,1/4900)$ of $864r^4+2160r^3-6060r^2+4972r-1.$ Every minimizer has four areas $(1+3r_7)/7$ and three areas $(1-4r_7)/7$, although its geometry need not be unique. The proofs combine finite combinatorial classification with exact symbolic and integer-interval certificates.
  For nine triangles, a tilted-strip construction gives the explicit algebraic upper bound $Δ(9)\le 0.0001273496861283553341\ldots,$ which is the exact minimum within that topology. Conversely, every dissection in the complete single-cap two-rail zig-zag family, with arbitrary continuous areas, has range greater than $1/3500$; hence a global minimizer must lie outside that family. The exact value of $Δ(9)$ remains open.

</details>


### [169] [Chromatic symmetric functions for annular webs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22708)
*Jaeseong Oh,Seokbeom Yoon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为环形网（annular webs）引入了染色对称函数的组合定义，通过构造网的Shareshian–Wachs对合证明其对称性，并给出hook Schur系数的显式公式；同时引入web LLT函数，得到其hook Schur系数的正Laurent多项式公式，进而给出环形网HOMFLY–PT多项式系数的组合表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将染色对称函数与LLT函数等对称函数工具推广到环形网这一拓扑/组合对象上，以统一Turaev同构下的对称函数理论，并为环形网的HOMFLY–PT多项式系数提供组合解释。

Method: 为环形网给出染色对称函数的组合定义；构造网的Shareshian–Wachs对合以证明对称性；证明其与Turaev同构所关联的对称函数一致；推导hook Schur系数公式；引入web LLT函数并给出其hook Schur系数的正Laurent多项式公式。

Result: 证明了环形网染色对称函数的对称性及其与Turaev同构下对称函数的吻合；得到hook Schur系数的显式公式；获得web LLT函数hook Schur系数的正Laurent多项式公式；并由该公式得到环形网HOMFLY–PT多项式系数的组合表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作将染色对称函数与LLT理论推广至环形网，建立了网的对合、对称函数与HOMFLY–PT不变量之间的联系，为相关组合与拓扑不变量提供了新的组合框架。

Abstract: We introduce a combinatorial definition of chromatic symmetric functions for annular webs. We prove their symmetry by constructing a web analogue of the Shareshian--Wachs involution and show that they coincide with the symmetric functions associated to annular webs via Turaev's isomorphism. We then derive explicit formulas for their hook Schur coefficients. We also introduce web LLT functions, whose hook Schur coefficients admit positive Laurent-polynomial formulas. These formulas yield a combinatorial expression for the coefficients of the HOMFLY--PT polynomial of an annular web.

</details>


### [170] [The polynomial characterization of tope graphs of the lopsided sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22741)
*Xuan Zheng,Yan-Ting Xie,Shou-Jun Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明每个LOP的tope图与某个daisy cube共享立方多项式，给出立方多项式的显式表达，并刻画了LOP的tope图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答作者此前提出的问题：LOP的tope图是否与某个daisy cube共享立方多项式，并进一步刻画LOP的tope图类。

Method: 利用部分立方体（partial cubes）、daisy cubes、中值图与偏斜集（LOP）的tope图之间的关系，通过立方多项式与立方复形的f-多项式进行代数与结构分析。

Result: 证明每个LOP的tope图与某个daisy cube具有相同立方多项式；得到显式表达C_G(x)=f_K(x+1)；并给出偏斜集tope图类的等价刻画：(a) 极大pc-minor-closed类且每图与某daisy cube同立方多项式；(b) 每个对径子图与某daisy cube同立方多项式。

Conclusion: LOP的tope图类可由其立方多项式与daisy cube的关系及对径子图性质完全刻画，深化了对部分立方体子类的理解。

Abstract: The cube polynomial $C_G(x)$ generates the number of $k$-cubes on a graph $G$. As a subclass of partial cubes, the tope graphs of lopsided sets (LOPs) generalize daisy cubes and median graphs. In this paper, we prove that every tope graph of a LOP shares its cube polynomial with some daisy cube, thereby answering affirmatively a problem posed earlier by the authors. Furthermore, we present explicit expressions for the cube polynomials of tope graphs of LOPs: $C_G(x)=f_\mathcal{K}(x+1)$, where $f_\mathcal{K}(x)$ is the $f$-polynomial of the cubical complex $\mathcal{K}$ of $G$. Finally, we show that a class $\mathcal{G}$ of partial cubes is the class of tope graphs of LOPs if and only if the following equivalent conditions hold: (a) $\mathcal{G}$ is the maximal pc-minor-closed class such that every graph in $\mathcal{G}$ has the same cube polynomial as some daisy cube; (b) for every $G\in\mathcal{G}$, each antipodal subgraph of $G$ has the same cube polynomial as some daisy cube.

</details>


### [171] [Butson Hadamard Matrices from Pairs of Gauss Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22754)
*Cheng Yiin Ong,Bernhard Schmidt*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类Butson Hadamard矩阵$BH((F_q\times F_q,+),\mathrm{lcm}(6,d))$，其特点是字符值涉及两个Gauss和的乘积（含非二次Gauss和），并推广为基于"Butson-Jacobsthal函数"的一般构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的群不变Butson Hadamard矩阵构造中，字符值性质较为局限；本文希望给出一种新的构造，使其字符值涉及两个Gauss和之积（包括非二次Gauss和），从而区别于此前所有已知构造。

Method: 令$q$为素数幂，$d>1$为$q-1$的因子，在群$(F_q\times F_q,+)$上构造Butson Hadamard矩阵$BH((F_q\times F_q,+),\mathrm{lcm}(6,d))$；该构造是所谓"Butson-Jacobsthal函数"一般构造的特例，类比由Jacobsthal矩阵构造Hadamard矩阵的方法。

Result: 对任意素数幂$q$及$q-1$的任意因子$d>1$，成功构造出$BH((F_q\times F_q,+),\mathrm{lcm}(6,d))$矩阵；其对应群环元素的多数字符值为$F_q$上两个Gauss和之积，且包含非二次Gauss和。这一性质使其区别于此前所有已知的群不变Butson Hadamard矩阵构造。

Conclusion: 本文给出了基于Butson-Jacobsthal函数的新构造框架，并在$(F_q\times F_q,+)$上实现了$BH((F_q\times F_q,+),\mathrm{lcm}(6,d))$矩阵，其特征字符值与两个Gauss和之积相关（含非二次情形），为群不变Butson Hadamard矩阵提供了新的构造途径。

Abstract: Let $q$ be a prime power. We give a construction of Butson Hadamard matrices $BH((F_q\times F_q,+),lcm(6,d))$ for any divisor $d>1$ of $q-1$. Most character values of the group ring elements corresponding to these matrices involve products of two Gauss sums over $F_q$ (with non-quadratic Gauss sums occurring) and this property distinguishes our result from all previously known constructions of group invariant Butson Hadamard matrices. In fact, our $BH((F_q\times F_q,+),lcm(6,d))$ matrices arise as a special case of a more general construction based on what we call ``Butson-Jacobsthal functions'' that resembles the construction of Hadamard matrices from Jacobsthal matrices.

</details>


### [172] [Annihilating-Ideal Graphs and Orthogonality Graphs over $\mathbb{F}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22769)
*R. Nikandish*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造了一类有限局部环，其零化理想图可由 F_2^n 子空间的正交关系刻画；对 n=4 精确求出团数与色数，得到 ω=5<6=χ，从而该图非弱完美，推翻 Behboodi–Rakeei 猜想在非约化交换环情形下的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Behboodi–Rakeei 猜想关注零化理想图的弱完美性，既有研究多在约化环情形下讨论，非约化交换环情形尚缺乏反例；本文旨在构造合适环类以检验并否定该猜想。

Method: 构造一族有限局部环 R_n，使其零化理想图 AG(R_n) 与 F_2^n 的子空间正交关系自然对应；对 n=4 的情形，利用该正交结构精确计算 AG(R_4) 的团数与色数。

Result: 对 n=4 得到 ω(AG(R_4))=5 而 χ(AG(R_4))=6，即团数严格小于色数；因此 AG(R_4) 不是弱完美图。这给出了 Behboodi–Rakeei 猜想在非约化交换环情形下的反例。

Conclusion: 该族环及其正交图描述为研究零化理想图的组合性质提供了新工具；n=4 的反例表明 Behboodi–Rakeei 猜想对非约化交换环不成立，提示后续研究需对环的约化性等条件加以限制。

Abstract: We construct a family of finite local rings whose annihilating-ideal graphs are naturally described by orthogonality of subspaces of $\mathbb{F}_2^n$. For $n=4$ we determine the clique and chromatic numbers exactly and obtain \[ ω(\AG(R_4))=5<6=χ(\AG(R_4)). \] Thus $\AG(R_4)$ is not weakly perfect, and the Behboodi--Rakeei conjecture fails for non-reduced commutative rings.

</details>


### [173] [Sharp Edge-Edit Bounds at Every Level for Leaky Positive Semidefinite Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22804)
*Domenico Frijio*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定了1-漏正半定边删除猜想，并给出一个尖锐的替代定理：对任意漏级ℓ与边e，零强迫型参数差满足|Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G)−Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G−e)|≤2；若两图仅在被S内端点间的边不同，则参数差至多|S|。端点敏感的细化在特定条件下将正向增加限制为至多1，且正负号在所有正漏级下都可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原1-漏正半定边删除猜想断言删边后参数变化受限（可能被理解为单向或至多1），但该猜想不成立。作者旨在纠正该猜想，给出精确、尖锐的界，并刻画极端情形，从而厘清漏级、删边与零强迫型参数（正半定版本）之间的真实关系。

Method: 以正半定零强迫型参数Z⁺₍ℓ₎为核心对象，系统比较G与G−e；证明一般上界≤2，并推广到仅差于顶点集S内部边的两图（差≤|S|）。引入端点敏感 refinement：当G−e的某个最小集包含e的端点时，正向增加至多1。通过构造团-桥、团-叶等图族实现正负号尖锐性，并借助显式 forcing sequence、fort certificate 与精确验证器检查有限极端例子。

Result: 否定1-漏正半定边删除猜想；证明对任意ℓ与e有|Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G)−Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G−e)|≤2；更一般地，两图若仅差于S内部边，参数差≤|S|。端点敏感条件下正向增量≤1。正负号在每个正漏级下均尖锐：将两个K_{ℓ+1}用桥连接得Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G)=2ℓ、Z⁺₍ℓ₎(G−e)=2ℓ+2；对每个ℓ≥2，存在2ℓ+3阶连通团-叶对给出相反差值。余下的正差一漏情形由九顶点连通图达到：Z⁺₍₁₎(H)=4，Z⁺₍₁₎(G)=6。

Conclusion: 删边对正半定零强迫型参数的影响被精确控制在至多2，且可由端点集大小推广；端点敏感 refinement 进一步收紧为至多1。正负两个方向的极端差值均可由显式连通图族实现，最小有限极端例子为九顶点图。结果以 forcing sequence、fort certificate 和精确验证器相互印证，替代并强化了原1-漏边删除猜想。

Abstract: This work disproves the 1-leaky positive semidefinite edge-deletion conjecture and replaces it with a sharp theorem. For every leak level $\ell$ and edge $e$, one has $|Z^+_{(\ell)}(G)-Z^+_{(\ell)}(G-e)|\le 2$. More generally, if two graphs differ only on edges with both endpoints in $S$, their parameters differ by at most $|S|$. An endpoint-sensitive refinement recovers an increase of at most one whenever some minimum set for $G-e$ contains an endpoint of $e$. Both signs are sharp for every positive leak level. Joining two copies of $K_{\ell+1}$ by a bridge gives $Z^+_{(\ell)}(G)=2\ell$ and $Z^+_{(\ell)}(G-e)=2\ell+2$. For every $\ell\ge 2$, a connected clique-leaf pair of order $2\ell+3$ gives the opposite difference. The remaining positive-difference one-leak case is attained by connected graphs on nine vertices with $Z^+_{(1)}(H)=4$ and $Z^+_{(1)}(G)=6$. Explicit forcing sequences, fort certificates, and an exact verifier check the finite extremal example and stress-test the general results.

</details>


### [174] [An Odd Pfaffian Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22928)
*Priyanshu Pant,Ranveer Singh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文反驳了Miranda与Lucchesi关于非平凡Pfaffian数均为偶数的猜想，证明$K_{3,3}\sqcup K_{3,3}$的Pfaffian数为13，并构造了一个Pfaffian数为13的连通三次二部匹配覆盖图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Norine于2009年猜想Pfaffian数均为4的幂，Miranda与Lucchesi于2011年通过构造Pfaffian数为6的图推翻了该猜想，并提出新的猜想：每个非平凡Pfaffian数都是偶数。本文旨在检验并反驳这一新猜想。

Method: 通过构造具体的图实例并计算其Pfaffian数。主要计算了非连通图$K_{3,3}\sqcup K_{3,3}$的Pfaffian数，并进一步构造了一个连通的三次二部匹配覆盖图，同样具有Pfaffian数13。

Result: 证明了$K_{3,3}\sqcup K_{3,3}$的Pfaffian数为13（奇数），从而反驳了Miranda-Lucchesi猜想；同时构造出一个连通三次二部匹配覆盖图，其Pfaffian数也为13。

Conclusion: 非平凡Pfaffian数不一定是偶数，Miranda与Lucchesi的猜想不成立；Pfaffian数可以为奇数（如13），且奇Pfaffian数也可出现在连通的、结构良好的图类中。

Abstract: The Pfaffian number of a graph is the minimum number of Pfaffians needed to obtain its perfect-matching polynomial by linear combination. In 2009, Norine conjectured that every Pfaffian number is a power of four. Miranda and Lucchesi disproved this conjecture in 2011 by constructing a graph of Pfaffian number six, and conjectured instead that every nontrivial Pfaffian number is even. We disprove their conjecture by proving that the Pfaffian number of $K_{3,3}\sqcup K_{3,3}$ is 13. We also construct a connected cubic bipartite matching-covered graph with Pfaffian number 13.

</details>


### [175] [Two-disjoint-cycle-cover edge bipancyclicity of bipartite generalized hypercubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23031)
*Ke Lu,Ruichao Niu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明偶数维广义超立方体（各 $d_i$ 为偶数）在 $N\ge8$ 时具有带指定边的两不相交圈覆盖顶点双泛圈性，并给出唯一例外 $K_2\BoxProd C_{2p}$（$p\ge3$），导出边双泛圈性及偶 $k$ 元 $n$ 立方体的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两不相交圈覆盖（2-DCC）与双泛圈性是互连网络哈密顿性研究的重要方向，但已有结果多未同时保留指定边的信息。本文旨在对广义超立方体建立更强的"顶点双泛圈性"精确刻画，即在任意一对独立边上进行两圈划分。

Method: 研究对象为 $G=C(d_1,\ldots,d_n)=F_1\BoxProd\cdots\BoxProd F_n$，$n\ge2$，$d_i$ 均为偶数，$N=|V(G)|\ge8$，其中 $F_i=K_2$（$d_i=2$）或 $F_i=C_{d_i}$（$d_i\ge4$）。对任意有序独立边对 $e,f$ 与偶数 $4\le\ell\le N-4$，构造顶点集划分为长度 $\ell$ 与 $N-\ell$ 的圈 $J_1,J_2$，且 $e\in E(J_1)$、$f\in E(J_2)$，并刻划其成立的充要条件。

Result: 该划分对任意满足条件的 $e,f,\ell$ 均存在，当且仅当 $G$ 不同构于 $K_2\BoxProd C_{2p}$（$p\ge3$）；例外情形 $C(2,2)\cong C_4$ 不含 2-DCC，单独处理。由此可推出保留指定边信息的普通边双泛圈性，以及偶 $k$ 元 $n$ 立方体的专门结论。

Conclusion: 本文给出了广义超立方体两不相交圈覆盖顶点双泛圈性的精确（充要）强化定理，明确了唯一的非平凡例外图类，并统一覆盖了边双泛圈性与偶 $k$ 元 $n$ 立方体等特例。

Abstract: Let \(G=C(d_1,\ldots,d_n)=F_1\BoxProd\cdots\BoxProd F_n\) be a bipartite generalized hypercube with \(n\geq2\), all \(d_i\) even, and \(N=|V(G)|\geq8\), where \(F_i=K_2\) when \(d_i=2\), and \(F_i=C_{d_i}\) when \(d_i\geq4\). We prove the following exact strengthening of two-disjoint-cycle-cover vertex bipancyclicity. For every ordered pair of independent edges \(e,f\in E(G)\) and every even integer \(4\leq\ell\leq N-4\), the vertex set can be partitioned into two cycles \(J_1,J_2\) of lengths \(\ell\) and \(N-\ell\), respectively, with \(e\in E(J_1)\) and \(f\in E(J_2)\), if and only if \(G\) is not isomorphic to any \(K_2\BoxProd C_{2p}\) with \(p\geq3\). The graph \(C(2,2)\cong C_4\) is treated separately: it has no 2-DCC. Consequences that retain prescribed-edge information include ordinary edge bipancyclicity and the even \(k\)-ary \(n\)-cube specialization.

</details>


### [176] [An improved upper bound for the fair domination number of maximal outerplanar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23076)
*Yair Caro,Riste Škrekovski*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进极大外平面图的公平控制数上界，从17n/19降至7n/8，并构造下界族得到渐近常数介于7/9与7/8之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caro、Hansberg和Henning引入公平控制集与公平控制数fd(G)，证明n≥3阶极大外平面图满足fd(G)<17n/19，并提问该界是否渐近最优。本文旨在回答该问题并改进界值。

Method: 通过组合分析与构造性证明，对极大外平面图的结构性质进行分析，给出公平控制数的上界证明；同时构造一无限族极大外平面图以给出下界实例。

Result: 证明对任意n≥3阶极大外平面图G有fd(G)≤(7n-3)/8<7n/8；并构造无限族极大外平面图使fd(G)/n→7/9。因此最优渐近常数位于7/9与7/8之间，且原17n/19界非渐近最优。

Conclusion: 原17n/19不是渐近最优的；上界改进为7n/8，下界可达7/9，最优渐近常数仍待确定，范围在[7/9,7/8]。

Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a graph $G$ is a \emph{fair dominating set} if every two vertices outside $D$ have the same number of neighbors in $D$, and the \emph{fair domination number} $\mathrm{fd}(G)$ is the minimum cardinality of such a set. Caro, Hansberg and Henning, who introduced this parameter, proved that $\mathrm{fd}(G)<17n/19$ for every maximal outerplanar graph $G$ of order $n\geq3$, and asked whether this bound is asymptotically best possible. We show that it is not the case by proving $\mathrm{fd}(G)\leq(7n-3)/8<7n/8$ for every maximal outerplanar graph $G$ of order $n\geq3$, and we exhibit an infinite family of maximal outerplanar graphs with $\mathrm{fd}(G)/n\rightarrow7/9$, so that the best asymptotic constant lies between $7/9$ and $7/8$.

</details>


### [177] [Improved upper bound on the number of distinct k-decks for any k and alphabet size by counting the independent parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23106)
*Arman Nilforoushan,Farzad Parvaresh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究合成DNA存储中由shotgun测序产生的$k$-deck（长度$k$子序列出现频次向量）。作者分析固定所有更短deck后$k$-deck的自由度，给出不同字母多重数类中维度等于同多重数Lyndon词数的仿射子空间刻画与Möbius和闭式，导出改进上界$D_{q,k}(n)=O(n^{E_q(k)})$（$E_q(k)=\sum_{j=1}^k jL_q(j)-1$），并证明$D_{q,2}(n)=\Theta(n^{q^2-1})$与$D_{2,3}(n)=\Theta(n^9)$的匹配下界，验证其猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成DNA存储通过shotgun测序读出，返回的是短子序列而非原始存储词，这一读出机制的自然抽象是词的$k$-deck，即记录每个长度$k$词作为子序列出现频次的向量。两个存储词当且仅当$k$-deck不同时可由读出区分，因此长度$k$读出能保留多少信息，由长度$n$词的不同$k$-deck数目$D_{q,k}(n)$度量。该计数刻画了读出所保留的信息量，是理解DNA存储信息容量与可区分性的核心问题。

Method: 作者分析固定所有更短deck后$k$-deck剩余的自由度。对每个给定字母多重数的词类，将长度$k$的deck项限制在一个仿射子空间中，证明其维数恰等于具有相同多重数的Lyndon词数，并给出该数作为Möbius和的闭式表达。以$L_q(j)$表示字母表大小$q$下长度$j$的Lyndon词数，由此推出$D_{q,k}(n)$的上界。随后在两个非平凡情形中构造匹配下界，分别处理一般字母表的$k=2$与二元字母表的$k=3$。

Result: 得到改进上界$D_{q,k}(n)=O\!\left(n^{E_q(k)}\right)$，其中$E_q(k)=\sum_{j=1}^{k}j\,L_q(j)-1$；二元字母表情形为$D_{2,k}(n)=O\!\left(n^{4\cdot 2^{k-1}}\right)$。并证明两个匹配下界：对所有字母表大小$q$，$D_{q,2}(n)=\Theta\!\left(n^{q^2-1}\right)$；对二元字母表，$D_{2,3}(n)=\Theta(n^9)$。后者在$q=2,k=3$时证实了作者关于上界对所有$q,k$均具有正确次数的猜测。

Conclusion: 长度$k$读出在固定所有更短deck后的自由度由同多重数Lyndon词数精确刻画，并给出$k$-deck数目的改进上界；在$k=2$（任意字母表）与二元$k=3$情形下取得匹配下界，证实在$q=2,k=3$处上界的指数为真，支持了该上界对所有$q,k$均紧的猜想。

Abstract: Data stored in synthetic DNA is retrieved by shotgun sequencing, which returns short subsequences rather than the stored word itself. A natural abstraction of this readout is the $k$-deck of a word: the vector recording how often each word of length $k$ occurs as a subsequence. Two stored words are distinguishable from their readouts exactly when their $k$-decks differ, so the number $D_{q,k}(n)$ of distinct $k$-decks of words of length $n$ over an alphabet of size $q$ measures what a length-$k$ readout retains.
  We analyse the degrees of freedom remaining in a $k$-deck once all shorter decks are fixed. Within each class of words having prescribed letter multiplicities, the length-$k$ entries are confined to an affine subspace whose dimension is exactly the number of Lyndon words with the same multiplicities, which we give in closed form as a Möbius sum. Writing $L_q(j)$ for the number of Lyndon words of length $j$ over an alphabet of size $q$, we deduce the improved upper bound \[
  D_{q,k}(n)=O\!\left(n^{E_q(k)}\right),\qquad E_q(k)=\sum_{j=1}^{k}j\,L_q(j)-1 . \] In the case of a binary alphabet this bound satisfies $D_{2,k}(n)=O\!\left(n^{4\cdot 2^{k-1}}\right)$.
  We then prove matching lower bounds in the first two nontrivial cases: $D_{q,2}(n)=Θ\!\left(n^{q^2-1}\right)$ for every alphabet size $q$, and $D_{2,3}(n)=Θ(n^{9})$ for the binary alphabet. The latter confirms, for $q=2$ and $k=3$, our conjecture that the upper bound has the correct degree for every $q$ and $k$.

</details>


### [178] [Degree sequence condition for pancyclicity in tough graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23107)
*Songling Shan,Zachary Warren*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hoàng于1995年提出的关于t-tough图的Hamilton性与度序列条件的pancyclic（泛圈）猜想：当t=1时该猜想不成立，而当t≥7时该猜想成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hoàng在1995年提出了一个联系图t-tough性、Hamilton性与度序列的泛圈猜想，但该猜想长期以来未得到验证或否定，作者希望解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 采用极值图论与Hamilton图/泛圈图理论工具，针对t=1构造反例图族以证伪猜想；针对t≥7利用t-tough性蕴含的结构性质与度序列条件进行论证，证明图是泛圈或二部图。

Result: 对于t=1，构造出满足猜想全部条件但并不泛圈也不二部的图，从而否定猜想；对于所有t≥7，证明了猜想成立，即图必为泛圈图或二部图。

Conclusion: Hoàng的猜想并非对所有t成立，其正确性依赖于t的取值：t=1时有反例，t≥7时成立，介于两者之间的情形仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $t \ge 1$ be an integer, and let $G$ be a $t$-tough $n$-vertex graph with degree sequence $d_1, d_2, \ldots, d_n$ in non-decreasing order.
  In 1995, Hoàng conjectured that if $G$ is Hamiltonian and, for every integer $i$ satisfying $t\le i<n/2$, $d_i\le i$, and $d_{n-i+t}<n-i$, one has $d_j + d_{n-j+t} \ge n$ for all $j$ with $i < j < \frac{n}{2}$, then $G$ is pancyclic or bipartite.
  In this paper, we disprove the conjecture for $t = 1$ and confirm it for all $t \ge 7$.

</details>


### [179] [Perfect Codes in the Johnson Scheme Hardly Exist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23128)
*Tuvi Etzion,Xiande Zhang,Wenjie Zhong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文大幅缩小了Johnson方案J(n,w)中e-完美码可能存在的参数范围，证明当e∉{1,2,4,9,10,12,16}时不存在e-完美码，剩余七种情形留待后续论文解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delsarte于1973年猜想Johnson方案J(n,w)中不存在非平凡的完美码。尽管其他重要方案中的完美码存在性问题大多已解决，Johnson方案中的这一问题至今仍未解决，因此需要进一步推进该猜想的证明。

Method: 通过理论分析（可能涉及编码理论中的谱方法、Delsarte线性规划界或相关代数组合工具）对e-完美码在Johnson方案中的存在性条件进行限制，排除除少数特例外的大部分e值。

Result: 证明了当e∉{1,2,4,9,10,12,16}时，Johnson方案中不存在e-完美码，从而将可能的e值大幅缩减至七个特例。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了Delsarte猜想的研究，将Johnson方案中e-完美码的可能存在情形限制在七个特定e值上，这些剩余情形将在后续论文中予以考虑和解决。

Abstract: In his pioneer work from 1973, Delsarte conjectured that there are no nontrivial perfect codes in the Johnson scheme J$(n,w)$. While in most other important schemes the existence problem for perfect codes was settled, the problem is still open in the Johnson scheme. In this work we considerably reduce the possible existence of such codes. We prove that there are no $e$-perfect codes in the Johnson scheme when $e \not\in \{1,2,4,9,10,12,16\}$. These seven cases will be considered and solved in a follow up paper.

</details>


### [180] [Counting edge-colorings of a complete graph avoiding a rainbow $K_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23179)
*Fabricio S. Benevides,Josefran de O. Bastos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究避免彩虹 $K_4$ 的 $r$-边着色计数 $\rho_{r,4}(K_n)$，推翻了"大多数此类着色为 5-着色"的自然猜想，并确定了 $r$ 的精确阈值：$6\le r\le 24$ 时 $\rho_{r,4}(K_n)=(\binom{r}{5}+o(1))5^{\binom{n}{2}}$，$r\ge 25$ 时 $\rho_{r,4}(K_n)=r^{(n^2/4)+o(n^2)}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Balogh 与 Li 及 Bastos 等人证明了 $k=3$ 时大多数 Gallai 着色是 2-着色。一个自然的类比猜想是：当 $k=4$、$r\ge 5$ 且 $n$ 很大时，大多数不含彩虹 $K_4$ 的 $r$-边着色应为 5-着色。本文旨在检验并量化这一猜想在何处失效。

Method: 证明结合了超图容器方法（hypergraph containers）与图移除引理（graph removal lemma）、色板（color palette）的结构性估计，以及对接近固定五色色板的着色的精细计数。上界的指数增长率由一类二分构造给出：两部分的内部边各赋一种公共颜色。

Result: 猜想一般不成立。存在 $r$ 的精确阈值：当 $6\le r\le 24$ 时，$\rho_{r,4}(K_n)=(\binom{r}{5}+o(1))5^{\binom{n}{2}}$；当 $r\ge 25$ 时，使用至多五种颜色的着色比例趋于零，且 $\rho_{r,4}(K_n)=r^{(n^2/4)+o(n^2)}$。二分构造达到后者的指数增长率。下界可容易地推广到任意 $k$。

Conclusion: 本文否定了"大多数彩虹-$K_4$-free $r$-着色为 5-着色"的类比猜想，精确确定了 $r$ 的临界值，并给出了两个区间内的指数增长率。结果与近期关于避免彩虹团或给定彩虹模式的着色计数研究相关，且下界方法可推广至一般 $k$。

Abstract: For $k, r, n$ natural numbers let $ρ_{r,k}(K_n)$ be the number of $r$-edge-colorings of $K_n$ that do not contain a rainbow copy of a $K_k$, that is, a copy of $K_k$ in which all edges receive different colors. When $k=3$, the quantity $ρ_{r,3}(K_n)$ represents the number of Gallai Colorings. It was proved by Balogh and Li and independently by Bastos, Benevides and Han, that most of the Gallai colorings are 2-colorings, for $n$ large. A natural analogue conjecture would be that when $k=4$, $r\ge 5$ and $n$ large, most rainbow-$K_4$-free $r$-edge-colorings are $5$-colorings. We show that this is not true in general and identify an exact threshold for $r$ where this ceases to be true. For the range where the conjecture is false, we determine the exponential growth of $ρ_{r,4}(K_n)$ for every fixed $r$. More precisely, for \(6\le r\le24\), we prove that \(ρ_{r,4}(K_n)=(\binom{r}{5}+o(1))5^{\binom{n}{2}}\); and for each \(r\ge25\), the proportion using at most five colors tends to zero, and \(ρ_{r,4}(K_n)=r^{(n^2/4)+o(n^2)}\). A bipartite construction, with all edges within the two parts assigned one common color, achieves the latter exponential growth rate. The lower bounds can be easily generalized for every $k$. Those results are related to other recent results about counting colorings that avoid rainbow cliques or given rainbow patterns in general. Our proof combines hypergraph containers with the graph removal lemma, structural estimates for color palettes and a refined count of colorings close to a fixed five-color palette.

</details>


### [181] [A cyclic non-Ramsey heptagon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23327)
*Dömötör Pálvölgyi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个含七个圆上点的非拉姆齐构型，从而否定了 Erdős 等六人提出的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős、Graham、Montgomery、Rothschild、Spencer 与 Straus 曾猜想圆上点集的拉姆齐性质成立，本文旨在检验并推翻该猜想。

Method: 显式构造圆上七个点的几何构型，并证明该构型不是拉姆齐的。

Result: 给出七点反例，证明原猜想不成立。

Conclusion: 圆上点集的拉姆齐性质不能推广到七个点，原猜想被否定。

Abstract: We present a construction with seven points on a circle that is not Ramsey, disproving a conjecture of Erdős, Graham, Montgomery, Rothschild, Spencer and Straus.

</details>


### [182] [Multiset Colorings of Random Graphs Across Density Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23362)
*Arash Ahadi,Sharareh Alipour*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机图的multiset chromatic number χ_m(G)，证明G(n,1/2)以高概率满足χ_m(G)≤3（改进此前上界5），并对一般p给出χ_m(G)≤4；同时对接近完全图的稠密随机图刻画出χ_m(G)=2/β+O(1)的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: multiset chromatic number要求顶点划分使得每对相邻顶点在三部分中的邻居数向量不同，此前的上界为5，存在改进空间；同时对于图接近完全（p→1）时χ_m(G)的增长规律尚未被精确刻画。

Method: 将精心选取的初始划分中未解决的边转化为布尔立方体上的超平面，应用Linial–Radhakrishnan的essential covers理论；接近完全图的下界用谱方法，上界用多项分布反集中不等式与Lovász Local Lemma。

Result: 对G(n,1/2)，以高概率χ_m(G)≤3（原上界为5），且该三部分界对所有固定0.185<p<0.509成立；对任意固定p∈(0,1)，χ_m(G)≤4以高概率成立。对G(n,1−n^{−(1−β)})，以高概率有2/β≤χ_m(G)≤⌊2/β⌋+5，即χ_m(G)=2/β+O(1)。

Conclusion: 随机图的multiset chromatic number在稀疏情形下常数有界（≤3或≤4），在接近完全时以2/β的速率增长；所用工具将组合划分问题与布尔立方体覆盖及概率方法相联系。

Abstract: We show that almost every graph admits a partition of its vertex set into three parts such that no two adjacent vertices have the same number of neighbors in each of the three parts. Equivalently, for $G\sim G(n,1/2)$, $χ_m(G)\le3$ with high probability, improving the previously known bound of five. Here $χ_m(G)$ denotes the multiset chromatic number of $G$, the minimum number of parts in a vertex partition whose neighbor-count vectors distinguish every pair of adjacent vertices. In fact, the three-part bound holds for every fixed $0.185<p<0.509$. More generally, for every fixed $p\in(0,1)$, $G\sim G(n,p)$ satisfies $χ_m(G)\le4$ with high probability. These results are obtained by converting the unresolved edges of a carefully chosen initial partition into hyperplanes of a Boolean cube and applying the Linial--Radhakrishnan theory of essential covers.
  We also determine how $χ_m(G)$ grows when the graph is polynomially close to complete. For every fixed $β\in(0,1)$ and $G\sim G\!\left(n,1-n^{-(1-β)}\right)$, with high probability $\frac{2}β\le χ_m(G)\le \left\lfloor\frac{2}β\right\rfloor+5$. Thus $χ_m(G)=2/β+O(1)$. The lower bound is spectral, while the upper bound follows from multinomial anti-concentration and the Lov'asz Local Lemma.

</details>


### [183] [The Last Seven Open Radii for Perfect Codes in the Johnson Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23368)
*Xiande Zhang,Wenjie Zhong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Johnson方案中当e∈{1,2,4,9,10,12,16}时不存在非平凡的e-完美码，并与已有工作"Perfect Codes in the Johnson Scheme Hardly Exist"结合，完整证明了Delsarte猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delsarte (1973) 猜想Johnson方案中不存在非平凡的完美码。该猜想长期未获完全解决，需结合多个e值情形的分类结果才能完成证明。

Method: 针对e∈{1,2,4,9,10,12,16}这些具体参数，利用编码理论与关联方案（association scheme）中完美码的判定条件进行排除性证明，得出这些情形下不存在非平凡的e-完美码。

Result: 证明了对e∈{1,2,4,9,10,12,16}，Johnson方案中不存在非平凡的e-完美码。

Conclusion: 本文结果与"Perfect Codes in the Johnson Scheme Hardly Exist"一文互为补充，二者结合完整地证明了Delsarte猜想：Johnson方案中不存在非平凡的完美码。

Abstract: Delsarte (1973) conjectured that there are no nontrivial perfect codes in the Johnson scheme. In this paper, we prove that there are no nontrivial $e$-perfect codes in the Johnson scheme for $e\in\{1,2,4,9,10,12,16\}$. This paper complements "Perfect Codes in the Johnson Scheme Hardly Exist", thus proving Delsarte's conjecture completely.

</details>


### [184] [Classification of strictly resistance nonnegative graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23394)
*Hailey Jay Garcia*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的电阻非负性(RN)与电阻正性(RP)，证明每个2-连通严格RN图必为两部分大小相差1的二分图，从而不存在1-tough严格RN图，并证明每个1-tough RN图也是RP图，最后量化了RN图在1以下可取得的toughness值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图论中，电阻曲率概念（Devriendt与Lambiotte提出）为图的结构分析提供新视角。研究RN与RP图类之间的关系，特别是它们与图的连通性/韧性(toughness)的联系，有助于理解这些图类的结构限制。

Method: 通过定义正边权使电阻曲率非负(RN)与正(RP)，引入严格RN概念（RN但非RP）。利用图的结构分析工具研究2-连通严格RN图的二分结构，并结合toughness条件进行分析。

Result: 证明每个2-连通严格RN图是两部分大小相差1的二分图；由此推出不存在1-tough严格RN图；进而证明每个1-tough RN图都是RP图；并精确量化了RN图在1以下可达到的toughness值。

Conclusion: RN与RP图类之间存在由二分结构和toughness阈值决定的结构性界限，1-tough成为区分RN与RP的关键阈值。

Abstract: We say that a graph is resistance nonnegative or RN if it admits a positive edge-weight that yields nonnegative resistance curvature in the sense of Devriendt and Lambiotte. Analogously, a graph may be resistance positive or RP; we say a graph is strictly RN if it is RN but not RP.
  In this paper, we show that every $2$-connected strictly RN graph is bipartite with parts whose sizes differ by one, demonstrating that there are no $1$-tough strictly RN graphs. As a consequence, we prove that every $1$-tough RN graph is also RP. Lastly, we quantify the exact toughness values an RN graph can attain below $1$.

</details>


### [185] [Edge and spectral conditions for rainbow pancyclicity in graph collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23532)
*Lihua You,Xiaoxue Zhang,Xinghui Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图集合的彩虹泛圈性，证明若每个图边数至少为 C(n-1,2)+1 且 n≥3，则图集合除三种例外情形外均为彩虹泛圈，从而回答了已有文献中的公开问题并加强了相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩虹哈密顿性已有研究，但彩虹泛圈性（包含所有长度 3 到 n 的彩虹圈）尚未解决；作者希望回答 [Discrete Math., 348(2025), 114600] 中的 Problem 1，并将彩虹哈密顿性推广到彩虹泛圈性，同时改进关于 ρ(Gi)>n-2 的定理。

Method: 设 G 为 n 个具有共同顶点集的 n 阶图组成的集合。若对每个 i 都有 e(Gi) ≥ C(n-1,2)+1 且 n≥3，作者证明 G 为彩虹泛圈，仅排除三种明确刻画的例外图集合；作为推论，在 ρ(Gi)>n-2 条件下给出彩虹泛圈性结论，并进一步刻画 ρ(Gi)≥n-2 时所有非彩虹泛圈的图集合。

Result: 证明了在 e(Gi) ≥ C(n-1,2)+1 条件下，除三种显式例外外图集合是彩虹泛圈的；得到若 ρ(Gi)>n-2 则 G 为彩虹泛圈，除非 G1=...=Gn≅K1∨(K_{n-2}∪K1)；并完整刻画了 ρ(Gi)≥n-2 时非彩虹泛圈的所有图集合。

Conclusion: 该工作回答了已有文献的 Problem 1，将彩虹哈密顿性加强为彩虹泛圈性，并改进了相关定理；同时在 ρ(Gi)≥n-2 条件下给出了非彩虹泛圈图集合的完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{G}=\{G_1,\dots,G_{n}\}$ be a collection of not necessarily distinct $n$-vertex graphs with a common vertex set $V$. A cycle $C$ with $V(C)\subseteq V$ and $|E(C)|\leq n$ is called \emph{rainbow} in $\mathbf{G}$, if there exists an injection $φ\colon E(C)\to [n]$ such that $e\in E(G_{φ(e)})$ for each $e\in E(C)$. The graph collection $\mathbf{G}$ is said to be \emph{rainbow pancyclic} if it contains a rainbow cycle of every length from 3 to $n$. In this paper, we show that if $e(G_i)\ge \binom{n-1}{2}+1$ for each $i\in[n]$ with $n\ge 3$, then $\mathbf{G}$ is rainbow pancyclic, apart from three explicitly described exceptional graph collections. This answers Problem $1$ of [Discrete Math., \textbf {348}(2025), 114600] and strengthens the result from rainbow Hamiltonicity to rainbow pancyclicity. As a consequence, we obtain that if $ρ(G_i)>n-2$ for each $i\in[n]$, then $\mathbf{G}$ is rainbow pancyclic unless $G_1=G_2=\dots=G_n\cong K_1\vee(K_{n-2}\cup K_1)$, which improves Theorem $5$ of [Discrete Math., \textbf {348}(2025), 114600]. We also characterize all graph collections that are not rainbow pancyclic under the condition $ρ(G_i)\ge n-2$ for each $i\in[n]$.

</details>


### [186] [Embedding rooted blow-ups of tree posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23543)
*Balázs Patkós*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明树偏序集的鲁棒型 Bukh 定理：若集族大小达到 $(h(T)-1+\varepsilon)\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$，则包含任意树偏序集 $T$ 的 $b$-blow-up 弱拷贝，其中 $b=\lfloor\delta n\rfloor$，从而解决了 Treglown 与作者的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bukh 定理给出了 $2^{[n]}$ 中包含弱 $T$ 拷贝的阈值条件，其后有若干强化与推广。本文旨在建立"鲁棒"版本，即不仅保证弱拷贝存在，还保证含有根化 blow-up 结构，以统一导出计数、随机版本及新的反 Ramsey 结果。

Method: 定义树偏序集 $T$ 关于 $x$ 的 $b$-blow-up $T(x,b)$：按距离 $d(x,u)$ 将元素 $u$ 替换为 $b^{d}$ 个新元素，并令每个 $v$ 的拷贝连接 $b$ 个 $u$ 的拷贝且不同 $v$ 拷贝对应不相交的 $u$ 拷贝集。利用极值集合论中的鲁棒嵌入技术证明：对任意 $T$、$x$、$\varepsilon>0$，存在 $\delta$ 使任意含 $(h(T)-1+\varepsilon)\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$ 个集合的 $\mathcal F\subseteq 2^{[n]}$ 都包含弱 $T(x,\lfloor\delta n\rfloor)$。

Result: 对任意树偏序集 $T$、$x\in T$ 与 $\varepsilon>0$，存在 $\delta>0$，使得任何满足上述大小的集族 $\mathcal F$ 都含有弱 $T(x,\lfloor\delta n\rfloor)$ 拷贝；由此解决了 Treglown 与作者提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 该结论是 Bukh 定理的鲁棒强化。作为应用，可由该嵌入结果推出已知的渐近计数版本与随机版本，并获得树偏序集的新极大反 Ramsey 结果。

Abstract: A tree poset $T$ is a poset whose Hasse diagram is a tree. Bukh proved that if a family $\mathcal F\subseteq 2^{[n]}$ contains $(h(T)-1+\varepsilon)\binom{n}{\lfloor \frac{n}{2}\rfloor}$ sets, then $\mathcal F$ contains a weak copy of $T$, where $h(T)$ is the height of $T$, the number of elements in a longest chain of $T$. Several strengthenings and generalizations of this result have been obtained. We prove the following robust variant. For a tree poset $T$ and $x\in T$, the $b$-blow-up $T(x,b)$ rooted at $x$ is the tree poset that we obtain from $T$ by replacing every element $u$ by $b^d$ new elements, where $d$ is the distance $d(x,u)$ in the Hasse diagram of $T$ and an edge $uv$ with $v$ being closer to $x$ is replaced by edges such that every new copy of $v$ is joined to $b$ new copies of $u$ such that these new copies form pairwise disjoint sets for the copies of $v$. We prove that for any tree poset $T$, $x\in T$, and $\varepsilon>0$ there exists $δ$ such that if $\mathcal F\subseteq 2^{[n]}$ contains $(h(T)-1+\varepsilon)\binom{n}{\lfloor \frac{n}{2}\rfloor}$ sets, then $\mathcal F$ contains a weak copy of $T(x,\lfloor δn\rfloor)$. This settles a conjecture of Treglown and the author. As applications, we derive the known asymptotic counting and random versions of Bukh's theorem from this stronger embedding result, and obtain new maximal anti-Ramsey results for tree posets.

</details>


### [187] [The existence spectrum of near triple arrays with four rows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23584)
*GuangZhou Chen,Yaxin Yue,Yong Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究四行近三重阵列的存在性，证明了存在 $(4\times c,v)$-近三重阵列当且仅当 $v\geq c\geq 4$，除了七组例外 $(c,v)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三重阵列由 Agrawal 于 1950-1960 年代提出。Gordeev、Markström 和 Öhman 通过放松三重阵列的三个相交性质提出了近三重阵列，并完全解决了三行情形。本文旨在将存在性刻画进一步推广到四行情形。

Method: 通过放松三重阵列的三个相交性质（允许两个值集中在平均相交数附近，以及两个连续的重数取值）来定义近三重阵列，并对四行情形进行构造与存在性分析。

Result: 证明了存在 $(4\times c,v)$-近三重阵列当且仅当 $v\geq c\geq 4$，除了 $(c,v)\in\{(4,9),(5,7),(5,10),(6,8),(7,9),(10,12),(11,13)\}$ 七组例外。

Conclusion: 本文将三行近三重阵列的存在性结果完整推广至四行，给出了四行近三重阵列几乎完全的存在性刻画，仅留下有限个例外。

Abstract: In the 1950s and 1960s, Agrawal introduced a class of experimental designs that later became known as triple arrays. Gordeev, Markström and Öhman proposed near triple arrays by relaxing all three intersection properties of triple arrays, allowing two values concentrated around the average intersection size, as well as two consecutive values for the replication numbers. They completely resolved the existence of near triple arrays with three rows, showing that there exists a $(3\times c,v)$-near triple array if and only if $v\geq c\geq 3$ except for $(c,v)\in\{(3,6),(4,6),(5,8)\}$. In this paper, we further investigate the existence of near triple arrays with four rows and prove that there exists a $(4\times c,v)$-near triple array if and only if $v\geq c\geq 4$ except for $(c,v)\in\{ (4,9),(5,7),(5,10),(6,8),(7,9),(10,12),(11,13)\}$.

</details>


### [188] [On Kahn's flow conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23595)
*Peter Keevash*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了Kahn的流猜想，即Chvátal关于下集最大相交子族猜想的强形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kahn的流猜想是Chvátal关于下集最大相交子族猜想的强化版本，此前尚未被证明，具有重要理论意义。

Method: 摘要未提供具体证明方法。

Result: 成功证明了Kahn的流猜想成立。

Conclusion: Kahn的流猜想得证，从而也确立了Chvátal猜想强形式成立。

Abstract: We prove Kahn's flow conjecture, which is a strong form of Chvátal's conjecture on the largest intersecting subfamily of a downset.

</details>


### [189] [Hilbert functions over the exterior algebra and f-vectors in higher rank I: Amata-Crupi monomial modules, Kozlov polytopes, and r-vectors of simplicial complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23605)
*Jan Snellman*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了一类分级自由模商模的Hilbert函数集合的凸包，它是Kozlov单纯形在秩r下的推广（Minkowski和），并给出自包含证明及新的刻面描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kozlov在1997年证明了Kozlov单纯形是n个顶点上单纯复形的f-向量的凸包（对应r=1情形）。本文希望将这一结果推广到秩r的一般情形，即分级自由E-模的单项子模商模的Hilbert函数集合的凸包，并补充文献中缺失的刻面描述。

Method: 设E为域上n个生成元的外代数，F为r个生成元、次数d_1≤…≤d_r的分级自由E-模。研究F/M（M为单项子模）的Hilbert函数集合的凸包，证明其为r个平移的Kozlov单纯形的Minkowski和。由局部Lubell-Yamamoto-Meshalkin不等式给出Kozlov定理的简短证明，并分离出使论证成立的子模类（理想直和模）。

Result: 证明凸包是r个Kozlov单纯形平移 copies 的Minkowski和，r=1时退化为Kozlov定理；给出Kozlov单纯形的显式刻面描述；刻画理想直和模类，并举反例说明该类之外的子模Hilbert函数未必是f-向量的平移和；用不含代数的语言单独陈述组合结果（涉及单纯复形的r-向量与ff-向量）；描述Minkowski和的顶点结构，对全1腿向量在所有秩及Kozlov腿向量在秩2时证明顶点存活答案。

Conclusion: 本文将Kozlov关于f-向量凸包的结果推广到秩r的分级自由外代数模情形，凸包为Kozlov单纯形的Minkowski和，并提供自包含证明、新的刻面描述、理想直和模的刻画以及Minkowski和顶点结构的初步结果。

Abstract: Let $E$ be the exterior algebra on $n$ generators over a field, and let $F$ be a graded free $E$-module with $r$ generators, of degrees $d_1 \le \dots \le d_r$. We determine the convex hull of the set of Hilbert functions of the quotients $F/M$, where $M$ runs over the monomial submodules of Amata and Crupi. The hull is the Minkowski sum of $r$ shifted copies of one and the same simplex: the Kozlov simplex, which Kozlov proved in 1997 to be the convex hull of the $f$-vectors of simplicial complexes on $n$ vertices. For $r=1$ the statement is Kozlov's theorem, so this is a rank-$r$ generalization of it. We give a self-contained proof, including a short proof of Kozlov's theorem from the local Lubell-Yamamoto-Meshalkin inequality, and an explicit facet description of the Kozlov simplex that appears not to be recorded in the literature. Along the way we isolate the class of submodules for which the argument works, the ideal-direct-sum modules, and show by example that the Hilbert function of a graded submodule outside that class need not be a shifted sum of $f$-vectors at all. The combinatorial content is stated separately, in language that uses no algebra, as a result on $r$-vectors of simplicial complexes and their $ff$-vectors. Finally we describe the vertex structure of the Minkowski sum -- which sums of vertices of the summands survive as vertices -- proving the answer for the all-ones leg vector at every rank and for the Kozlov leg vector at rank two.

</details>


### [190] [Sylvester simplices: Triangulations and Ehrhart-theoretic aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23710)
*Jhon B. Caicedo,Federico Castillo,Martina Juhnke,Germain Poullot*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Sylvester单纯形 $\mathsf{Sylv}_d^k$ 的Ehrhart理论性质，构造了flag、regular和unimodular三角剖分，证明其 $h^\ast$-向量单峰，确定部分 $f^\ast$-向量分量，并证明其在维数 $\leq 6$ 时满足Ehrhart magic positivity而在维数7时不满足，最后给出低维表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sylvester单纯形被猜想是所有具有恰好 $k$ 个内点格多胞形中体积最大者，更强地猜想其逐分量最大化 $h^\ast$-向量；然而其自身的Ehrhart理论性质鲜有研究，理解甚少，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造Sylvester单纯形的flag、regular与unimodular三角剖分；以此为基础研究其Ehrhart理论量，包括 $h^\ast$-向量、$f^\ast$-向量、local与boundary $h^\ast$-向量及Ehrhart多项式。

Result: 证明Sylvester单纯形的 $h^\ast$-向量是单峰的；显式确定 $f^\ast$-向量的部分分量取值；证明其Ehrhart magic positive在维数至6时成立但在维数7时不成立；给出维数7及以下Sylvester单纯形的Ehrhart理论量表格（格点数、Ehrhart多项式、local与boundary $h^\ast$-向量、$f^\ast$-向量）。

Conclusion: Sylvester单纯形具有良好Ehrhart理论结构（单峰 $h^\ast$-向量、三角剖分），但其Ehrhart magic positivity仅在低维成立，维数7构成反例。

Abstract: The Sylvester simplex $\mathsf{Sylv}_d^k$ is a $d$-dimensional lattice simplex with exactly $k$ interior lattice points. Sylvester simplices are conjectured to be the volume maximizers among all $d$-dimensional lattice polytopes with exactly $k$ interior lattice points for any $k\geq 1$. Even stronger, it is conjectured that they maximize (entry-wise) the $h^\ast$-vector among all $d$-dimensional lattice polytopes with exactly $k$ interior lattice points. Yet, Sylvester simplices seem to be rarely studied in their own right. In particular, their Ehrhart-theoretic properties are far from being well understood.
  In the present article, we tackle this problem. We describe flag, regular and unimodular triangulations for the Sylvester simplices, and prove that their $h^\ast$-vectors are unimodal. Moreover, we explicitly determine the values of some entries of their $f^\ast$-vectors, and prove that they are Ehrhart magic positive up to dimension $6$ but not in dimension $7$. We conclude by detailing tables of Ehrhart-theoretic quantities (numbers of lattice points, Ehrhart polynomials, local and boundary $h^\ast$-vectors, $f^\ast$-vectors) for Sylvester simplices of dimensions 7 and lower.

</details>


### [191] [A Phase Transition for Small Dense Subhypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23720)
*Peiru Kuang,Yan Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀超图中全局平均度如何迫使小规模稠密子超图出现，确定了阈值函数 $t_r(n,d,s)$ 在临界点 $s=r/(r-1)$ 处的相变行为，并在多个参数区间给出渐近紧的界，解决了 Feige–Wagner 问题及 Feige 的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极值组合学中的局部—全局原理长期关注局部结构与全局参数之间的关系。本文的动机是刻画全局密度（平均度至少为 $d$）如何强制 $r$-一致超图中出现小的稠密子超图（顶点数不超过 $t$、平均度至少为 $s$），并回答 Feige–Wagner 提出的、后被 Janzer–Sudakov–Tomon 重述为 Problem 3.3 的问题。

Method: 定义 $t_r(n,d,s)$ 为最小的整数 $t$，使得每个 $n$ 个顶点、平均度至少为 $d$ 的 $r$-图都包含一个顶点数不超过 $t$ 且平均度至少为 $s$ 的非空子超图。针对固定 $r\ge3$、$s>1$ 的情形，通过分析该量随参数变化的规律，揭示其在临界值 $s=\frac{r}{r-1}$ 处发生相变。

Result: 证明了 $t_r(n,d,s)$ 的行为在 $s=\frac{r}{r-1}$ 处存在相变，并确定了该函数在若干参数区间内的取值，得到渐近紧的界。特别地，当 $r=3$、$s=2$ 时，所得结果蕴含 Feige 的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文在相差多对数因子的意义下解决了 Feige–Wagner 问题（即 Janzer–Sudakov–Tomon 的 Problem 3.3），确立了均匀超图中局部稠密子超图阈值函数的相变图景，并统一推广了此前的相关猜想与结果。

Abstract: The local--global principle, which concerns the relationship between local structure and global parameters, has attracted considerable attention in extremal combinatorics over the past few decades. In this paper, we study how global density forces small dense subhypergraphs in uniform hypergraphs. For fixed $r\ge3$ and $s>1$, let $t_r(n,d,s)$ be the smallest integer $t$ such that every $n$-vertex $r$-graph of average degree at least $d$ contains a nonempty subhypergraph on at most $t$ vertices with average degree at least $s$. We show that the behavior of $t_r(n,d,s)$ undergoes a phase transition at $s=mainr/(r-1)$. We determine $t_r(n,d,s)$ and obtain asymptotically sharp bounds in several parameter regimes. This answers, up to polylogarithmic factors, a question of Feige and Wagner that was later restated as Problem~3.3 by Janzer, Sudakov and Tomon. In particular, when $r=3$ and $s=2$, our result implies a conjecture of Feige.

</details>


### [192] [Proof of Almkvist's conjecture on the unimodality of partition polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23779)
*Jianxi Mao,Wenle Shi,Bao-xuan Zhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Almkvist关于q-二项式系数型生成函数$F_{r,n}(q)$单峰性的猜想，即对所有偶数$r$及任意$n\ge1$、所有奇数$r$及任意$n\ge11$，$F_{r,n}(q)$均为单峰多项式，从而完全解决了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $F_{r,n}(q)$的系数计数的是每部分最多出现$r-1$次、部分大小不超过$n$的整数分拆，其单峰性是分拆理论中的经典问题。$r=2$的情形（即$\prod(1+q^k)$）已由Hughes证明，并有Stanley的代数方法与Odlyzko、Richmond的分析方法等不同证明。Almkvist随后猜想一般$r$的情形单峰，并验证了$3\le r\le20$以及$r=100,101$，但一般情形长期未决。

Method: 论文对一般参数$r$和$n$下的生成函数$F_{r,n}(q)=\prod_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1-q^{rk}}{1-q^k}$的单峰性进行证明，通过（代数或分析）手段处理Almkvist猜想所涵盖的两类情形：偶数$r$与任意$n\ge1$，以及奇数$r$与$n\ge11$，并覆盖此前未被验证的无限多参数取值。

Result: 证明了Almkvist猜想在全部情形下成立：对任意偶数$r$和任意$n\ge1$，以及任意奇数$r$和任意$n\ge11$，$F_{r,n}(q)$都是单峰的。该结果将此前有限范围的验证推广到所有$r,n$，完全解决了猜想。

Conclusion: Almkvist关于$F_{r,n}(q)$单峰性的猜想被完全证明，$r=2$的经典结果成为其特例，分拆生成函数单峰性问题的这一长期开放情形得到彻底解决。

Abstract: For integers $r\ge2$ and $n\ge1$, let $$ F_{r,n}(q)=\prod_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1-q^{rk}}{1-q^k}. $$ The coefficient of $q^j$ in \(F_{r,n}(q)\) counts partitions of $j$ into parts at most $n$, each occurring at most $r-1$ times. Hughes proved that $F_{2,n}(q)=\prod_{k=1}^{n}(1+q^k)$ is unimodal for every $n\ge 1$. This result was reproved by Stanley using an algebraic approach and Odlyzko and Richmond using an analytic approach. Almkvist conjectured that $F_{r,n}(q)$ is unimodal in the following two cases: every even $r$ and every $n\ge 1$; every odd $r$ and every $n\ge 11$. He proved that this conjecture is true for $3\le r \le 20$ and $r=100,101$. In this paper, we completely settle the conjecture.

</details>


### [193] [Sensitivity and Block Sensitivity of Elementary Symmetric Boolean Functions of Arbitrary Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23781)
*Yuan Li,Jing Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了初等对称布尔函数 σ_{n,d} 的 sensitivity、平均 sensitivity 与 block sensitivity，并刻画了 sensitivity、block sensitivity 与 certificate complexity 之间的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初等对称布尔函数 σ_{n,d} 的各类复杂度度量此前缺乏完整、统一的确定；同时 sensitivity、block sensitivity 与 certificate complexity 之间的层级关系在对称函数上尚未被完全分类，作者希望弥补这一空白。

Method: 利用 Lucas 定理给出 Hamming 权重值序列的统一二进制描述，由此导出 sensitivity 与平均 sensitivity 公式，并将 block sensitivity 的计算归约为至多四个显式候选；结合任意次数的 certificate complexity 公式进行分析；并证明关于一般对称布尔函数的通用不等式 bs(f) ≤ max{s(f), C(f)-1}。

Result: 对每个 1 ≤ d ≤ n 完全确定了 σ_{n,d} 的 sensitivity、平均 sensitivity 与 block sensitivity；证明任意非恒定对称布尔函数满足 bs(f) ≤ max{s(f), C(f)-1}，故仅可能出现三种模式 s=bs=C、s=bs<C、s<bs<C；对 σ_{n,d} 给出三种模式各自的充要条件，并给出严格层级 s(σ_{n,d})<bs(σ_{n,d})<C(σ_{n,d}) 的充要刻画及无穷多个满足该层级的函数族。

Conclusion: 文章完成了初等对称布尔函数复杂度度量的完整确定与分类，建立了对称函数上 sensitivity、block sensitivity 与 certificate complexity 的通用约束与层级结构，为相关复杂度度量的关系研究提供了统一的刻画工具。

Abstract: Let $σ_{n,d}$ denote the elementary symmetric Boolean function of $n$ variables and degree $d$. We completely determine the sensitivity, average sensitivity, and block sensitivity of $σ_{n,d}$ for every $1\le d\le n$. Using Lucas' theorem, we obtain a uniform binary description of the Hamming-weight value sequence, from which the sensitivity and average-sensitivity formulas follow and the computation of block sensitivity reduces to at most four explicit candidates. Combining these results with the arbitrary-degree formula for certificate complexity, we determine the exact relations among sensitivity, block sensitivity, and certificate complexity.
  We also prove a general result for symmetric Boolean functions: every nonconstant symmetric Boolean function $f$ satisfies \[
  \bs(f)\le \max\{s(f),C(f)-1\}. \] Consequently, only the three patterns \[
  s=\bs=C,\qquad s=\bs<C,\qquad s<\bs<C \] can occur for nonconstant symmetric Boolean functions. For elementary symmetric Boolean functions, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for each of these three patterns, thereby completely classifying the relations among $s(σ_{n,d})$, $\bs(σ_{n,d})$, and $C(σ_{n,d})$. In particular, we obtain a necessary and sufficient characterization of the full strict hierarchy \[
  s(σ_{n,d})<\bs(σ_{n,d})<C(σ_{n,d}), \] and exhibit infinite families for which it holds.

</details>


### [194] [On the majority game chromatic number of forests and other graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23803)
*Yash Chawda,Saraswati Girish Nanoti,Brahadeesh Sankarnarayanan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究多数着色的博弈版本，改进了无环图的最大度限制下的博弈多数色数上界，并首次系统研究了该博弈的计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数着色（unfriendly partition）在静态情形下色数有界，但其二人Maker-Breaker博弈版本μ_g(G)一般无界。Bosek等证明μ_g(G) ≤ col_g(G)，而无环图col_g(G) ≤ 4，故μ_g(G) ≤ 4。作者希望改进这一上界，并填补该博弈计算复杂性研究的空白。

Method: 通过构造性博弈策略分析，对最大度不超过4的无环图证明μ_g(G) ≤ 3；对路径、星图、完全图证明μ_g(G) ≤ 2。并采用归约方法研究预先着色扩展问题的复杂性及其博弈版本。

Result: 证明任意最大度不超过4的无环图满足μ_g(G) ≤ 3；路径、星图、完全图满足μ_g(G) ≤ 2，改进Bosek等的结果。复杂性方面：以χ(G)种颜色的多数着色预着色扩展问题是NP完全的，其博弈版本是PSPACE完全的；即使仅用2种颜色，两者仍分别为NP完全和PSPACE完全。

Conclusion: 该工作收紧了几类图的博弈多数色数上界，并开创了多数着色博弈的计算复杂性研究，表明即使在2色情形下问题依然高度困难。

Abstract: A majority coloring (also called an unfriendly partition) of a graph $G$ is a vertex coloring of $G$ in which no vertex has more than half of its neighbors colored with its own color. The least number of colors required for a majority coloring of $G$ is the majority chromatic number $μ(G)$.
  The majority coloring game, introduced by Bosek--Grytczuk--Jakóbczak (2019), is a two-player Maker--Breaker-type game where the players alternately color vertices while maintaining the majority condition at each vertex. The least number of colors required for the first player to have a winning strategy on $G$ is the majority game chromatic number $μ_g(G)$. In contrast with the static case, Bosek et al. show that $μ_g(G)$ is unbounded in general, while $μ_g(G) \le \mathrm{col}_g(G)$, where $\mathrm{col}_g(G)$ is the game coloring number of $G$.
  It is known that for any acyclic graph $G$, $\mathrm{col}_g(G) \le 4$, and hence $μ_g(G) \le 4$. We improve this bound by showing that $μ_g(G) \le 3$ for any acyclic graph $G$ of maximum degree at most $4$. We also show that $μ_g(G) \le 2$ if $G$ is a path, a star, or a complete graph, improving results of Bosek et al.
  We also initiate the study of the computational complexity of the majority coloring game. We show that the pre-coloring extension problem for majority coloring on $G$ with a palette of $χ(G)$ colors is NP-complete, and that its game version is PSPACE-complete. Furthermore, the problem remains NP-complete, and its game version remains PSPACE-complete, even with a palette of $2$ colors.

</details>


### [195] [Edge complexity of weighted graphs: involutory symmetries and NP-hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23842)
*Vishal Gupta,Alex Iosevich*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将加权图的边复杂度（邻接矩阵Fourier ℓ¹/ℓ²范数比的最小值）与图的对称性联系起来：引入足够大权重的通用顶点给出显式Fourier ℓ¹下界，等号成立当且仅当原图具有无不动点的对合自同构；并由此证明加权边复杂度的加性近似是NP难，判定存在实Fourier标号是NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边复杂度是一个组合优化量，其最小值难以刻画与计算。作者希望找到该最小值的结构性刻画，并确定其计算复杂性，从而理解该量在算法上是否可有效逼近。

Method: 通过给原图添加一个权重足够大的通用顶点，构造出显式Fourier ℓ¹下界；利用该下界证明等号成立等价于原图存在无不动点对合自同构，并给出双侧估计，将超出该下界的量与到最近具此类对称性的加权图的Frobenius距离平方相比较。对加权度数为常数的源图，给出权重趋于无穷时的精确主项。此外采用归约方法证明简单源图情形下的更强分离及实数Fourier标号判定问题。

Result: （1）通用顶点构造给出显式下界，等号恰对应无不动点对合自同构；（2）双侧估计在源图加权度数为常数时确定权重趋于无穷的精确主项；（3）对简单源图，证明加权边复杂度的加性1/(256 N^{7/2})近似是NP难的，即使在奇数阶N的连通图、至多两种不同正整数权重且每个权重不超过N^2时亦然；（4）判定一个简单图是否存在实Fourier标号是NP完全的；（5）给出一个七顶点例子，说明即使存在实Fourier标号，每个最小化标号仍可能具有非实Fourier系数，并在附录中给出精确有理证书。

Conclusion: 加权边复杂度的最小值由一种图对称性（无不动点对合自同构）精确刻画，超出该下界的程度可由到最近具有该对称性的图的Frobenius距离控制，进而导致强不可近似性结果；同时，Fourier标号的实性判定是NP完全的，且最小化标号不必是实的。

Abstract: The edge complexity of a weighted graph is the smallest ratio of the Fourier $\ell^1$ and $\ell^2$ norms of its adjacency matrix over all vertex labelings. We study the difficulty of finding this minimum by relating it to a graph symmetry. Adding a universal vertex with sufficiently large incident weight produces an explicit Fourier $\ell^1$ lower bound. We show that equality holds exactly when the source graph has a fixed-point-free involutory automorphism. Two-sided estimates compare the excess above this bound with the squared Frobenius distance to the nearest weighted graph having such a symmetry. For sources of constant weighted degree, these estimates determine the exact leading term as the added weight tends to infinity. A stronger separation for simple source graphs proves that additive $\frac{1}{256N^{\frac{7}{2}}}$ approximation of weighted edge complexity is NP-hard, even on connected graphs of odd order $N$ with at most two distinct positive integer weights, each at most $N^2$. We also prove that recognizing a simple graph with a real Fourier labeling is NP-complete. A seven-vertex example shows that every minimizing labeling can have nonreal Fourier coefficients even when real Fourier labelings exist. An exact rational certificate for this example is included in the appendix.

</details>


### [196] [$1$-cross intersecting set pair systems are small](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23891)
*Dylan King,Van Magnan,Cory Palmer*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了1-cross intersecting set pair systems的最大规模，证明了Füredi、Gyárfás和Király提出的猜想：m(n,n,1)/C(2n,n) → 0 as n → ∞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于组合数学中set pair systems的极值问题，特别是Füredi、Gyárfás和Király在2023年提出的关于1-cross intersecting set pair systems规模的猜想，旨在解决该猜想并深化对这类系统渐近性质的理解。

Method: 论文通过数学证明方法，针对1-cross intersecting set pair systems，在|A_i| ≤ a和|B_i| ≤ b的约束下，分析m(a,b,1)的上界，并利用组合计数和渐近分析证明m(n,n,1)/C(2n,n)的极限为0。

Result: 证明了猜想成立：m(n,n,1)/C(2n,n) → 0 as n → ∞，即1-cross intersecting set pair systems的最大规模相对于中心二项式系数C(2n,n)是渐近可忽略的。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了Füredi、Gyárfás和Király的猜想，为set pair systems的极值理论提供了重要进展。

Abstract: A set pair system $\{(A_i,B_i)\}_{i=1}^m$ is $1$-cross intersecting if $|A_i \cap B_i|=0$ for all $i$ and $|A_i \cap B_j| = 1$ whenever $i \neq j$. Let $m(a,b,1)$ denote the maximum size $m$ of a $1$-cross intersecting set pair system $\{(A_i,B_i)\}_{i=1}^m$ where $|A_i| \leq a$ and $|B_i| \leq b$ for all $i$. We prove a conjecture of Füredi, Gyárfás, and Király [Combin. Probab. Comput. 32 (2023)] that asserts $m(n,n,1)/\binom{2n}{n} \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$.

</details>


### [197] [Rényi stability of $B_h$ sets: a two-order phase diagram and sharp deletion principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23922)
*Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究 $B_h$ 集合（$h$ 项和唯一表示）在加权扰动下的稳定性问题，完整刻画了 Rényi 碰撞阶 $\alpha$ 与预算阶 $\beta$ 构成的 $(\alpha,\beta)$ 平面上的删除问题。稳定性恰好在 $\beta\le 1$ 且 $\alpha\ge \beta$ 时成立：区域内部最优删除率为多项式，边界 $\beta=1$ 处为对数且主常数精确；区域外通过超临界预算与轻原子稀释两种机制失效，并精确计算极限缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于 $B_h$ 集合的加权删除问题只考虑了单阶 formulation，即令预算阶与碰撞阶相等（对角线 $\beta=\alpha$），无法区分“熵损失”与“权重分散程度”这两个本质不同的控制参数。本文旨在打破这一限制，在完整的 $(\alpha,\beta)$ 平面上确定稳定性区域、最优删除率及失效机制，从而给出更精细的稳定性刻画。

Method: 引入两个独立的 Rényi 阶：碰撞阶 $\alpha$（度量 $h$ 重和映射的熵损失）与预算阶 $\beta$（控制权重的分散程度）。在此框架下分析加权删除问题，确定稳定性成立的条件 $\beta\le 1$ 且 $\alpha\ge \beta$。主要工具是一个具有最优常数的 sharp list-coarsening 不等式，用于推导上界；并构造匹配的下界例子说明相界与速率的紧性。此外推广到 $B_h[g]$ 集合。

Result: 稳定性区域恰为 $\beta\le 1$ 且 $\alpha\ge \beta$。区域内最优删除率：当 $\beta<1$ 时为多项式速率，在边界 $\beta=1$ 时为对数速率且首项常数精确。区域外稳定性失效，通过两种不同机制：超临界预算与轻原子稀释，并分别精确计算其极限缺陷。上界由 sharp list-coarsening 不等式（最优常数）给出，该不等式还导出一个无熵删除定理、关于表示函数矩的有限组合推论，以及对 $B_h[g]$ 集合的推广。匹配构造证明相界与速率均尖锐。

Conclusion: 论文在 $(\alpha,\beta)$ 平面上完全解决了 $B_h$ 集合的加权删除稳定性问题：稳定性区域为 $\beta\le 1,\ \alpha\ge\beta$，并给出区域内精确的最优删除率（内部多项式、边界对数）与区域外两种失效机制及精确极限缺陷。核心工具为带最优常数的 list-coarsening 不等式，附带多项推论并推广至 $B_h[g]$ 集合。

Abstract: A set $B$ in an abelian group is a $B_h$ set if every $h$-term sum has a unique representation up to permutation; for $h=2$ these are the Sidon sets. We study a weighted removal problem for this collision-free property: if the $h$-fold sum map has small Rényi entropy loss, how much probability mass must be deleted to leave a $B_h$ support? Two Rényi orders naturally arise: a collision order $α$, measuring the entropy loss, and a budget order $β$, controlling how spread out the weighting may be. Existing one-order formulations tie the two together on the diagonal $β=α$. We determine the resulting stability problem on the full $(α,β)$-plane. Stability holds exactly when $β\le1$ and $α\geβ$. Inside this region the optimal deletion rate is polynomial for $β<1$ and logarithmic on the boundary $β=1$, where the leading constant is exact; outside it, stability fails through two distinct mechanisms: a supercritical budget and dilution by light atoms. In each case the limiting defect is computed exactly. The upper bounds follow from a sharp list-coarsening inequality with optimal constant, which also yields an entropy-free removal theorem, a finite combinatorial consequence for moments of the representation function, and extensions to $B_h[g]$ sets. Matching constructions show that the phase boundaries and rates are sharp.

</details>


### [198] [Turán problems with bounded matching number in $k$-uniform hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24353)
*Jialin Liu,Mingyang Guo,Xiumei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了含匹配约束的超图Turán型极值问题，确定了图情形下 $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,F\})$ 在 $s$ 接近 $n/2$ 区间的新结果，并给出 $k$-一致超图中 $\ex_k(n,\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k\cup\{M_{s+1}^k\})$ 与 $\ex_k(n,\{M_{s+1}^k,H_{\ell+1}^k\})$ 在特定参数范围内的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon–Frankl 与 Gerbner 近期确定了 $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,F\})$ 在 $n$ 充分大等情形下的值，但对匹配参数 $s$ 的不同取值区间（特别是 $s$ 接近 $n/2$ 或 $n/k$ 的区间）尚缺乏结果。本文旨在补全这些参数范围，并将问题从图推广到 $k$-一致超图。

Method: 针对图情形，利用极值集合论与稳定性分析，证明对满足 $\chi(F)>3$ 的图 $F$，存在常数 $\beta>0$ 与 $s_0$，使得在 $\max\{s_0,n/2-\beta n\}<s<n/2$ 时 $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,F\})=\ex_2(2s+1,F)$。对超图情形，定义由 $(\ell+1)$-集合所有点对被边覆盖的 $k$-图族 $\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k$ 及其成员 $H_{\ell+1}^k$，通过构造与稳定性论证确定相应极值数。

Result: 证明了图情形：对任意 $\chi(F)>3$ 的图 $F$，当 $\max\{s_0,n/2-\beta n\}<s<n/2$ 时 $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,F\})=\ex_2(2s+1,F)$。超图情形：对 $\ell\ge k\ge3$，确定了 $s\le n/[8(k-1)^{k-2}(\ell-1)^3]$ 时 $\ex_k(n,\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k\cup\{M_{s+1}^k\})$ 的值；并对充分大的 $s$，分别在 $n/k-\beta n<s<n/k$ 与 $s\le n/[8(k-1)^{k-2}(\ell-1)^3]$ 时确定了 $\ex_k(n,\{M_{s+1}^k,H_{\ell+1}^k\})$。

Conclusion: 论文在匹配参数的不同范围内推广并补全了 Alon–Frankl 与 Gerbner 关于禁匹配与禁图/禁超图并集的 Turán 数结果，给出了图情形 $s$ 接近 $n/2$ 以及超图情形 $\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k$、$H_{\ell+1}^k$ 与匹配并集的极值数精确表达式。

Abstract: For a family $\mathcal{F}$ of $k$-graphs,
  $\ex_k(n,\mathcal{F})$ denotes the maximum number of edges in an $n$-vertex
  $\mathcal{F}$-free $k$-graph. Let $M_{s+1}^k$ denote a matching of size $s+1$ in $k$-uniform hypergraphs. Recently, Alon and Frankl (JCTB, 2024) determined $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,K_{\ell+1}\})$
  for all $n\geq 2s+1$ and $\ell\geq 2$. For every non-bipartite graph $F$, Gerbner (JGT, 2024) determined $\ex_2(n,\{M_{s+1}^2,F\})$ for sufficiently large $n$. In this paper, we investigate this problem for different ranges of the matching parameter. First we prove that for every graph $F$ with $χ(F)>3$, there exist constants $β>0$ and $s_0$ such that $\ex_k(n,\{M^2_{s+1}, F\})=\ex_2(2s+1,F)$ for $\max\{s_0,n/2-βn\}<s<n/2$.
  For integers $\ell\ge k\ge3$, let $\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k$ be the family of
  all $k$-graphs $F$ with at most $\binom{\ell+1}{2}$ edges for which there is
  an $(\ell+1)$-set $L$ such that every pair of vertices of $L$ is covered by
  an edge of $F$, and let $H_{\ell+1}^k$ be the $k$-uniform hypergraph obtained from the complete graph $K_{\ell+1}$ by enlarging each edge with a set of $k-2$ new vertices, which is a member of $\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k$.
  We determine $\ex_k\bigl(n,\mathcal{K}_{\ell+1}^k\cup\{M_{s+1}^k\}\bigr)$
  for $s\leq \frac{n}{8(k-1)^{k-2}(\ell-1)^3}$. For sufficiently large $s$, we also determine $\ex_k\bigl(n,\{M_{s+1}^k,H_{\ell+1}^k\}\bigr)$
  for $\frac{n}{k}-βn<s<\frac{n}{k}$ and $s\leq \frac{n}{8(k-1)^{k-2}(\ell-1)^3}$, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [How to Construct High Barrycades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24373)
*Jakub Binięda,Michał Dębski,Grzegorz Gutowski,Mateusz Milewski*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了barrycade与corral构造问题，给出了高度h、阶n=2h+3的显式构造与随机启发式求解装置，将barrycade猜想最优阶n=2h-2的验证范围推进到h≤50，并验证corral在n=2h-1及Tomoki Nakamigawa关于循环群良分散划分的猜想（部分数≤20）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: barrycade问题由Barry Cipra提出、Richard K. Guy命名，要求h个1..n的排列的所有真部分和两两不同。简单计数表明仅当n≥2h-2时可能可行，且猜想对任意高度h≥2、阶n≥2h-2均存在解，但此前缺乏系统构造与大范围验证；相关工作corral（排列构成循环排列）及循环群良分散划分猜想也待推进。

Method: 1）对任意高度h≥1，显式构造阶n=2h+3的barrycade；2）设计随机启发式算法，在h≤50范围内搜索猜想最优阶n=2h-2的barrycade；3）对相关corral问题，对任意h≥1显式构造阶n=2h的corral，并采用类似启发式搜索猜想最优阶n=2h-1的corral；4）利用该启发式验证Tomoki Nakamigawa关于循环群良分散划分的猜想（部分数≤20）。

Result: 1）对每个h≥1，得到高度h、阶n=2h+3的barrycade构造；2）对每个2≤h≤50，启发式找到高度h、阶n=2h-2的barrycade，从而证实该范围内猜想最优阶成立；3）对每个h≥1，得到高度h、阶n=2h的corral构造；4）对每个1≤h≤50，启发式找到高度h、阶n=2h-1的corral；5）验证了Nakamigawa关于循环群良分散划分的猜想在部分数不超过20时成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造与随机启发式，大幅推进了barrycade与corral问题最优阶猜想的验证范围，并支持循环群良分散划分猜想。但h≤50之外的barrycade猜想与部分数更大的划分猜想仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Given two positive integers height $h$ and order $n$, the barrycade construction problem asks for a set of $h$ permutations of the integers from $1$ to $n$ such that all the proper partial sums given by these permutations are pairwise distinct. The name barrycade was coined by Richard K. Guy and refers to Barry Cipra, who introduced this kind of arrangement problem. A simple calculation shows that a solution can only exist for $n \ge 2h-2$ and it is conjectured that there always exists a solution for every height $h \ge 2$ and order $n \ge 2h-2$.
  In this work, for every height $h \ge 1$, we present a construction of a barrycade of height $h$ and order $n = 2h+3$. We also present a randomized heuristic that allows us to find a barrycade of height $h$ and conjectured optimal order $n=2h-2$, for every height $2 \leq h \leq 50$. Thus, we confirm the conjectured optimal order for all heights up to $50$.
  We also consider a related corral construction problem, where the permutations define a cyclic arrangement. In this setting, for every height $h \ge 1$, we present a construction of a corral of height $h$ and order $n=2h$. A heuristic approach, similar to the one used for barrycades, allows us to find a corral of height $h$ and conjectured optimal order $n=2h-1$, for every height $1 \leq h \leq 50$. We confirm Tomoki Nakamigawa's conjecture on well-dispersed partitions of cyclic groups for the number of parts up to $20$.

</details>


### [200] [Completing the Existence Problem for Integer Relative Heffter Arrays $\mathrm{H}_k(n;k)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24399)
*Lorenzo Mella*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了整数相对Heffter数组 $\mathrm{H}_k(n;k)$ 存在性问题的遗留情形：对任意 $n\equiv 0 \pmod{4}$、$n\geq 12$ 构造出 $\mathrm{H}_5(n;5)$，从而完全解决了该存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heffter数组是2015年由Archdeacon引入的组合结构，应用于循环圈系统和图在曲面上的双嵌入。Costa等人在2020年提出的相对Heffter数组推广了经典概念。他们解决了 $k\neq 5$ 时的存在性问题，但 $k=5$ 且 $n\equiv 0\pmod{4}$ 的情形除 $n=8,16$ 两个零散例子外仍悬而未决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过组合构造方法，给出任意 $n\equiv 0 \pmod{4}$、$n\geq 12$ 情形下整数相对Heffter数组 $\mathrm{H}_5(n;5)$ 的显式构造。

Result: 对每个 $n\equiv 0 \pmod{4}$、$n\geq 12$ 成功构造出 $\mathrm{H}_5(n;5)$。

Conclusion: 整数相对Heffter数组 $\mathrm{H}_k(n;k)$ 的存在性问题被完全解决。

Abstract: Heffter arrays, introduced by Archdeacon in 2015, are combinatorial structures with applications to cyclic cycle systems and biembeddings of graphs on surfaces. Costa, Morini, Pasotti and Pellegrini proposed in 2020 the notion of relative Heffter arrays as a generalization of classical Heffter arrays, inspired by the concept of relative difference families. In their work, the existence problem of integer relative Heffter arrays $\mathrm{H}_k(n;k)$ was solved for all $k\neq 5$, while the case $k=5$ and $n \equiv 0 \pmod{4}$ remained open, apart from two sporadic examples with $n=8,16$. In this article, we consider this open problem and we construct an $\mathrm{H}_5(n;5)$ for every $n\equiv 0 \pmod{4}$, $n\geq 12$. As a consequence, the existence problem of integer relative $\mathrm{H}_k(n;k)$ is completely settled.

</details>


### [201] [Sharp connectivity thresholds for mixed rigidity packings and improved bounds for highly connected orientations of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24463)
*Hanzhi Bai,Jørgen Bang-Jensen,Jin Yan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了关于刚性生成子图打包的统一定理：任意正整数 d_1,…,d_s，每个 ∑d_i(d_i+1)-连通图都包含两两边不交的生成子图 H_i，使 H_i 为 d_i-刚性；在 ∑d_i(d_i+1)≥4 时该连通度界是紧的。由此统一解决多个猜想，并改进 Thomassen 猜想相关的连通定向上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Garamvölgyi、Jordán、Király 与 Villányi 提出两个关于刚性生成子图打包的紧连通度猜想：等维情形，以及一个 d-刚性生成子图与一棵生成树的打包。作者希望给出统一框架，同时解决这些猜想并推进 Thomassen 关于高连通图定向的猜想。

Method: 证明一个统一定理：对任意正整数 d_1,…,d_s，每个 ∑_{i=1}^s d_i(d_i+1)-连通图包含两两边不交的生成子图 H_1,…,H_s，其中 H_i 为 d_i-刚性。方法上结合紧刚性打包结果，并利用删除两个边不交（充分）刚性图后剩余边的性质来改进 f(q) 的二次上界。

Result: 定理在 ∑d_i(d_i+1)≥4 时连通度界紧；作为特例解决两个猜想，并证实 Garamvölgyi、Jordán、Király 的猜想：每个 tk(k+1)-连通图含 t 个两两边不交的 k-连通生成子图；给出打包一个 d-刚性生成子图与 r 棵两两边不交生成树的紧阈值 d(d+1)+2r。对 Thomassen 猜想，得到 f(q)≤(25q^2+41q-16)/2（q≥3）与 f(q)≤8q^2+212q+1404=(8+o(1))q^2（充分大 q），将先前二次界首项系数从 320 降至 25/2 与 8。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一的刚性生成子图打包定理，统一并解决了若干连通度猜想，同时显著改进了高连通图存在 q-连通定向所需连通度的二次上界，利用剩余边进一步优化了 Garamvölgyi 等人的结果。

Abstract: Garamvölgyi, Jordán, Király and Villányi [{{\bf Forum Math. Pi} \textbf{13} (2025), Paper No.~e11}] posed two sharp connectivity conjectures for packing rigid spanning subgraphs: one for the equal-dimensional case and the other for the packing of a $d$-rigid spanning subgraph with a spanning tree. We prove a unified theorem: for arbitrary positive integers $d_1,\ldots,d_s$, every $\sum_{i=1}^{s}d_i(d_i+1)$-connected graph contains pairwise edge-disjoint spanning subgraphs $H_1,\ldots,H_s$ such that $H_i$ is $d_i$-rigid for every $i$. The connectivity bound is sharp whenever $\sum_{i=1}^{s}d_i(d_i+1)\ge4$.
  As special cases, the theorem settles both conjectures, confirms the conjecture of Garamvölgyi, Jordán and Király [{\bf J. Combin. Theory Ser. B} \textbf{166} (2024), 1--29] that every $tk(k+1)$-connected graph contains $t$ pairwise edge-disjoint $k$-connected spanning subgraphs, and gives the sharp threshold $d(d+1)+2r$ for packing one $d$-rigid spanning subgraph together with $r$ pairwise edge-disjoint spanning trees.
  We also obtain two upper bounds related to Thomassen's conjecture on highly connected orientations of graphs. If $f(q)$ is the least integer such that every $f(q)$-connected graph has a $q$-connected orientation, then $f(q)\le(25q^2+41q-16)/2$ for every $q\ge3$ and $f(q)\le8q^2+212q+1404=(8+o(1))q^2$ for all sufficiently large $q$; these two results reduce the leading coefficient in the previous quadratic bound from $320$ to $25/2$ for every $q\ge3$ and $8$ for all sufficiently large $q$. Compared to the bound for $f(q)$ obtained by Garamvölgyi et al. we obtain the better bounds, not only through our tight rigidity result but also by exploiting the leftover edges when we remove two edge-disjoint spanning (sufficiently) rigid graphs.

</details>


### [202] [Positive formulas for q-Zeta numerators of Ferrers-cell posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24541)
*Qihang Wang,Weiye Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出Ferrers细胞偏序集及其最小增广格的Chapoton q-Zeta分子显式正公式，经构造性带符号EL-标号化为边界容许路径词的下降计数，并用有限转移矩阵递归恢复多元下降集多项式；对梯形边界给出Gaussian二项式描述、q=1时的Jacobi多项式变换及渐近结果，并应用于A_r、B_r、C_r型正根偏序集，得到全秩系数正性与若干精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Chapoton提出的q-Zeta分子在Ferrers细胞偏序集上的正性与组合解释问题，并将其推广至区间与最小增广格，同时统一处理A_r、B_r、C_r型标准正根偏序集的特殊化情形，以建立系数正性、下降集多项式与渐近行为之间的联系。

Method: 对Ferrers细胞偏序集F_b及其区间构造带符号EL-标号，将分子表示为边界容许路径词的下降枚举式；使用有限转移矩阵递归计算完整多元下降集多项式；对梯形边界借助Gaussian二项式公式刻画各区间与t-切片；在q=1处通过Jacobi多项式变换分析零点、交错与极限分布；并利用生成函数与增长速率分析推广至A_r、B_r、C_r型。

Result: 得到Ferrers细胞偏序集及其最小增广格各区间q-Zeta分子的显式正公式与多元下降集多项式；梯形边界下每个区间和t-切片有Gaussian二项式公式；q=1时出现简单负零点、严格固定偏移交错、显式反正弦推前极限，并得到代数生成函数与增长速率(1+sqrt t)^2；在B_r、C_r型上获得全秩系数正性、反向投票公式、[t^k]H_{P_r,rk}(1,t)=C(r-1,k)^2及具有唯一首项的尖锐切片次数。

Conclusion: 该工作以构造性带符号EL-标号和转移矩阵递归为核心工具，系统解决了Ferrers细胞偏序集及A_r、B_r、C_r型正根偏序集的Chapoton q-Zeta分子的正性与组合结构问题，统一了格论、下降统计与特殊函数视角。论文另披露其主要结果通过生成式AI工作流获得，并经过后续证明与引文审查。

Abstract: We give explicit positive formulas for Chapoton's $q$-Zeta numerators of the Ferrers-cell posets $F_{\mathbf b}=\{(i,c):1\leq i\leq r, i\leq c\leq b_i\}$, where $b_1\geq\cdots\geq b_r\geq r$, and for every interval of their minimum-augmented lattices. A constructive signed EL-labelling expresses the numerator as a descent enumerator over boundary-admissible path words. A finite transfer-matrix recursion recovers the full multivariate descent-set polynomial.
  For trapezoidal boundaries, Gaussian-binomial formulas describe every interval and every $t$-slice. At $q=1$, a Jacobi-polynomial transform gives simple negative zeros, strict fixed-offset interlacing, and an explicit arcsine push-forward limit. We also obtain algebraic fixed-offset generating functions and the growth rate $(1+\sqrt t)^2$ for $t\geq0$.
  The standard positive-root posets of types $A_r$, $B_r$, and $C_r$ are specializations, graded by root height minus one with Chapoton's fixed denominator. For types $B_r$ and $C_r$, this yields all-rank coefficientwise positivity, the reversed-ballot formula, the specialization $[t^k]\mathbb{H}_{P_r,\operatorname{rk}}(1,t)=\binom{r-1}{k}^2$, and sharp slice degrees with unique leading monomials.
  The main results of this paper were obtained through a generative-AI workflow using OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol, Anthropic Claude Fable 5, and Grok 4.6. OpenAI GPT-6 Astra was used for subsequent proof and citation review and manuscript revision. Further details appear in the disclosure at the end of the paper.

</details>


### [203] [On splitting properties of the stability problem with integer choice functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24581)
*Alexander V. Karzanov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究稳定广义分配模型，通过分裂技术将其稳定解构成的分配格嵌入稳定匹配格与稳定分配格中，推广了Fleiner在全单位容量下的分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alkan-Gale模型（整数版稳定广义分配）的稳定解集S_{G,b,C}虽构成分配格，但该格结构复杂、难以构造和操作。作者希望将其表示为更简单的稳定性模型（稳定匹配格或稳定分配格），以简化其结构与分析。

Method: 作者采用一种分裂（splitting）技术，将带有边容量b和满足替代性与尺寸单调性公理的选择函数C的稳定广义分配模型，嵌入到稳定匹配格中，并更紧凑地嵌入到Baiou-Balinski稳定分配模型的格中，作为其子格。

Result: 证明了S_{G,b,C}可作为一个子格嵌入稳定匹配格，并可更紧凑地嵌入稳定分配格（Baiou-Balinski模型）。这一结果推广了Fleiner关于全单位容量情形下的分离（detachment）结论。

Conclusion: 稳定广义分配的格结构可由更简单的稳定匹配与稳定分配模型表示，从而为构造和操作该格提供了简化途径，并统一推广了已有的分离结果。

Abstract: We consider the integer version of Alkan--Gale's model on stability in a two-sided market, called the stable generalized allocation one. It is given by a triple $(G,b,C)$, where $G=(V,E)$ is a finite bipartite graph with nonnegative integer capacities $b(e)\in{\mathbb Z}_+$ of edges $e\in E$, and for each vertex (``agent'') $v\in V$, the preferences on the set $E_v$ of its incident edges depend on a choice function $C_v$. The latter acts on the set of vectors in ${\mathbb Z}_+^{E_v}$ bounded by the capacities and obeys the standard axioms of substitutability and size monotonicity. Alkan--Gale's prominent theorem implies that the stability problem in this case always has a stable solution $x\in{\mathbb Z}_+^E$ and, moreover, the set ${\cal S}_{G,b,C}$ of these solutions (``stable generalized allocations'') forms a distributive lattice.
  However, this lattice is rather intricate to construct and work with, and we wonder whether it can be represented via a ``simpler'' stability model. Answering this issue, we arrange a sort of splitting techniques to embed ${\cal S}_{G,b,C}$, as a sublattice, in the lattice of stable matchings and, more compactly, in the lattice of stable allocations (as in Baiou--Balinski's stability model). This generalizes Fleiner's result on a detachment in the special case with all-unit capacities.
  Keywords: stable marriage, stable allocation, choice function, rotation, distributive lattice, poset representation

</details>


### [204] [Symmetric Quantum Latin Squares with Maximal Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24653)
*Yiwen Zhang,Haitao Cao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对所有 n≥6，存在具有最大基数的对称量子拉丁方；对所有 k≥1，存在具有最大基数的对称幂等量子拉丁方（阶为 4k+2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子拉丁方是经典拉丁方在量子信息情境下的推广，其对称性、幂等性及基数等组合性质是该领域的核心研究问题。此前对这些特殊类型量子拉丁方的存在性（尤其是达到最大基数者）尚缺乏一般性结论，本文旨在填补这一空白。
