Method: 采用构造性方法：对任意 n≥6 给出最大基数对称量子拉丁方的显式构造；对阶为 4k+2（k≥1）的情形给出最大基数对称幂等量子拉丁方的构造。

Result: 证明了两个存在性定理：(1) 对所有 n≥6，存在最大基数的对称量子拉丁方；(2) 对所有 k≥1，存在最大基数的对称幂等量子拉丁方，其阶为 4k+2。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了两类特殊量子拉丁方在基数最优意义下的存在性范围，推进了量子拉丁方的组合构造理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that there exist a symmetric quantum Latin square with maximal cardinality of order $n$ for all $n\geq 6$, and a symmetric idempotent quantum Latin square with maximal cardinality of order $4k+2$ for all $k\geq 1$.

</details>


### [205] [Clique-dependent strongly sublinear treewidth and strongly sublinear tree-independence number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24684)
*Andrea Munaro*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了遗传图类的强次线性树独立数与（在固定团数约束下）强次线性树宽等价，并将该等价推广到团依赖多项式扩张、特定形式的平衡分离子和基于团的强次线性平衡分离子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chudnovsky、E S 和 Lokshtanov（arXiv 2025）近期给出了树宽与树独立数之间关系的结果，本文希望建立其强次线性对应版本，并统一来自次指数时间精确算法与多项式时间近似方案研究中独立出现的若干图类性质。

Method: 通过证明更广泛的等价定理：对遗传图类，强次线性树独立数、固定团数下强次线性树宽、团依赖多项式扩张、大小为 Kω(G)^s|V(G)|^{1-β} 的平衡分离子以及强次线性规模的基于团的平衡分离子相互等价；并据此构造算法。

Result: 遗传图类具有强次线性树独立数当且仅当其有界团数子图具有强次线性树宽；上述若干条件彼此等价，即它们刻画同一类遗传图类。

Conclusion: 等价定理表明这些独立发展的性质实际上描述同一类遗传图类，并推出每个具有强次线性树独立数的遗传类都存在次指数时间算法，可在给定 G∈C 时计算具有强次线性独立数的树分解。

Abstract: We establish a strongly sublinear counterpart of a recent result of Chudnovsky, E S, and Lokshtanov (arXiv 2025) on treewidth and tree-independence number. Namely, we prove that a hereditary graph class has strongly sublinear tree-independence number if and only if, for every fixed clique bound, its graphs of bounded clique number have strongly sublinear treewidth. In fact, this is part of a broader equivalence theorem. For hereditary classes, these conditions are also equivalent to having clique-dependent polynomial expansion, to admitting balanced separators whose size is bounded by $Kω(G)^s |V(G)|^{1-β}$ for fixed $K,s,β>0$, and to admitting balanced clique-based separators of strongly sublinear size (equivalently, weight). Thus, we show that all these properties, which arose independently in the study of subexponential-time exact algorithms and polynomial-time approximation schemes, in fact describe the same hereditary graph classes. As a consequence of our equivalence theorem, we also show that every hereditary class $\mathcal C$ with strongly sublinear tree-independence number admits a subexponential-time algorithm that, given $G\in\mathcal C$, computes a tree decomposition of $G$ with strongly sublinear independence number.

</details>


### [206] [On infinite families of $[P_n]$-irregular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24707)
*Tatiana Dovzhenok,Ilya Lukashenko,Andrei Mikhalev,Yahor Filiuta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究 $[F]$-不规则图，证明对任意 $n\ge 3$ 的路径 $P_n$ 存在无穷多个 $[P_n]$-不规则图，并给出 $[P_3]$-不规则图的存在性刻画，最后提出强猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 $F$-不规则性关注图中包含各顶点的子图计数差异，本文试图提出并研究与之平行的 $[F]$-不规则性概念，即基于诱导子图计数在顶点间的互异性，从而拓展现有图不规则性理论。

Method: 引入 $[F]$-不规则图定义：固定图 $F$，若图 $G$ 中每个顶点所诱导出的与 $F$ 同构的子图数量两两不同，则称 $G$ 为 $[F]$-不规则图。围绕路径 $P_n$ 展开构造性与存在性分析，并研究 $[P_3]$-不规则图的阶数条件。

Result: 对任意 $n\ge 3$ 的路径 $P_n$，存在无穷多个 $[P_n]$-不规则图；非平凡 $[P_3]$-不规则图存在当且仅当其阶数 $k\ge 7$。

Conclusion: 该工作奠定了 $[F]$-不规则图的研究基础，并基于所得结果提出关于 $[F]$-不规则图的强猜想，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: This paper presents the first systematic study of $[F]$-irregular graphs, a concept that parallels classical $F$-irregularity. For a fixed graph $F$, a graph $G$ is $[F]$-irregular if the numbers of its induced subgraphs isomorphic to $F$ containing a given vertex are pairwise distinct for all vertices of $G$. We prove that there exist infinitely many $[P_n]$-irregular graphs for any path $P_n$ of order $n \ge 3$. We establish that a non-trivial $[P_3]$-irregular graph of order $k$ exists if and only if $k \ge 7$. Finally, we propose the Strong Conjecture on $[F]$-irregular graphs.

</details>


### [207] [The $\mathbb{Z}$-Divisor Graph on $\mathbb{Q} \cap [1,7]$ is Planar and 3-Colorable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24724)
*Michael D. Walton,Zachary D. Walton*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了整数区间[n,m]上的divisor graph在m≤7n时是planar graph，且7是最优常数；并证明此类图是3-colorable。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整数区间上的divisor graph的平面性与染色性质，刻画保证平面性的区间长度比例阈值。

Method: 针对区间[n,m]上的divisor graph，证明当m≤7n时该图为planar graph，并通过构造或反例论证7为最优常数；进一步证明其3-colorable。

Result: 当m≤7n时divisor graph是planar graph；7是保证平面性的最大常数；该类图是3-colorable。

Conclusion: 确定了整数区间divisor graph平面性的最优阈值7，并给出其三色可染性结论。

Abstract: We prove that a divisor graph on an interval of integers is a planar graph whenever the interval $[n,m]$ satisfies $m\leq 7n$, and that 7 is the greatest such real number for which we can guarantee that the graph is planar. We prove these graphs are 3-colorable.

</details>


### [208] [Three-Layer Intersecting Temperate Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24747)
*Kada Williams*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明关于交错温和集族最大结构的Petr-Turek猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Petr与Turek提出的猜想，确定三层集族中最大交错温和集族的精确结构。

Method: 组合极值集合论方法，利用交错条件与温和（temperate）性质分析[2k]^{(k)}∪[2k]^{(k+1)}∪[2k]^{(k+2)}上的最大集族结构。

Result: 证明每个最大交错温和集族必包含所有含某固定元素a的k-集、所有(k+1)-集以及所有不含a的(k+2)-集。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了这类三层集族最大交错温和集族的极值结构，验证了Petr-Turek猜想。

Abstract: In response to a conjecture of Petr and Turek, we prove that every largest intersecting temperate collection of sets $\mathcal{A}\subseteq [2k]^{(k)}\cup [2k]^{(k+1)}\cup [2k]^{(k+2)}$ includes all $k$-sets containing some given $a\in[2k]$, all $(k+1)$-sets, and all $(k+2)$-sets not containing $a$.

</details>


### [209] [Discrepancy theory, Tverberg's theorem, and regression depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24775)
*Aleksey Lopez,Pablo Soberón*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了带容错的Tverberg定理的新上界，并将其推广到超平面族，方法是通过建立Tverberg型结果与差异理论之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tverberg定理是离散几何中的核心结果，带容错的版本允许移除少量点后仍保证凸包相交。已有界不够精确，作者希望改进带容错Tverberg定理的定量界，并将其扩展到超平面情形。

Method: 通过建立Tverberg型结果与差异理论（discrepancy theory）之间的联系来推导界。对点集情形证明N = rt + Θ_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)})；对超平面族情形，利用回归包（regression hulls）概念证明rt + O_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)}√log(t+1))个超平面即可保证分区后回归包相交（即使移除任意t个超平面）。

Result: 点集情形：N = rt + Θ_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)})。超平面族情形：rt + O_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)}√log(t+1))。这些界改进了已有结果，并且带容错Tverberg定理被成功推广到R^d中的超平面族。

Conclusion: 带容错Tverberg定理的新界可由Tverberg型结果与差异理论之间的联系导出，且该框架可自然推广到超平面族，得到回归包相交的相应定量保证。

Abstract: We prove new bounds for Tverberg's theorem with tolerance. We show that $N = rt+Θ_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)})$, where $N$ is the smallest number such that any set of $N$ points in $\mathbb{R}^d$ has a partition into $r$ parts such that the convex hulls of the parts intersect even if we remove any $t$ of the points.
  We extend Tverberg's theorem with tolerance to families of hyperplanes in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and show that for any set of $rt + O_{d,r}(t^{1/2-1/(2d)}\sqrt{\log (t+1)})$ hyperplanes in $\mathbb{R}^d$ there exists a partition of them into $r$ parts such that the regression hulls of the parts intersect even if any $t$ hyperplanes are removed.
  Our bounds follow from establishing a connection between Tverberg-type results and discrepancy theory.

</details>


### [210] [The $g$-theorem in smooth approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24790)
*Joel Hakavuori*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了逼近光滑凸体的单纯多胞形序列的原始Betti数会超越g-定理给出的上界，与Adiprasito-Nevo-Samper关于下界发散的结果互补。证明依赖关于单项式集合的shadow大小的新下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adiprasito-Nevo-Samper已证明此类序列的原始Betti数从下界发散，本文旨在补全另一侧，即证明其同样从上界发散，从而完整刻画逼近光滑凸体的单纯多胞形的Betti数行为。

Method: 通过对单项式集合的shadow大小建立新的下界，并将该下界应用于逼近光滑凸体的单纯多胞形序列的原始Betti数分析。

Result: 证明逼近光滑凸体的单纯多胞形序列的原始Betti数从上界发散，即超出g-定理所给的上界。

Conclusion: 该结果与Adiprasito-Nevo-Samper的下界发散结果互补，说明g-定理的上、下界对光滑凸体的单纯逼近均非紧确；所建立的单项式shadow下界是核心工具。

Abstract: We show that a sequence of simplicial polytopes approximating a smooth convex body has primitive Betti numbers diverging from the upper bound given by the $g$-theorem. This complements a result of Adiprasito-Nevo-Samper, who proved the corresponding divergence from the lower bound. The proof uses a new lower bound for the size of the shadow of a set of monomials.

</details>


### [211] [Castelnuovo-Mumford regularity of skew-symmetric matrix Schubert varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24957)
*Jack Chen-An Chou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文计算了斜对称矩阵舒伯特簇的Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性，给出了辛Grothendieck多项式次数的组合公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜对称矩阵舒伯特簇与辛群在旗簇上的轨道闭包密切相关，其环面等变K类为辛Grothendieck多项式，研究其正则性具有代数几何与组合学意义。

Method: 通过给出辛Grothendieck多项式次数的组合公式，并刻画其最高次齐次分量，来计算斜对称矩阵舒伯特簇的Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性。

Result: 得到了斜对称矩阵舒伯特簇的Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性，并计算出其最大Castelnuovo-Mumford正则性。

Conclusion: 辛Grothendieck多项式次数与最高次齐次分量的组合刻画为斜对称矩阵舒伯特簇的正则性提供了有效计算工具。

Abstract: Skew-symmetric matrix Schubert varieties are determinantal varieties obtained by intersecting matrix Schubert varieties with the space of skew-symmetric matrices. They are closely related to the orbit closures of the symplectic group action on the flag variety, and their torus-equivariant K-classes are the symplectic Grothendieck polynomials. We compute the Castelnuovo-Mumford regularity of skew-symmetric matrix Schubert varieties by giving a combinatorial formula for the degree of symplectic Grothendieck polynomials. In addition, we characterize the highest-degree homogeneous component of a symplectic Grothendieck polynomial and compute the maximal Castelnuovo-Mumford regularity of skew-symmetric matrix Schubert varieties.

</details>


### [212] [Extremal subtrees of critical beta-splitting trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24973)
*Anna Brandenberger,Byron Chin,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过将临界beta-分裂树与数据压缩及Huffman最小冗余码建立联系，确定了该树实例最可能和最不可能的形态，并解答了Aldous与Janson提出的关于clade分布的组合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aldous与Janson在arXiv:2303.02529中提出第7号问题，询问临界beta-分裂树的clade分布，但缺乏确定极端形态（最可能/最不可能）的工具。

Method: 建立临界beta-分裂树与Huffman最小冗余码（数据压缩）之间的联系，利用该对应关系分析树的形态与clade分布。

Result: 确定了临界beta-分裂树实例的最可能与最不可能形态，并据此回答了Aldous与Janson提出的关于clade分布的组合问题。

Conclusion: Huffman编码与临界beta-分裂树之间的对应关系是分析该随机树极端形态及clade分布的有效工具，解决了公开问题7。

Abstract: We determine the most and least likely shapes for an instance of the critical beta-splitting tree via a connection to data compression and Huffman's minimum redundancy codes. This allows us to answer combinatorial questions about the distribution of clades posed by Aldous and Janson, stated as problem 7 in arXiv:2303.02529.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [A Note on Partially Anti-invariant Submanifolds of Kenmotsu Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22263)
*Muhammad Shuaib,Cenap Ozel,Meraj A. Khan,Yakup Yildirim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究Kenmotsu流形的部分反不变子流形，考察分布可积性、全测地叶状结构条件、结构张量平行时的几何性质，并利用曲率张量与常ψ截面曲率的Kenmotsu空间形式给出多种几何刻画，同时刻画全脐部分反不变子流形并建立不等式及其等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在Kenmotsu流形框架下推广部分反不变子流形的研究，借助分布可积性、叶状结构及结构张量平行性等条件，揭示该类子流形的内在几何结构，并利用Kenmotsu空间形式的曲率性质获得更精细的几何与不等式刻画。

Method: 通过研究相关分布的可积性及其诱导叶状结构成为全测地的条件；分析结构张量N与P平行时分布的几何性质；利用Kenmotsu流形的曲率张量以及常ψ截面曲率的Kenmotsu空间形式性质进行推导。

Result: 得到了诱导叶状结构为全测地的条件；给出结构张量N、P平行时分布的几何性质；推导出部分反不变子流形的多种几何刻画；刻画了全脐部分反不变子流形；建立了Kenmotsu空间形式中部分反不变子流形的一个不等式，并刻画了等号成立情形。

Conclusion: 该研究系统刻画了Kenmotsu流形中部分反不变子流形的几何结构，拓展了相关子流形理论，并为后续研究提供了可积性、全测地性、平行性与曲率刻画及不等式方面的结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and investigate partially antiinvariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu manifolds. The integrability of the involved distributions is examined, and conditions ensuring that the induced foliations are totally geodesic are obtained. We further explore the geometric properties of these distributions when the associated structure tensors N and P are parallel. By making use of the curvature tensor of Kenmotsu manifolds and the properties of Kenmotsu space forms with constant psi sectional curvature, we derive various geometric characterizations of partially anti-invariant submanifolds. In addition, we characterize totally umbilical partially anti-invariant submanifolds and establish an inequality for partially anti invariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu space forms, including the characterization of the equality case.

</details>


### [214] [Elimination of the parameters in an elliptic parametrization of the Schwarz primitive surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22313)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构建Schwarz原始曲面P（钻石曲面D的共轭）基本片的参数化，利用椭圆函数恒等式完成从代数消元到可加分离性的证明，给出精确归一化常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与姊妹论文[D]共享同一出发点，仅取不同的实部，从而参数化Schwarz原始曲面P的fundamental patch；旨在建立并证明其核心的椭圆函数恒等式(⋆)。

Method: 定义区域Ω（由四条圆弧围成的曲线正方形）及超椭圆权W=√(1+14ζ⁴+ζ⁸)，构造θ、f、g、𝔥相关函数；取X=(κ Re f, κ Re g, 1/2+κ Im 𝔥)，κ=3/(2K[1/9])。Part I建立代数工具并确定归一化常数ϖ=κ⁻¹=K[-3]=1/2 K[3/4]=2/3 K[1/9]；Part II执行消元证明恒等式(⋆)；Part III证明该恒等式具有可加分离性。

Result: 精确确定归一化常数ϖ=κ⁻¹=K[-3]=1/2 K[3/4]=2/3 K[1/9]；完成消元并证明恒等式(⋆)：sn(ϖ(x+y),-3)·sn(ϖ(x-y),-3)=1/3 sn(3ϖ(z-1/2),1/9)；并证明(⋆)可加分离。

Conclusion: 通过精确归一化与代数消元，本文完成了恒等式(⋆)的证明，并揭示其可加分离结构，将P曲面的参数化与椭圆函数恒等式统一起来。

Abstract: Let $Ω$ be the curvilinear square bounded by the four circular arcs $|ζ\mp1|=\sqrt{2}$, $|ζ\mp i|=\sqrt{2}$, and let $W=\sqrt{1+14ζ^{4}+ζ^{8}}$. Put \[
  θ(ζ)=\arcsin\frac{2(1+i)ζ}
  {\sqrt{1+4iζ^{2}-ζ^{4}}}, \] \[
  f=\tfrac14\bigl(-iF[θ,\tfrac14]+F[θ,\tfrac34]\bigr),\quad \quad
  g=\tfrac14\bigl(\ \ iF[θ,\tfrac14]+F[θ,\tfrac34]\bigr), \] \[
  \mathfrak h=(2-\sqrt3)\,
  F\bigl[\arcsin\bigl(i(2+\sqrt3)ζ^{2}\bigr),(2-\sqrt3)^{4}\bigr]. \] These are, verbatim, the three functions of our companion paper $\mathrm{[D]}$ on the diamond surface $\mathrm{D}$: the two papers start from the same point, and differ only in which real parts are taken. With $X=(κ\,\operatorname{Re}f,\ κ\,\operatorname{Re}g,\ \tfrac12+κ\,\operatorname{Im}\mathfrak{h})$ and $κ=3/(2K[1/9])$, the map $X$ parametrizes a fundamental patch of Schwarz's primitive surface $\mathrm{P}$, the conjugate of $\mathrm{D}$.
  Part I builds the algebraic apparatus $\ldots$ the normalizing constant is identified exactly: $\varpi:=κ^{-1}=K[-3]=\tfrac12 K[3/4]=\tfrac23 K[1/9]$. Part II carries out the elimination and completes the proof of \[
  (\star)\qquad
  \operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{+}y),-3\bigr)\,\operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{-}y),-3\bigr)
  =\tfrac13\operatorname{sn}\bigl(3\varpi(z-\tfrac12),\tfrac19\bigr). \] Part III shows that $(\star)$ is, after all, additively separable $\ldots$ for an overview of this paper and $\mathrm{[D]}$, please see arXiv:2609.14206.

</details>


### [215] [The suboptimal Penrose inequality for charged initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22418)
*Eunice Ng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri的Penrose型不等式推广到带电（Einstein-Maxwell、磁场消失）情形，证明ADM质量m可由面积A与电荷Q从下方控制；渐近双曲初值集类似结论以双曲能量表述；在轴对称下可加强以含角动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri）建立了Penrose型不等式，但限于无电荷情形，尚未覆盖带电初值集，因此需要把该不等式推广到Einstein-Maxwell带电设置。

Method: 针对完备、3维、渐近平坦且磁场消失的Einstein-Maxwell初值集，构造并证明质量下界不等式；并平移论证至渐近双曲初值集，用双曲能量替代ADM质量；在附加轴对称假设下，进一步将不等式加强以纳入角动量。

Result: 存在小通用常数C，使m≥C(√(A/(16π))+Q²√(π/A))；渐近双曲情形有对应结论（ADM质量换为双曲能量）；轴对称时可加强以包含角动量。

Conclusion: 结果把Penrose型不等式从无电荷推广到带电情形，为电荷与角动量参与的质量-面积-电荷/角动量下界提供统一框架。

Abstract: Given a complete, 3-dimensional, asymptotically flat initial data set for the Einstein-Maxwell equations with vanishing magnetic field, we show that there exists a small universal constant $\mathcal{C}$ such that $m\geq\mathcal{C}(\sqrt{\mathcal{A}/(16π)}+\mathcal{Q}^2\sqrt{π/\mathcal{A}}\,)$. An analogous statement holds for asymptotically hyperboloidal initial data sets with the ADM mass replaced by hyperbolic energy. Under the additional assumption of axisymmetry, the inequality may be further strengthened to include angular momentum. These results extend the Penrose-type inequalities recently obtained by Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri to the charged setting.

</details>


### [216] [The sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds in codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22447)
*Raphael Tsiamis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为欧氏空间中一类包含任意维数与余维数、具有局部有界第一变差的整变分流形的变分流形类，证明了 Michael-Simon 不等式；在余维2时该不等式为最优，并推出变分流形的sharp等周不等式，等号仅由圆盘取得，证明采用 Brendle-Eichmair 的最优传输方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的 Michael-Simon 不等式从子流形推广到更一般的变分流形（varifolds）框架，覆盖任意维数与余维数、具局部有界第一变差的整变分流形，并进一步获得最优形式的几何不等式（如sharp等周不等式）。

Method: 采用 Brendle-Eichmair 提出的最优传输（optimal transportation）方法进行证明。

Result: 建立了一类变分流形的 Michael-Simon 不等式；在余维2情形下该不等式是最优的，并蕴含变分流形的sharp等周不等式，其等号情形恰好对应圆盘。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Michael-Simon 型不等式推广至一般变分流形，并在余维2情形下给出最优结果及等周不等式的等号刻画，表明最优传输方法是处理此类几何不等式的有效工具。

Abstract: We prove a Michael-Simon inequality for a class of varifolds in Euclidean space that includes all integral varifolds with locally bounded first variation of arbitrary dimension and codimension. In codimension $2$, this inequality is sharp and implies the sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds, which attains equality precisely for the round disk. Our proof uses the optimal transportation approach of Brendle-Eichmair.

</details>


### [217] [A Differential-Geometric Framework for Risk-Optimal Asset Reallocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22459)
*Georgios Leventidis,Evangelos Melas*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个微分几何框架，将组合再平衡路径视为概率单纯形上的测地线，以最小化过渡期累积风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合再平衡通常只关注目标权重的最优性，而忽略了从当前配置到目标配置过渡过程中所累积的风险。本文旨在将过渡管理形式化为几何最短路径问题。

Method: 将长仓组合表示为概率单纯形上的点，赋予由市场协方差风险与持仓相关集中度风险构成的正定黎曼度量；将再平衡轨迹的累积风险定义为黎曼长度，从而最小风险过渡为测地线。与线性再平衡及投影梯度上升进行对比。

Result: 仅对市场协方差风险定价时，度量为常值且平坦，测地线即为直线路径；引入持仓相关风险后几何变弯曲，测地线在同端点下弱占优于竞争路径。Fisher-Rao集中度项带来温和但系统的节约，内生拥挤度量产生非凸风险脊，测地线可通过临时分散化绕行。数值实验、蒙特卡洛转移与回归分析表明，当直接路径穿过强拥挤区域时收益最大。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了过渡管理的一般几何表述，并可扩展以容纳更丰富的风险度量与交易成本结构。

Abstract: We develop a differential-geometric framework for risk-optimal portfolio reallocation. A long-only portfolio is represented as a point of the probability simplex, endowed with a positive-definite Riemannian metric combining market covariance risk with position-dependent concentration risk. The cumulative risk of a rebalancing trajectory is identified with its Riemannian length, so the least-risk transition between a current allocation and a Markowitz target is a geodesic. We compare this route with direct linear rebalancing and projected gradient ascent. When only market covariance risk is priced, the metric is constant and flat, and the geodesic is exactly the straight-line path. Once position-dependent risk is introduced, the geometry becomes curved and the geodesic weakly dominates competing paths with the same endpoints. A Fisher-Rao concentration term produces modest but systematic savings, while an endogenous crowding metric creates non-convex risk ridges that geodesics can bypass through temporary diversification. Numerical experiments, Monte Carlo transitions, and regression analysis show that the largest gains occur when the direct path crosses strongly crowded regions. The framework provides a general geometric formulation of transition management and can accommodate richer risk metrics and transaction-cost structures.

</details>


### [218] [Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions on $\mathbb{CP}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22659)
*Yusen Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 的 $1$-凸域上证明了第一 Dirichlet 特征函数严格对数凹，给出定量下界，并导出水平集一致凸性与基本间隙下界 $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>46/5$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复射影空间中第一 Dirichlet 特征函数的对数凹性（log-concavity），并将定量曲率估计应用于几何与谱性质。

Method: 对 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 中每个光滑 $1$-凸域 $\Omega$，证明其第一 Dirichlet 特征函数 $u$ 满足定量估计 $\nabla^2(-\log u) > \max\{\psi(s),8/5\}g$，其中 $s=|\nabla f|^2$，$\psi(s)=s/\sqrt{1+s}-\log(1+s)$。

Result: 得到严格对数凹性；作为推论，获得 $u$ 正则水平集的一致凸性估计，以及基本间隙界 $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>46/5$。

Conclusion: 该工作为复射影空间中特征函数的对数凹性提供定量版本，并由此推出水平集几何与谱间隙的显式界。

Abstract: We study the log-concavity property of first Dirichlet eigenfunctions on domains in $\mathbb{CP}^2$. For every smooth $1$-convex domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{CP}^2$, we prove the quantitative estimate \[ \nabla^2(-\log u) > \max\left\{ψ(s),\frac85\right\}g, \text{ where } ψ(|\grad f|^2) = \frac{s}{\sqrt{1+s}}-\log(1+s), \] for its first Dirichlet eigenfunction $u$. In particular, $u$ is strictly log-concave.
  As consequences, we obtain a uniform convexity estimate for the regular level sets of $u$ and the fundamental gap bound $λ_2-λ_1>46/5$.

</details>


### [219] [Chain-level decoupling of the physical heterotic $G_2$ deformation complex at the standard embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22565)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在极小标准嵌入下，对异弦 $G_2$ 紧化的物理形变复形的一阶系数复形给出了显式的链级解耦，证明其上同调在各阶分解为瞬子与几何系数上同调之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异弦 $G_2$ 紧化的形变复形将瞬子形变与几何场及其一阶 $\alpha'$ 修正耦合在一起，此前缺乏显式的全阶解耦，阻碍了对该物理约化系数复形上同调结构的直接理解。论文旨在给出此类显式解耦。

Method: 在极小标准嵌入（规范丛为切丛、规范联络为 Levi-Civita 联络、背景 $G_2$ 结构无挠）下，构造链级解耦：第一 Atiyah 耦合通过一个延拓诱导联络变分的斜协变导数实现零同伦；第二个同伦由 endomorphism 值上链斜部的协变余微分给出，将丛扇区到几何扇区的耦合消去；残余几何曲率项与第一变换的贡献相消。这些恒等式在任意上链、每个规范阶上成立，得到互逆的微分算子链映射。论证仅需 $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$。

Result: 所得互逆链映射使该复形的上同调在所有阶上分解为瞬子系数上同调与几何系数上同调的直和。据作者所知，此前没有显式的全阶分裂能去除这一物理约化标准嵌入系数复形中的内部规范与几何耦合。

Conclusion: 论文在极小标准嵌入且 $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$ 的条件下，实现了异弦 $G_2$ 紧化一阶系数复形的显式全阶解耦，从而将其上同调分解为瞬子与几何两部分。

Abstract: The physical deformation complex of heterotic $G_2$ compactifications couples instanton deformations to geometric fields and their first-order $α'$ corrections. We give an explicit chain-level decoupling of its first-order coefficient complex at the minimal standard embedding, where the gauge bundle is the tangent bundle, the gauge connection is the Levi--Civita connection, and the background $G_2$ structure is torsion-free. The first Atiyah coupling is null-homotopic through a skew covariant derivative extending the induced connection variation. A second homotopy, given by the covariant codifferential of the skew part of an endomorphism-valued cochain, removes the coupling from the bundle sector to the geometric sector. The residual geometric curvature term cancels against the contribution of the first transformation. These identities hold on arbitrary cochains in every canonical degree and yield mutually inverse differential-operator chain maps. Consequently, the cohomology decomposes into instanton and geometric coefficient cohomologies in all degrees. The argument requires only $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$. To our knowledge, no previous explicit all-degree splitting removes the internal gauge and geometric couplings of this physically reduced standard-embedding coefficient complex.

</details>


### [220] [Elastic BB knots are multifold circles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22575)
*Philipp Reiter,Heiko von der Mosel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在广义Fáry-Milnor不等式假设下，证明桥指标与辫指标相等的单分支BB纽结类中的弹性纽结是恰好覆盖相应次数的圆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由弯曲能与ropelength小倍数之和构成的弹性纽结极小化问题，旨在对一类纽结（BB纽结类）给出弹性纽结的完整分类。

Method: 在C^1闭包中假设总曲率至少为桥指标乘以2π；不对ropelength泛函求导，而是将极小闭合辫在其实心环面纤维中收缩，构造具有二次弯曲能超出量与线性阶厚度的一族比较曲线，从而得到厚度下界；再通过中间尺度的变分获得极限曲线对归一化弯曲能的自由驻定性，结合常曲率与自由驻定性将分类归结为线性分布型常微分方程。

Result: 证明了单分支BB纽结类中的弹性纽结必为恰好覆盖其桥指标（等于辫指标）次数的圆。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了对非光滑ropelength泛函的微分，借助比较曲线与中间尺度变分实现了弹性纽结的分类，验证了BB纽结类中极小者为多重圆这一结论。

Abstract: We study elastic knots obtained by minimizing the bending energy of a unit loop together with a small multiple of ropelength. We assume a generalized Fáry-Milnor inequality on the $C^1$-boundary of a knot class: every $H^2$-limit curve in the $C^1$-closure has total curvature at least $2π$ times the bridge index. Under this assumption we prove that every elastic knot in a one-component BB knot class, that is, a class whose bridge and braid indices coincide, is the round circle covered precisely that many times.
  The proof does not differentiate the nonsmooth ropelength functional. A minimal closed braid is contracted in the fibres of a solid torus, producing a family of comparison curves with quadratic bending-energy excess and thickness of linear order. This yields a lower thickness bound for the regularized minimizers. Variations on an intermediate scale then give free stationarity of the limiting curve for the normalized bending energy. Constant curvature and free stationarity reduce the classification to a linear distributional ordinary differential equation.

</details>


### [221] [Sharp harmonic-mean inequalities for Neumann and Aharonov--Bohm spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24488)
*Daguang Chen,Chengxi Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了曲面上 Neumann 和 Aharonov–Bohm Neumann 谱的尖锐双特征值等周不等式，等号仅在圆盘处成立，并给出了磁球冠谱排序等关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双特征值（两特征值）等周不等式，将经典 Neumann 谱结果推广到 Aharonov–Bohm 磁 Neumann 情形，尤其是在曲率有上界的单连通曲面上寻求尖锐界与刚性刻画。

Method: 结合二维倒数 Rayleigh–Ritz 原理与 Green 层比较；利用分解 L_0=T^*T、L_1=TT^* 及精确的 Neumann–Dirichlet 谱位移，证明磁球冠的谱排序定理。

Result: 对光滑、单连通且 proper 的 Ω⊂S^2，μ_2(Ω) 与 μ_3(Ω) 的调和平均不超过等面积测地圆盘的第一正 Neumann 特征值，等号仅圆盘；对曲率有上界的单连通曲面得到前两个 Aharonov–Bohm 特征值的磁类比；还给出尖锐的全球面与闭曲面界及由共形模和磁通量表达的环域不等式。

Conclusion: 建立了 Neumann 与磁 Neumann 谱的尖锐双特征值等周不等式，并证明磁球冠谱排序定理，等号情形由圆盘刻画。

Abstract: We prove sharp two-eigenvalue isoperimetric inequalities for Neumann and Aharonov--Bohm Neumann spectra on surfaces. If $Ω\subset\mathbb S^2$ is smooth, simply connected and proper, then the harmonic mean of $μ_2(Ω)$ and $μ_3(Ω)$ is bounded above by the first positive Neumann eigenvalue of the equal-area geodesic disk, with equality only for disks. For simply connected surfaces with Gaussian curvature bounded above, we obtain the magnetic analogue for the first two Aharonov--Bohm eigenvalues. The proof combines a two-dimensional reciprocal Rayleigh--Ritz principle with Green-level comparison. A key additional ingredient is a spectral ordering theorem for magnetic spherical caps: for $0<ν<1/2$, the first two eigenvalues lie in the angular sectors of effective orders $ν$ and $1-ν$. We prove this by the factorization $L_0=T^*T$, $L_1=TT^*$ and an exact Neumann--Dirichlet spectral shift. We also obtain sharp full-sphere and closed-surface bounds, and an annular inequality in terms of conformal modulus and flux.

</details>


### [222] [Pólya--Szegö Inequality on Submanifolds of Riemannian Manifolds with Nonnegative Curvature and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22676)
*Anh Xuan Do,Nguyen Lam,Guozhen Lu,Raoní Ponciano*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在完备非紧、截面非负的黎曼流形的n维子流形上证明了Pólya–Szegö不等式，其重排为R^n上的Schwarz重排，常数依赖平均曲率的L^n范数与Brendle等周量，并由此推出多种Sobolev型不等式，在临界情形得到Moser–Trudinger不等式与精确增长估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是在一般子流形（任意余维）上建立可类比欧氏空间的函数重排与Sobolev型不等式理论，并在平均曲率较小的条件下获得具有最优/精确常数的分析不等式，特别是临界Sobolev情形下的Moser–Trudinger型结果。

Method: 方法是对定义在完备非紧非负截面曲率黎曼流形的n维子流形Σ上的函数证明Pólya–Szegö不等式；所用重排为R^n上的Schwarz重排，常数由Σ平均曲率的L^n范数与Brendle给出的等周量控制。随后将该不等式作为工具，推导Sobolev、Log-Sobolev、Hardy与Gagliardo–Nirenberg不等式；在临界Sobolev情形处理有限体积与无穷体积子流形分别得到Moser–Trudinger不等式与精确增长不等式。

Result: 得到了子流形上的Pólya–Szegö不等式及其在任意余维、小总平均曲率假设下的Sobolev、Log-Sobolev、Hardy、Gagliardo–Nirenberg不等式；临界Sobolev情形下，有限体积子流形上获得Moser–Trudinger不等式，无穷体积子流形上获得精确增长不等式；在合适假设下Pólya–Szegö常数为1，此时各不等式中的临界常数与欧氏空间的最优常数一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将欧氏空间中的重排方法与最优Sobolev型不等式推广到非负曲率流形的任意余维子流形，并在小总平均曲率条件下给出与欧氏最优常数一致的临界不等式。

Abstract: We prove a Pólya--Szegö inequality for functions defined on an $n$-dimensional submanifold $Σ$ of a complete noncompact Riemannian manifold with nonnegative sectional curvature. The associated rearrangement is a Schwarz rearrangement on $\mathbb R^n$, and the constant depends on the $L^n$-norm of the mean curvature of $Σ$ and an isoperimetric quantity obtained by Brendle. As applications, we derive Sobolev, Log-Sobolev, Hardy, and Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequalities on submanifolds of arbitrary codimension under a small total mean curvature assumption. In the critical Sobolev case, we obtain Moser--Trudinger inequalities on finite-volume submanifolds and exact growth inequalities on submanifolds with infinite volume. Under suitable assumptions, the Pólya--Szegö constant equals one; in this case, the critical constants in the inequalities coincide with the sharp Euclidean ones.

</details>


### [223] [Projectively induced Kähler--Einstein surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22832)
*Andrea Loi,Mirel Caibăr*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文分类了由全纯等距浸入到有限维复射影空间所诱导度量的紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面，证明仅有Veronese嵌入实现的(PP², m g_FS)和Segre–Veronese嵌入实现的(PP¹×PP¹, m(g_FS⊕g_FS))两类，进而推出此类曲面均为齐性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由全纯等距浸入复射影空间诱导度量的紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面的分类问题，且不附加对称性假设、不限制余维数，以刻画此类度量的刚性并回答其是否齐性。

Method: 核心创新是余维数无关地排除Fano指数为1的分支：结合公共反规范根构造与Gram–Gauss秩估计，并在次数为5的情形使用等变曲率论证。

Result: 唯一可能的曲面为(PP², m g_FS)与(PP¹×PP¹, m(g_FS⊕g_FS))，m∈Z_{>0}，分别由Veronese嵌入和Segre–Veronese嵌入实现。

Conclusion: 所有由全纯等距浸入到有限维复射影空间诱导度量的连通紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面都是齐性的。

Abstract: We classify compact Kähler--Einstein surfaces whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space. No symmetry assumption and no bound on the codimension are imposed. We prove that the only such surfaces are \[ (\PP^2,m g_{\FS}) \quad\text{and}\quad \bigl(\PP^1\times\PP^1,m(g_{\FS}\oplus g_{\FS})\bigr), \qquad m\in\mathbb Z_{>0}, \] realized respectively by the Veronese and Segre--Veronese embeddings.
  The main new ingredient is a codimension-independent exclusion of the entire Fano-index-one branch, combining a common anticanonical root construction with Gram--Gauss rank estimates and, in degree five, an equivariant curvature argument. Consequently, every connected compact Kähler--Einstein surface whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space is homogeneous.

</details>


### [224] [Free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^m$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23172)
*Roberto De Leo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意 \(m\ge1\)，临界维数 \(q_m=m(m+3)/2\) 下，环面 \(\mathbb{T}^m\) 到 \(\mathbb{R}^{q_m}\) 的自由映射存在，即 \(Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset\)。核心工具是一种乘积构造，并结合四元数乘法显式构造低维交叉自由映射，最终通过归纳法得到一般结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由映射的临界维数 \(q_m=m(m+3)/2\) 是一个自然且重要的阈值问题。此前作者已在 \(m\le5\) 的低维情形显式构造了结果，本文旨在将该存在性推广到所有维度 \(m\ge1\)，解决一般 \(m\) 下的临界维数自由映射存在问题。

Method: 主要方法是乘积构造：若在 \(\mathbb{T}^a\) 和 \(\mathbb{T}^b\) 上分别存在临界维数的自由映射，并存在一个在 \(\mathbb{T}^a\times\mathbb{T}^b\) 上处处混合 Hessian 可逆的交叉自由映射，则可得到 \(\mathbb{T}^{a+b}\) 上临界维数的自由映射，且其密切矩阵为块三角形式。交叉自由映射对每个变量是可加的；利用四元数乘法在 \(\mathbb{T}^2\times\mathbb{T}^2\) 和 \(\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4\) 上显式构造交叉自由映射，再结合已有的低维情形 \(m\le5\)，通过归纳完成证明。

Result: 证明了 \(Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset\) 对所有 \(m\ge1\) 成立，即在临界维数 \(q_m=m(m+3)/2\) 下，环面到欧氏空间的自由映射总是存在的。

Conclusion: 该工作将低维显式构造推广到所有维度，确立了临界维数自由映射在环面上的普遍存在性。乘积构造与四元数乘法构造交叉自由映射是关键，且密切矩阵的块三角结构保证了归纳步骤的可行性。

Abstract: The critical dimension for free maps on m-manifolds is $q_m=m(m+3)/2$. We show that $Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset$ for every $m\ge 1$. The main tool is a product construction: given free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^a$ and on $\mathbb{T}^b$, together with a cross-free map on $\mathbb{T}^a\times \mathbb{T}^b$, whose mixed Hessian is invertible everywhere, we obtain a free map in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^{a+b}$, the osculating matrix of the product being block-triangular. Cross-free maps are additive in each argument, and in the torus setting none exists when one factor is one-dimensional. We construct explicit cross-free maps on $\mathbb{T}^2\times \mathbb{T}^2$ and on $\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4$ using quaternionic multiplication. Combined with the low-dimensional cases $m\leq 5$, constructed explicitly by the author in a previous publication, these yield the result by induction.

</details>


### [225] [Spheres under a Cauchy-Riemann inequality in $E(κ,τ)$, and non-CMC Hopf tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23227)
*Hilário Alencar,Harold Rosenberg*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对 $E(\kappa,\tau)$（$\tau\ne0$）中浸入的球面拓扑曲面，Abresch--Rosenberg 微分 $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ 上弱于全纯性的单侧 Cauchy--Riemann 型不等式已迫使平均曲率 $H$ 为常数；该拓扑假设在 Berger 球面上是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在齐性空间 $E(\kappa,\tau)$ 中刻画 CMC 浸入通常依赖 $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ 的全纯性（见姊妹篇），而全纯性条件较强且证明依赖代数论证。本文希望用更弱的分析性条件（单侧 CR 型不等式）替代全纯性，并给出与代数论证逻辑独立的证明，同时考察曲面拓扑假设是否必要。

Method: 将 Bers--Vekua 相似性原理与线场的 Poincaré--Hopf 指标公式相结合，对每个 $E(\kappa,\tau)$（含圆球面）给出统一证明；通过 Hopf 型淹没 $\pi:E(\kappa,\tau)\to M^2(\kappa)$ 构造紧致非 CMC 环面作为反例；并借经典 Poincaré--Hopf 定理给出 $\det S<0$ 曲面的拓扑结论。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ 上的单侧 CR 型不等式即可强制 $H$ 为常数，且该结论对一切 $E(\kappa,\tau)$ 统一成立；在包括圆球面在内的每个 Berger 球面上，存在满足同型不等式（常数界）的紧致非 CMC 环面，即基空间中非恒定测地曲率简单闭曲线在 $\pi$ 下的原像；并且任意处处 $\det S<0$ 的闭曲面必为环面，Hopf 管（$\det S\equiv-\tau^2$）即属此类。

Conclusion: 单侧 CR 型不等式可作为比全纯性更弱的替代条件来推出 CMC，且证明独立于代数方法；本文的球面拓扑假设是尖锐的，因为 Berger 球面上存在非 CMC 环面反例。此外，处处 $\det S<0$ 的闭曲面必为环面。

Abstract: For a smooth, connected, oriented surface $Σ$, topologically a sphere, immersed in the homogeneous space $E(κ,τ)$ with $τ\ne0$, we show that a one-sided Cauchy--Riemann-type inequality on the original Abresch--Rosenberg differential $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ -- much weaker than requiring $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ to be holomorphic -- already forces the mean curvature $H$ to be constant. The proof is uniform across every $E(κ,τ)$, including the round sphere, and combines the Bers--Vekua similarity principle with the Poincaré-Hopf index formula for line fields; it is logically independent of the algebraic argument used, in the companion paper~\cite{AlencarRosenberg2026}, to characterize CMC immersions by holomorphy of $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ alone. We also show that the topological hypothesis is sharp: on every Berger sphere, including the round one, there exist compact non-CMC tori -- the preimages under the Hopf-type submersion $π:E(κ,τ)\to M^2(κ)$ of simple closed curves in the base with nonconstant geodesic curvature -- satisfying the same inequality with a constant bound. As an elementary consequence of the classical Poincaré-Hopf theorem for line fields, we record that every closed surface on which $\det S<0$ everywhere -- a condition satisfied, in particular, by every Hopf tube, where $\det S\equiv-τ^2$ -- is a torus.

</details>


### [226] [Ricci-pinched 3-Manifolds with second-fundamental-form-pinched Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23261)
*Chao Xia,Jincong Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在超二次体积增长假设下，具有凸边界、Ricci张量夹紧及第二基本形式夹紧的完备连通三维流形等距于欧氏半空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Ricci夹紧三维流形的刚性定理推广到带边界的流形情形，探讨边界条件下刚性结论是否依然成立。

Method: 采用位势论方法：构造并研究混合Dirichlet--Neumann的p-容量位势，建立其自由边界水平集的单调性公式，并使用适配该情形的弱Gauss--Bonnet公式。

Result: 在超二次体积增长假设下，带凸边界、夹紧Ricci张量和夹紧第二基本形式的完备连通三维流形等距于欧氏半空间。

Conclusion: 建立了Ricci夹紧三维流形刚性定理的边界类比，表明位势论与弱Gauss--Bonnet工具可用于带边界刚性问题的研究。

Abstract: We prove a boundary analogue of the rigidity theorem for Ricci-pinched three-manifolds under a superquadratic volume-growth assumption. More precisely, we show that a complete connected three-manifold with convex boundary, pinched Ricci tensor, and pinched second fundamental form is isometric to the Euclidean half-space. The proof is potential-theoretic. We construct and study mixed Dirichlet--Neumann $p$-capacitary potentials, establish monotonicity formulas for their free-boundary level sets, and use a weak Gauss--Bonnet formula adapted to this setting.

</details>


### [227] [Congruence theorems for capillary hypersurfaces and a substatic Heintze--Karcher inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23304)
*Felippe Guimarães*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了支撑于超平面或球面上的欧氏空间毛细超曲面的同余定理，并在子静态流形中建立了毛细超曲面的Heintze–Karcher型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ros此前对闭超曲面给出了同余定理，本文旨在将其推广到带边界的毛细超曲面情形（支撑于超平面或球面），并在更一般的子静态流形框架下建立相应的几何不等式。

Method: 采用同余定理的证明技巧以及Heintze–Karcher型不等式的几何分析方法，处理毛细边界条件（超曲面与支撑超平面或球面成固定接触角）。

Result: 得到了支撑于超平面或球面上的欧氏空间毛细超曲面的同余定理，并证明了子静态流形中毛细超曲面的Heintze–Karcher型不等式。

Conclusion: Ros关于闭超曲面的同余结果可推广至毛细超曲面情形，且Heintze–Karcher型不等式在子静态流形的毛细设定下仍然成立。

Abstract: We prove congruence theorems for capillary hypersurfaces of Euclidean space supported on a hyperplane or on a sphere, extending a result of Ros for closed hypersurfaces. We also establish a Heintze--Karcher type inequality for capillary hypersurfaces in substatic manifolds.

</details>


### [228] [On Positively Curved Einstein Four-Manifolds with Euler Characteristic at Most Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23337)
*Liang Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了：在四维情形下，若完备可定向爱因斯坦流形具有严格正截面曲率且欧拉示性数 χ(M) ≤ 3（即同胚于 S^4 或 CP^2），则必等距于标准球面 S^4 或 Fubini–Study 度量的 CP^2；并由此推出若该流形还容许非平凡 Killing 场，结论同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于正截面曲率的爱因斯坦四维流形，存在一个猜想：其必等距于标准 S^4 或 Fubini–Study 的 CP^2。该猜想在一般情形下尚未解决，本文旨在特定的拓扑条件下（同胚于 S^4 或 CP^2）确认这一猜想，从而在重要特例上推进问题的解决。

Method: 在完备、可定向、具有严格正截面曲率的爱因斯坦四维流形上，利用欧拉示性数条件 χ(M) ≤ 3 结合 Freedman 的四维流形分类定理，将拓扑类型限定为同胚于 S^4 或 CP^2；随后在此拓扑约束下结合爱因斯坦条件与正曲率性进行几何分析，导出等距刚性结论。并进一步考察存在非平凡 Killing 场的情形作为应用。

Result: 证明了：完备、可定向、严格正截面曲率的爱因斯坦四维流形 M，若 χ(M) ≤ 3（等价于同胚于 S^4 或 CP^2），则 M 等距于标准的 round S^4 或 Fubini–Study 度量的 CP^2。作为应用，若该流形还容许非平凡 Killing 场，则同样等距于 round S^4 或 Fubini–Study CP^2。

Conclusion: 在拓扑受限于 S^4 或 CP^2（或等价地 χ(M) ≤ 3）以及存在非平凡 Killing 场的条件下，正截面曲率爱因斯坦四维流形的分类猜想得到确认，流形必等距于 round S^4 或 Fubini–Study CP^2。这为一般四维情形的猜想提供了部分重要证据。

Abstract: It is conjectured that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold with strictly positive sectional curvature must be isometric to either the round four-sphere $S^4$ or the complex projective plane $\mathbb{CP}^2$ with the Fubini--Study metric. In this paper, we confirm this conjecture when the manifold is homeomorphic to $S^4$ or $\mathbb{CP}^2$. More precisely, we show that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold $M$ with strictly positive sectional curvature and Euler characteristic $χ(M) \le 3$ (equivalently, by Freedman's classification theorem, homeomorphic to $S^4$ or $\mathbb{CP}^2$) is isometric to either the round $S^4$ or the Fubini--Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$. As an application, we obtain that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold with strictly positive sectional curvature admitting a nontrivial Killing field is isometric to either the round $S^4$ or the Fubini--Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$.

</details>


### [229] [Nonclosure of stationary Yang-Mills connections under weak convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23338)
*Xiaoli Han,Jiayu Li,Yang Wen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在平凡向量丛 $B^5\times\mathbb{R}^{15}$ 上构造了一列 $\operatorname{SO}(15)$ 结构群的平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络，其弱极限是非平稳的弱杨–米尔斯联络，从而证明带孤立奇点的平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络对弱收敛不封闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Tian在杨–米尔斯紧性工作中提出的平稳性问题启发，需要考察平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络在弱极限下是否保持平稳性。

Method: 在平凡向量丛 $B^5\times\mathbb{R}^{15}$ 上、以 $\operatorname{SO}(15)$ 为结构群，显式构造一列平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络，并分析其弱收敛行为。

Result: 所构造序列的弱极限是一个非平稳的弱杨–米尔斯联络，说明平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络在弱拓扑下不封闭；该现象与Ding、Li和Li在调和映射中发现的现象相类比。

Conclusion: 带孤立奇点的平稳弱杨–米尔斯联络缺乏弱闭性，这是Yang–Mills紧性理论中的一个新障碍，并给出调和映射相应结果的杨–米尔斯版本。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of stationary weak Yang-Mills connections on the trivial vector bundle $B^5\times\mathbb{R}^{15}$ with structure group $\operatorname{SO}(15)$ whose weak limit is a nonstationary weak Yang-Mills connection. Motivated by the stationarity question arising in Tian's work on Yang-Mills compactness, our result establishes the failure of weak closure for stationary weak Yang-Mills connections with an isolated singularity. It also provides a Yang-Mills analogue of the phenomenon exhibited by Ding, Li and Li for harmonic maps.

</details>


### [230] [A Generalization of Molino's Theory to Riemannian Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23341)
*Lily Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文把经典的 Molino 理论从紧流形上的 Riemannian 叶层结构推广到具有紧连通对象流形的正则 Riemannian groupoid 情形，并用基本 Lie algebroid 与同伦意义下伴随表示的一阶上同调刻画正规表示，同时给出 Riemannian Morita 等价下的比较结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemannian groupoid 可同时描述 Riemannian 叶层及其对称性，而 Molino 理论原本只处理紧流形上的 Riemannian 叶层结构。作者希望将这一结构定理推广到正则 Riemannian groupoid 框架，以统一刻画叶层及其对称性，并厘清基本 Lie algebroid 与正规表示在其中的本质作用。

Method: 首先观察到正则 Riemannian groupoid 的轨道叶层在对象流形上诱导出 Riemannian 叶层；随后证明该 groupoid 的正规表示可自然延拓为对轨道叶层的 Molino 结构的作用，从而给出正则 Riemannian groupoid 的基本结构描述。此外，借助基本 Lie algebroid 刻画 Molino 理论，并将正规表示等同于同伦意义下伴随表示的一阶上同调表示；最后在 Riemannian Morita 等价下建立比较结果，给出基本 Lie algebroid 拉回到公共细化后同构的充分条件。

Result: 主要结果证明了正规表示自然延拓为轨道叶层 Molino 结构上的作用，由此得到正则 Riemannian groupoid 的基本结构描述。同时厘清了基本 Lie algebroid 在 Molino 理论中的本质作用，并将正规表示与同伦意义下伴随表示的一阶上同调表示等同；在 Riemannian Morita 等价下建立了比较结果，并对正则 Riemannian groupoid 给出基本 Lie algebroid 拉回至公共细化后同构的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该文将 Molino 理论成功推广到正则 Riemannian groupoid，统一刻画叶层结构与对称性，并通过基本 Lie algebroid、同伦伴随表示的上同调以及 Morita 等价下的比较，深化了对 Riemannian groupoid 结构不变性的理解。

Abstract: Riemannian groupoids describe Riemannian foliations together with their symmetries. In this article, we extend classical Molino's theory, which concerns the structure of Riemannian foliations on compact manifolds, to the setting of regular Riemannian groupoids with compact connected object manifolds.
  We observe that the orbit foliation associated with a regular Riemannian groupoid defines a Riemannian foliation on the object manifold. The main result shows that the normal representation of a Riemannian groupoid extends naturally to an action on Molino's structures of the orbit foliation, thereby yielding the fundamental structural description of a regular Riemannian groupoid.
  In addition to the main result, we clarify the essential role played by basic Lie algebroids in Molino's theory and identify the normal representation with the degree-one cohomology representation of the adjoint representation up to homotopy. We also establish comparison results under Riemannian Morita equivalence. For regular Riemannian groupoids, we give sufficient conditions for the associated basic Lie algebroids to become isomorphic after pullback to a common refinement.

</details>


### [231] [Liouville theorem for quasi-harmonic sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23359)
*Jiayu Li,Linlin Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了万有覆盖上存在凸函数的紧黎曼流形不允许拟调和球面存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟调和球面的不存在性，弱化凸函数所需的全局增长条件以推广既有结果。

Method: 设N为紧黎曼流形，其万有覆盖支持凸函数（不附加全局增长条件），据此证明该类流形上不存在拟调和球面。

Result: 在该较弱假设下证明了流形不允许拟调和球面存在。

Conclusion: 具有支持凸函数万有覆盖的紧黎曼流形不存在拟调和球面，凸函数无需全局增长条件。

Abstract: Let $N$ be a compact Riemannian manifold whose universal covering supports a convex function. We prove that it admits no quasi-harmonic sphere. No global growth condition is imposed on the convex function.

</details>


### [232] [Rigidity of Einstein Manifolds under Two Eigenvalue Conditions on the Curvature Operator of the Second Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23597)
*Haiqing Cheng,Kui Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在第二类曲率算子两个下界条件下，证明了维数 $n\ge6$ 的闭爱因斯坦流形的刚性定理：流形必为平坦或球空间形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究爱因斯坦流形在第二类曲率算子谱条件下何时具有刚性，并推广已有文献（CW26）中的条件，放宽第二类平均曲率下界约束。

Method: 设第二类曲率算子 $\mathring R$ 的特征值为 $\lambda_1\le\cdots\le\lambda_N$，$N=(n-1)(n+2)/2$，$\bar\lambda$ 为平均值。对 $L_1,L_2\ge0$ 及合适范围内的 $\alpha>1$，施加 $\lambda_1\ge-L_1\bar\lambda$ 与 $\frac1\alpha\sum_{j=1}^\alpha\lambda_j\ge-L_2\bar\lambda$ 两个下界条件；在两参数满足附加显式关系时进行刚性分析。

Result: 在考虑的参量范围内 $L_2>\theta(n,\alpha)$，故本文第二个条件严格弱于 CW26 的对应条件，而第一个条件与 CW26 条件互不蕴含；在 $L_1$ 与 $L_2$ 满足附加显式关系时，证明流形或为平坦，或为球空间形式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了 $n\ge6$ 闭爱因斯坦流形在第二类曲率算子部分特征值下界下的刚性刻画，推广并弱化了既有结果，表明此类谱约束足以迫使流形为平坦或球空间形式。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for closed Einstein manifolds of dimension $n\ge6$ under two lower bounds on the curvature operator of the second kind. More precisely, for $L_1, L_2\ge0$ and some real number $α>1$ in a suitable range, we consider \[ λ_1\ge-L_1\barλ, \qquad \frac1α\sum_{j=1}^αλ_j \ge-L_2\barλ. \] Here $λ_1\le\cdots\leλ_N$ are the eigenvalues of the curvature operator of the second kind $\mathring R$, $N=(n-1)(n+2)/2$, and $\barλ=N^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^Nλ_j$. Let $θ(n, α)$ be the constant appearing in \cite[Theorem~1.1]{CW26}; see (2). In the parameter range considered here, $L_2>θ(n, α)$, so the second condition is strictly weaker than the corresponding condition in \cite{CW26}, while the first condition and that condition do not imply each other. Under an additional explicit relation between $L_1$ and $L_2$, we prove that the manifold is either flat or a spherical space form.

</details>


### [233] [Convergence of cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian mean curvature flow in positive Kähler-Einstein manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23627)
*Naotoshi Fujihara,Toru Kajigaya,Albert Wood*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在闭、正Kähler–Einstein流形中，从闭嵌入余齐性一Lagrangian出发的Lagrangian平均曲率流长期存在、保持嵌入并光滑图收敛到极小Lagrangian；并将Behrndt的广义Lagrangian平均曲率流在几乎Einstein且C>0情形下约化为紧二维orbifold上的加权曲线缩短流，获得有限时间奇点刻画与长期子收敛，最终用Łojasiewicz–Simon不等式提升为光滑图收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lagrangian平均曲率流的长期存在性与收敛性是几何流中的核心问题。已有结果多依赖对称性或特殊初始条件，本文希望利用余齐性一Lagrangian的结构把高维流问题降维到二维，从而在自然精确性与正则性假设下统一处理Kähler–Einstein及更一般的几乎Einstein情形，并刻画奇点与收敛行为。

Method: 首先将Lagrangian平均曲率流约化为紧二维orbifold上的加权曲线缩短流；随后在orbifold设定下建立加权曲线缩短流的Grayson型有限时间奇点描述与长期子收敛定理；最后通过Łojasiewicz–Simon不等式把子收敛提升为全流的光滑图收敛。对于Ricci形式满足ρ=Cω+ndd^cf且C>0的几乎Einstein流形，采用类似假设得到f-极小Lagrangian的子收敛，并在(M,g,f)解析时升级为光滑图收敛。

Result: 在闭、正Kähler–Einstein流形中，从闭嵌入余齐性一Lagrangian出发的Lagrangian平均曲率流在自然精确性与正则性假设下对所有时间存在、保持嵌入，并光滑且图式收敛到极小Lagrangian。对Behrndt广义Lagrangian平均曲率流，在几乎Einstein且C>0及类似流假设下得到到f-极小Lagrangian子流形的子收敛，解析情形下可升级为光滑图收敛。

Conclusion: 通过将高维Lagrangian平均曲率流降维为orbifold上的加权曲线缩短流，并借助奇点分析、长期子收敛与Łojasiewicz–Simon提升，本文统一证明了余齐性一Lagrangian在Kähler–Einstein及几乎Einstein情形下的长期存在性与光滑收敛性。

Abstract: We prove that Lagrangian mean curvature flow starting from a closed, embedded, cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian in a closed, positive Kähler--Einstein manifold exists for all time, remains embedded, and converges smoothly and graphically to a minimal Lagrangian, under natural exactness and regularity assumptions. We also study the generalised Lagrangian mean curvature flow of Behrndt in Kähler manifolds which are almost-Einstein in the sense that the Ricci form satisfies $ρ= Cω+ ndd^cf$, and which satisfy $C>0$. With analogous assumptions on the flow, we obtain subconvergence to an $f$-minimal Lagrangian submanifold, with an upgrade to smooth graphical convergence in the case that $(M,g,f)$ is analytic. The proof proceeds by first reducing the flow to a weighted curve shortening flow on a compact two-dimensional orbifold. We then establish both a Grayson-type description of finite-time singularities and a long-time subconvergence theorem for weighted curve shortening flow in the orbifold setting. Finally, we use a Łojasiewicz--Simon inequality argument to upgrade to smooth convergence of the full flow.

</details>


### [234] [Extremal Kähler--Ricci solitons on Fano manifolds are Kähler--Einstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23630)
*Yasufumi Nitta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了 Fano 流形上任一 extremal Kähler–Ricci soliton 必为 Kähler–Einstein，从而完整解决了 Calamai–Petrecca 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calamai 与 Petrecca 提出：extremal Kähler–Ricci soliton 是否一定是 Kähler–Einstein？该问题此前仅在特殊情形下被验证，缺乏一般性结论，本文旨在给出完全一般的解答。

Method: 在 Fano 流形的紧 Kähler 设定下，对 extremal Kähler–Ricci soliton 方程与 extremal Kähler 度量的变分结构进行分析，通过论证 soliton 向量场的性质并借助相关刚性结果，推出 soliton 向量场必须为零。

Result: 证明了 Fano 流形上 extremal Kähler–Ricci soliton 的 soliton 向量场恒为零，因而该度量同时满足 Kähler–Einstein 条件。

Conclusion: extremal Kähler–Ricci soliton 与 Kähler–Einstein 度量在 Fano 情形下等价，Calamai–Petrecca 问题获得完全解决，也为 extremal Kähler 度量与 Ricci soliton 的分类研究提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We prove that every extremal Kähler--Ricci soliton on a Fano manifold is Kähler--Einstein. This solves the problem of Calamai and Petrecca in full generality.

</details>


### [235] [Reduction of Zieve's Conjecture on the Hurwitz problem to Three Branch Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23663)
*Xitaiyu Liu,Tianyang Sun,Bin Xu,Yu Ye,Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了 Zieve 关于球面上候选数据可实现性的猜想可归约到其三分支点情形：若所有 Zieve-可容许三元组可实现，则分支出点≥4 的 Zieve-可容许数据均可实现；由此 Zieve 猜想蕴含 Edmonds–Kulkarni–Stong 的素数次数猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zieve 猜想刻画了球面覆盖中候选数据的可实现性条件（每分支分拆各部分最大公约数为 1 且满足 lcm 条件），但该猜想涉及任意多分支出点的情形，直接处理困难。作者希望将问题化归为分支点数目最少（三点）的核心情形，从而简化证明结构，并建立该猜想与既有素数次数猜想之间的联系。

Method: 采用归约（reduction）策略：定义 Zieve-可容许三元组与 Zieve-可容许数据，证明若所有 Zieve-可容许三元组（三支点情形）可实现，则任意含至少四个分支点的 Zieve-可容许数据也可实现，从而把一般情形归约到三支点情形。文中还包含对归约方案探索、证明发展、符号术语一致性检验及稿件撰写修订的说明。

Result: 证得 Zieve 猜想可归约到其三支点情形——即所有 Zieve-可容许三元组的可实现性足以推出所有（≥4 分支点）Zieve-可容许数据的可实现性。作为推论，Zieve 猜想蕴含 Edmonds、Kulkarni 与 Stong 提出的素数次数猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Zieve 猜想的核心困难集中于三支点情形，为验证该猜想提供了更简单的充分条件，并确立了它与素数次数猜想之间的蕴含关系，从而加深了对球面覆盖候选数据可实现性理论的理解。

Abstract: M. E. Zieve conjectured that a candidate datum over the sphere is realizable whenever the gcd of the parts of every branch partition is one and it satisfies the lcm condition. We prove that this conjecture reduces to its three-branch-point case. More precisely, if every Zieve-admissible triple is realizable, then every Zieve-admissible datum with at least four branch points is realizable. In particular, Zieve's conjecture implies the prime-degree conjecture posed by A. L. Edmonds, R. S. Kulkarni and R. E. Stong.
  Generative-AI disclosure. OpenAI's GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra assisted throughout the research and preparation of this article, including the exploration of reduction strategies, proof development and checking, consistency checks on notation and terminology, and manuscript drafting and revision. The authors reviewed all AI-assisted material with careful scrutiny and take full responsibility for all mathematical claims and for the final text.

</details>


### [236] [Blaschke Conjecture and Complex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23822)
*Kyobeom Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文用复几何方法研究Blaschke猜想，引入"大切触二次纤维化"（great quadric fibration），并借助VMRT识别定理证明了在切丛整体上具有适应复结构的Blaschke流形同构于紧秩一对称空间（CROSS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blaschke猜想断言：单射半径等于直径的流形必等距于紧致秩一对称空间（CROSS），但经典方法难以统一解释这类流形的诸多结构性质。作者希望用复几何视角同时解释Clifford结构、Hopf纤维化与微分同胚类等特征。

Method: 引入大切触二次纤维化，作为"大球面纤维化"的复类比，并要求其全空间为复子流形；将该二次丛与背景Fano簇的极小有理切簇（VMRT）等同，在Blaschke猜想情形下使用bend-and-break论证，再结合Hwang-Mok的VMRT识别定理。

Result: 要求全空间为复子流形可同时解释Blaschke流形预期的Clifford结构、Hopf纤维化与微分同胚类；大切触二次丛在标准CROSS情形下与背景Fano簇的VMRT一致；证明了在切丛整体上存在适应复结构的Blaschke流形满足猜想，即同构于CROSS。

Conclusion: 复几何框架为Blaschke猜想提供了统一解释，并通过VMRT识别在适应复结构假设下证明了该猜想；该假设的适用范围与可否去除仍是后续问题。

Abstract: We provide a complex-geometric approach to the Blaschke conjecture, i.e., that a manifold whose injectivity radius equals its diameter is isometric to a compact rank-one symmetric space (CROSS). In particular, we introduce the great quadric fibration, a complex analogue of the great sphere fibration. Requiring its total space to be a complex submanifold simultaneously explains many properties that a Blaschke manifold is expected to possess, including its Clifford structure, Hopf fibration, and diffeomorphism class. Furthermore, the great quadric bundle coincides with the variety of minimal rational tangents (VMRT) of the ambient Fano variety in the standard CROSS cases, and we explain this through a bend-and-break argument in the setting of the Blaschke conjecture under a suitable complexification assumption. Combining this with Hwang-Mok VMRT recognition, we prove the Blaschke conjecture for manifolds admitting an adapted complex structure on their entire tangent bundles.

</details>


### [237] [Stable Capillary Minimal Hypersurfaces in $\R^4_+$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24251)
*Jia Li,Chenghang Lu,Chao Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了$\R^4_+$中完备、双侧、稳定毛细极小超曲面在接触角满足$|\cot\theta|<\kappa_*$（$\kappa_*\approx0.45$）时为平坦的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究毛细极小超曲面的刚性（唯一性）问题，推广经典稳定极小超曲面的平坦性结果，刻画接触角对稳定性的限制范围，并给出显式阈值$\kappa_*$。

Method: 在$\R^4_+$（半空间）中考虑完备、双侧、稳定的毛细极小超曲面，以接触角$\theta$为参数，结合稳定性不等式（第二变分）与毛细边界条件进行分析。

Result: 得到刚性定理：当$|\cot\theta|<\kappa_*=4\Gamma(3/4)^2/\Gamma(1/4)^2\approx0.45$时，该类超曲面必为平坦的。

Conclusion: 接触角足够接近$90^\circ$的完备双侧稳定毛细极小超曲面在$\R^4_+$中必为超平面；阈值$\kappa_*$由Gamma函数显式给出。

Abstract: We prove that a complete, two-sided, stable capillary minimal hypersurface in $\R^4_+$ is flat provided its contact angle $θ$ satisfies $|\cotθ|<κ_*$, where $κ_*=4Γ(3/4)^2/Γ(1/4)^2\approx 0.45$.

</details>


### [238] [Second Jacobi eigenvalues and spectral rigidity for surfaces in Berger spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24406)
*Márcio Batista,Abraão Mendes*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文给出了浸入收缩Berger球中任意闭双侧曲面的标量Jacobi算子第二特征值的上界，涉及全平方平均曲率、Euler示性数及与Hopf方向夹角相关的非正项，并将其应用于非正Euler示性数情形下的符号判定与等号刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究闭曲面在Berger球（非标准球面几何）中标量Jacobi算子第二特征值，旨在在无最小性或常平均曲率假设下获得一般性上界，并揭示几何量（平均曲率、Euler示性数、Hopf方向夹角）对该特征值符号的影响。

Method: 证明结合了四类工具：将Berger球实现为复射影平面的测地超曲面；后者经第一标准嵌入进入Euclidean球面；加权Hersch--Li--Yau平衡论证；以及Willmore泛函的共形不变性。

Result: 建立了第二Jacobi特征值上界，包含全平方平均曲率、Euler示性数与由曲面法向与Hopf方向夹角决定的显式非正项；证明不依赖最小性或常平均曲率条件。应用上，当1/3≤α≤1且Euler示性数非正时，第二Jacobi特征值非正，α>1/3时严格负；在α=1/3临界值处等号成立当且仅当像同余于极小Clifford环面，嵌入情形下给出等号情形的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为Berger球中一般闭双侧曲面的Jacobi谱提供了带几何修正项的上界，并以Clifford环面刻画临界等号情形，推广了相关Hersch--Li--Yau型与Willmore型方法的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an upper bound for the second eigenvalue of the scalar Jacobi operator of an arbitrary closed two-sided surface immersed in a contracted Berger sphere. The estimate involves the total squared mean curvature, the Euler characteristic, and an explicit nonpositive term determined by the angle between the surface normal and the Hopf direction. The proof combines the realization of a Berger sphere as a geodesic hypersurface of a complex projective plane, the first standard embedding of the latter into a Euclidean sphere, a weighted Hersch--Li--Yau balancing argument, and the conformal invariance of the Willmore functional. No minimality or constant mean curvature assumption is imposed. As an application, if $1/3\leqα\leq1$ and the surface has nonpositive Euler characteristic, then its second Jacobi eigenvalue is nonpositive; it is strictly negative for $α>1/3$. At the critical value $α=1/3$, equality forces the immersed image to be congruent to the minimal Clifford torus; in the embedded category, this yields a complete characterization of the equality case.

</details>


### [239] [Anisotropic-Length-Preserving Weighted Anisotropic Curvature Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24431)
*Zhishuai Liu,Guoxin Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究凸闭平面曲线的加权各向异性保长曲率流，证明该流对任意光滑嵌入凸初始曲线全局存在，且各向异性曲率一致收敛到相应Wulff形状边界的曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性曲率流是经典曲率流的自然推广，其中法向速度依赖各向异性张量。保长条件下的加权版本能保持曲线长度不变，并具有归一化的收敛性质，探讨这类流的长期行为有助于理解一般非各向同性几何演化方程的正则性与渐近形态。

Method: 对光滑、嵌入、凸的闭平面曲线建立加权各向异性保长曲率流的演化方程；利用凸性、嵌入性及长度保持约束推导先验估计，证明解的一致抛物正则性与全局存在性；分析各向异性曲率的渐近行为。

Result: 证明该流对任意光滑嵌入凸初始曲线存在唯一光滑解且对所有时间存在；各向异性曲率随时间趋于无穷光滑收敛到对应同伦Wulff形状边界的曲率。

Conclusion: 加权各向异性保长曲率流将任意光滑凸闭曲线渐近变形为与其同伦的Wulff形状（在曲率意义上），表明该流具有优良的全局正则性与渐近稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the anisotropic-length-preserving weighted anisotropic curvature flow for smooth convex closed plane curves. For any smooth, embedded, closed, convex initial curve, the flow exists smoothly for all time, and the anisotropic curvature converges smoothly to that of the boundary of the corresponding homothetic Wulff shape as time tends to infinity.

</details>


### [240] [Dirac-harmonic maps from manifolds with boundary via index theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24551)
*Volker Branding,Nicolas Ginoux*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该手稿利用带边流形上的指标理论，证明Dirac调和映射的一般存在性结果，关键在于带边Atiyah-Patodi-Singer指标公式中的附加贡献可使指标非零，并给出基于曲面twistor旋量的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac调和映射是量子场论中超对称非线性sigma模型的数学版本；由于其作为无界能量泛函的临界点出现，建立一般存在性结果颇具挑战性。

Method: 基于带边流形上的指标理论，证明一般存在性结果；利用带边Atiyah-Patodi-Singer指标公式中的附加边界贡献，使指标非零；并在多种情形下应用该结果，同时构造基于曲面twistor旋量的显式例子。

Result: 证明了基于带边指标理论的一般存在性结果，并将其应用于多种情形；构造了基于曲面twistor旋量的显式例子。

Conclusion: 带边Atiyah-Patodi-Singer指标公式中的边界贡献可导致非零指标，从而为Dirac调和映射的存在性提供关键论证。

Abstract: Dirac-harmonic maps are a mathematical version of the supersymmetric non-linear sigma model of quantum field theory. Since they arise as critical points of an unbounded energy functional, it is a challenging task to establish general existence results. Within this manuscript we mainly prove a general existence result based on index theory for manifolds with boundary and apply it in various situations. Due to the presence of a boundary there are additional contributions in the Atiyah-Patodi-Singer index formula allowing for a non-vanishing index, which is the key argument in our proofs. Further explicit examples based on twistor spinors on surfaces are also presented.

</details>


### [241] [Geometric quantizazion and TYCZ coefficients of HyperKähler 4-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24578)
*Simone Cristofori*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有有效三-Hamiltonian圆周作用的完备超Kähler四流形上几何量子化存在的条件，并证明相关度量的第三系数a₃消失等价于平坦性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨完备超Kähler四流形在特定对称性（有效三-Hamiltonian圆周作用）下几何量子化的存在条件，并寻求度量的曲率特征（平坦性）与显式系数（a₃）之间的刻画关系。

Method: 以具有有效三-Hamiltonian圆周作用的完备超Kähler四流形为对象，考察几何量子化存在的条件；引入与这些度量相联系的第三系数a₃，并分析其消失性。

Result: 证明第三系数a₃的消失与度量的平坦性等价。

Conclusion: 在所述设定下，几何量子化存在性条件的研究归结为a₃这一不变量：a₃=0当且仅当度量为平坦，从而给出平坦性的代数/系数判别准则。

Abstract: In this paper we study conditions for the existence of a geometric quantization of a complete hyperKähler four manifold with an effective tri--Hamiltonian circle action and we show that the vanishing of the third coefficient $a_3$ associated to these metrics is equivalent to flatness.

</details>


### [242] [Kähler-Einstein cone metrics and parabolic bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24597)
*Martin de Borbon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧Kähler流形上引入沿简单正规交叉除子的抛物结构，证明锥角Kähler-Einstein度量对应的切丛抛物稳定性，并导出Miyaoka-Yau型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有锥奇点（沿除子锥角）的Kähler-Einstein度量的稳定性性质，将经典的Miyaoka-Yau不等式推广到对数/锥情形。

Method: 在切丛TX上定义抛物结构TX_*，沿每个D_i由滤过0⊂TD_i⊂TX|_{D_i}给出，法商上权重w_i=1-β_i；利用抛物Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式以及Tian扩张（λ>0）和Simpson典范Higgs丛（λ<0）上的抛物结构进行稳定性分析。

Result: 证明当X\D上存在锥角2πβ_i的Kähler-Einstein度量ω时，TX_*关于[ω]是抛物多重稳定的；对非零Einstein常数λ，Tian扩张（λ>0）和Simpson典范Higgs丛（λ<0）上的相应抛物结构也是多重稳定的；进而得到这些对的Miyaoka-Yau不等式。

Conclusion: 锥角Kähler-Einstein度量的存在性蕴含切丛附带的抛物结构的抛物多重稳定性，结合抛物Bogomolov-Gieseker不等式可推出对数/锥情形下的Miyaoka-Yau型不等式。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a compact Kähler manifold, let $D=\sum_iD_i$ be a simple normal crossing divisor, and fix $β_i\in(0,1)$. We introduce a parabolic structure $TX_*$ on $TX$, given along each $D_i$ by the filtration $0\subset TD_i\subset TX|_{D_i}$ with weight $w_i=1-β_i$ on the normal quotient. If $X\setminus D$ carries a Kähler--Einstein metric $ω$ with cone angle $2πβ_i$ along $D_i$, we show that $TX_*$ is parabolic polystable with respect to $[ω]$. When the Einstein constant $λ$ is non-zero, we introduce parabolic structures on the Tian extension ($λ>0$) and on Simpson's canonical Higgs bundle ($λ<0$), and prove that these are polystable too. Combined with the Bogomolov--Gieseker inequality for parabolic bundles, this yields a Miyaoka--Yau inequality for these pairs.

</details>


### [243] [Non-maximal quasi-isometric $\mathrm{PU}_{2,n+1}$-representations via alternating surfaces in complex pseudo-hyperbolic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24672)
*Qiongling Li,Junming Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对每个容许非极大Toledo不变量t，构造从闭曲面群到PU_{2,n+1}的不可约表示轨迹，其轨道映射为拟等距嵌入，并进一步构造Shilov边界上的余紧不连续区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲面群在PU_{2,n+1}中具有高维Toledo不变量的不可约表示，特别是寻找其轨道映射具有良好的几何性质（拟等距嵌入），并构造相应的几何结构（不连续区域）。

Method: 利用4-循环Higgs丛及其与复伪双曲空间中∂-交错曲面的对应关系进行构造；相关等变极小映射到对称空间为双Lipschitz嵌入。

Result: 这些轨迹在特征簇中的实余维数为10(g-1)或10(g-1)+2(n-1)，取决于Toledo值；轨道映射为拟等距嵌入；相关的等变极小映射是双Lipschitz嵌入；在Shilov边界上构造了余紧不连续区域，其商为光滑纤维丛，纤维同胚于S^{2n-1}×S^{2n-1}。

Conclusion: 成功构造了一类具有良好几何性质（拟等距/双Lipschitz嵌入）的曲面群表示轨迹，并在Shilov边界上得到余紧不连续区域及具有特定纤维的光滑纤维丛结构。

Abstract: For every admissible non-maximal Toledo invariant $t$, we construct a locus of irreducible representations of a closed genus-$g$ surface group into $\mathrm{PU}_{2,n+1}$ with Toledo invariant $t$ whose orbit maps are quasi-isometric embeddings. These loci have real codimension $10(g-1)$ or $10(g-1)+2(n-1)$ in the character variety depending on the Toledo value. Our construction uses $4$-cyclic Higgs bundles and their correspondence with $\partial$-alternating surfaces in complex pseudo-hyperbolic spaces. The associated equivariant minimal maps into the symmetric space are bi-Lipschitz embeddings. We further construct cocompact domains of discontinuity in the Shilov boundary. Their quotients are smooth fiber bundles over the surface with fiber homeomorphic to $S^{2n-1}\times S^{2n-1}$.

</details>


### [244] [The optimal isoperimetric inequality for varifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24716)
*Mario Santilli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有限质量与有限总一阶变差变分簇（允许任意奇异部分、分布平均曲率仅可积）的Brendle等周不等式，证明结合最优传输与切向Laplacian局部性定理，所得等周常数在余维一与余维二下为最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Brendle等周不等式推广到更一般的奇异变分簇情形，降低正则性假设（仅需有限总一阶变差、平均曲率可积、允许任意奇异部分），从而覆盖积分变分簇及带正下密度界的可求长变分簇。

Method: 证明采用最优传输方法，并利用关于环境凸函数切向Laplacian的局部性定理（locality theorem for the tangential Laplacian）。

Result: 在有限质量、有限总一阶变差、平均曲率仅可积且允许任意奇异部分的条件下，Brendle等周不等式成立；结论适用于积分变分簇，以及权测度集中于可数多个C^2子流形且具正下密度界的可求长变分簇。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了奇异变分簇的Brendle等周不等式；等周常数在余维一与余维二下为最优（sharp）。

Abstract: We prove the Brendle isoperimetric inequality for varifolds of finite mass and finite total first variation, allowing an arbitrary singular part. The distributional mean curvature is assumed only integrable. The result applies to integral varifolds and, more generally, to rectifiable varifolds with a positive lower density bound and weight measure concentrated on countably many $C^2$ submanifolds. The proof uses optimal transport and a locality theorem for the tangential Laplacian of an ambient convex function. The isoperimetric constant is sharp in codimensions one and two.

</details>


### [245] [The spherical cap conjecture for immersed disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24766)
*José M. Espinar*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了欧几里得三维空间中具有非零常平均曲率、边界正则且微分同胚映到圆周的光滑浸入圆盘必为嵌入的球冠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常平均曲率（CMC）曲面的分类是微分几何中的核心问题之一。对于以圆周为边界的圆盘型CMC浸入曲面，此前尚不清楚边界正则性条件是否足以排除自交等奇异行为，即是否存在非嵌入的浸入解，这一问题与经典的Plateau型边值问题密切相关。

Method: 论文采用几何分析的证明方法，研究对象为欧几里得三维空间中光滑浸入圆盘，其常平均曲率非零、边界处正则，且边界在微分同胚意义下映射到一圆周。证明依赖解析手段排除浸入的自交与分支等非嵌入性态，最终确立其为嵌入球冠。

Result: 证明了任何满足上述条件的光滑CMC浸入圆盘都是嵌入的球冠（即球面的一部分），从而排除了圆盘型CMC浸入曲面出现自交或非嵌入结构的可能性。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了圆周边界条件下常平均曲率圆盘型浸入的刚性刻画：浸入条件在边界正则约束下自动退化为嵌入球冠。这一结论强化了对CMC曲面边值问题解的几何结构的理解，并暗示类似的刚性现象可能推广到更一般的边界曲线或其他空间形式中。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth immersed disk in Euclidean three-space with nonzero constant mean curvature, regular up to the boundary and mapping its boundary diffeomorphically onto a circle, is an embedded spherical cap.

</details>


### [246] [White's cone over the Möbius band is area-minimising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24909)
*Marco A. M. Guaraco,Davide Parise*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明 $\mathbb R^4$ 中极小 Möbius 带 $\Sigma\subset\mathbb S^3$ 上的锥 $C(\Sigma)$ 在 $\mathbb Z_2$ 系数下局部质量最小，验证了 White 的猜想；其圆柱切片对应 Ros 研究过的非可定向极小螺旋面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作针对 Plateau 问题边界奇点模型：在环境维数 $4$ 及以上，Brian White 猜想合适的极小 Möbius 带上的锥是局部质量最小的。该结果有助于理解边界奇点的分类与正则性。

Method: 关键观察是：锥 $C(\Sigma)$ 与圆柱面 $\{|w|=r\}$ 的交（圆柱切片）在 $\mathbb R^3$ 的平坦秩一平移商空间中为极小曲面，且对应于 Ros 研究的非可定向极小螺旋面。据此证明 $C(\Sigma)$ 在 $\mathbb Z_2$ 系数下的局部质量最小性。此外，刻画满足“极小圆柱切片性质”的非平面极小超锥。

Result: $C(\Sigma)$ 在 $\mathbb Z_2$ 系数下局部质量最小，证实 White 猜想。圆柱切片为平移商空间中的极小曲面，对应非可定向极小螺旋面。并且，在刚体运动意义下，$C(\Sigma)\times\mathbb R^{n-4}\subset\mathbb R^n$ 是唯一一类边界为 $\mathbb R^{n-2}$、除边界外光滑且满足极小圆柱切片性质的非平面极小超锥。

Conclusion: 该结果在 $4$ 维及以上验证了 White 关于边界奇点模型的猜想，并给出满足极小圆柱切片性质的非平面极小超锥的唯一性刻画。

Abstract: We prove that the cone $C(Σ)$ in $\mathbb R^4$ over a suitable minimal Möbius band $Σ\subset \mathbb S^3$ is locally mass-minimising with coefficients in $\mathbb Z_2$. This proves a conjecture by Brian White in the context of models of boundary singularities of Plateau's problem in ambient dimension $4$ or higher. The key insight is that the cylindrical slices $C(Σ)\cap \{(z,w)\in \mathbb R^2 \times \mathbb R^2 : |w|=r\}$ are minimal surfaces in a flat rank-one translation quotient of $\mathbb R^3$. In fact, they correspond to non-orientable minimising helicoids previously studied by Antonio Ros. We also show that, up to rigid motions, the minimising cones $C(Σ)\times \mathbb R^{n-4}\subset \mathbb R^n$ are the only non-planar minimal hypercones with boundary $\mathbb R^{n-2}$ which are smooth away from the boundary that satisfy the minimal cylindrical slice property.

</details>


### [247] [A quantitative inequality for general area-minimizing hypercones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24958)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了一般（可能具非孤立奇异集）面积最小超锥的定量等周不等式：周长差相对于体积差的平方具有一致下界，将已有正则锥结果推广到一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有定量不等式仅适用于正则面积最小超锥，而一般面积最小超锥可能存在非孤立奇异集，需给出统一处理。

Method: 沿用校准（calibration）论证；关键在于向量场的构造。因逐点渐近估计不再适用，改在锥两侧的最小叶理（minimal foliations）上，以正 Jacobi 场为权积分投影核，利用核构造得到散度界，并借助弱 Harnack 不等式与增长估计获得向量场的线性增长。

Result: 存在常数 c_C>0，使得对所有 R>0 及紧支于 B_R 的有限周长集 F，有 (Per(F;B_R)-Per(E;B_R))/R^n ≥ c_C (|F△E|/R^{n+1})^2。

Conclusion: 将无权的定量不等式从正则面积最小超锥推广至一般面积最小超锥，包括具非孤立奇异集的情形。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{C}=\partial E\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ be an area-minimizing hypercone. The cone may have nonisolated singularities. We prove that there is a constant $c_{\mathbf{C}}>0$ such that \[
  \frac{\operatorname{Per}(F;B_R)-\operatorname{Per}(E;B_R)}{R^n}
  \geq c_{\mathbf{C}}
  \left(\frac{|F\triangle E|}{R^{n+1}}\right)^2 \] for every $R>0$ and every set $F$ of locally finite perimeter with $F\triangle E\Subset B_R$. This extends the unweighted quantitative inequality from regular area-minimizing hypercones to general area-minimizing hypercones.
  We follow the calibration argument in the author's earlier work on regular cones. The main change is the construction of the vector fields. The pointwise asymptotic estimates for positive Jacobi fields used in the regular case do not directly apply here. We use the minimal foliations constructed by Zhihan Wang on the two sides of the cone. On each leaf, we integrate projection kernels with a positive Jacobi field as the weight. The kernel construction gives the required divergence bound. Wang's weak Harnack inequality and growth estimates give the integral bounds needed to prove linear growth of the vector fields.

</details>


### [248] [Shrinking Kähler-Ricci solitons on toric fano fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24884)
*Tristan C. Collins,Genggeng Huang,Freid Tong,Yulun Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了光滑环面Fano纤维化上存在完备的梯度收缩Kähler-Ricci孤立子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler-Ricci孤立子是Ricci流的自相似解，其存在性对理解流的长时行为与奇点结构至关重要。环面簇作为典型的Fano流形来源，其上的孤立子构造是几何分析中的核心问题，而纤维化情形此前缺乏一般性结论。

Method: 采用环面几何的凸分析框架（动量多胞形、扇结构），结合Kähler-Ricci孤立子方程约化为实Monge-Ampère型方程，利用环面Fano纤维化的组合结构构造势函数并求解方程。摘要未给出细节，推测依赖环面退化与降维归纳。

Result: 证明了任一光滑环面Fano纤维化都容许一个完备的梯度收缩Kähler-Ricci孤立子，给出了该存在性的肯定回答，推广了环面Fano流形上的相应结论。

Conclusion: 环面Fano纤维化的组合结构足以保证梯度收缩Kähler-Ricci孤立子的存在性，为Ricci流奇点分析与环面几何的进一步研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove that a smooth toric Fano fibration admits a complete gradient shrinking Kähler-Ricci soliton.

</details>
